📚 本期文献进展 / Literature Brief

  • 本期登革病毒研究集中呈现以下进展。
  • 该研究证实了几内亚所有生态区域广泛暴露于 DENV、YFV 和 WNV 的血清学证据。DENV 最高的 血清流行率 位于 N'Zerekore (43.9%),YFV 位于 Kankan (54.7%),WNV 位于 Forecariah (53.0%)。成年人,特别是农民和牲畜饲养者,表现出较高的 DENV 和 YFV 血清阳性率。在致倦库蚊中检测到西尼罗河病毒,表明其在当地 传播 中的作用。
  • 疫苗 构建体的抗原性为 0.9319,全球人群覆盖率为 97.35%;与 TLR2 和 TLR4 的分子对接揭示了有希望的相互作用,最低能量分别为 -1240.5 kJ/mol 和 -1393.3 kJ/mol,MD 模拟证实了稳定性和灵活性。
  • 结构和动力学分析揭示了 E 蛋白如何扰乱膜结构,促进入口部位的负曲率和类似于早期半融合中间体的相邻区域的正曲率。 [10.1080/07391102.2026.2708780]。
  • 男性的 患病率 显着高于女性(23.4% vs. 原发感染占 72.4%,继发感染占 27.6%。DENV-2 占主导地位,其次是 DENV-3,与沙特阿拉伯之前报道的血清型模式一致。 [10.1007/s00284-026-05001-1].
  • This reporting window highlights the following research developments.
  • The study confirms widespread serological evidence of exposure to DENV, YFV, and WNV across all ecological regions of Guinea.
  • The economic burden of dengue in Mexico is substantial, peaking in 2013 with annual costs of $176.82 million (adjusted present value: $281.60 million).
  • The vaccine construct showed an antigenicity of 0.9319 with 97.35% population coverage worldwide; molecular docking with TLR2 and TLR4 revealed promising interactions with lowest energies of −1240.5 kJ/mol and −1393.3 kJ/mol, respectively, and MD simulations confirmed stability and flexibility.
  • Structural and dynamic analyses revealed how the E protein perturbs membrane architecture, promoting negative curvature at the entry site and positive curvature in adjoining regions that resemble early hemifusion intermediates.

📰 本期新闻动态 / News Brief

  • 印度药品监管总局(DCGI)已批准由武田制药开发的 Qdenga 疫苗,用于 4 至 60 岁人群,旨在应对该国不断上升的登革热感染和住院风险。
  • 印度批准了首款针对四种登革病毒血清型的疫苗 Qdenga,该疫苗在临床试验中显示出显著的预防效果 [news-7e1813ceabb9ed1d, news-a4f2255ed2acd63a]。
  • 临床数据显示,Qdenga 在接种后 18 个月内预防住院的有效率高达 90%,且保护效果可维持长达 4.5 年 [news-8bffa2445f71beb1, news-e4beda36a5e88985]。
  • 专家警告,疫苗接种应作为蚊虫控制措施的补充,且不建议孕妇、哺乳期妇女及免疫功能低下者使用 [news-d25484bcb1836bab, news-8bffa2445f71beb1]。
  • 研究发现,印度多种病毒株同时传播(高流行性传播)增加了重症风险,使疫苗接种策略更具挑战性 [news-3393f587785b8b76, news-5638eccaa07e295f]。
  • India's drug regulator, the DCGI, has approved the Qdenga vaccine for individuals aged 4-60 to combat the growing burden of dengue infections across the country.
  • India has approved Qdenga, a tetravalent vaccine targeting all four dengue virus serotypes, marking a major public health milestone [news-7e1813ceabb9ed1d, news-a4f2255ed2acd63a].
  • Clinical trials demonstrate Qdenga's efficacy in reducing hospitalization risk by 90% within 18 months of vaccination [news-8bffa2445f71beb1, news-e4beda36a5e88985].
  • Medical experts emphasize that vaccination should complement, rather than replace, comprehensive mosquito control and public awareness efforts [news-d25484bcb1836bab, news-8bffa2445f71beb1].
  • The emergence of hyperendemic transmission with multiple circulating strains complicates future vaccination and outbreak prediction [news-3393f587785b8b76, news-5638eccaa07e295f].

沙特阿拉伯西南部高地的 监测 和 媒介 的分子和血清学 监测 动态

Molecular and Serological Surveillance of Dengue Virus and Vector Dynamics in the Highlands of Southwestern Saudi Arabia
作者： Ahmad Hassn Ghzwani, Mubarak A. Alzubaidi, Hesham M. Al-Mekhlafi, Manal Mohamed Elhassan Taha, Zaki M. Eisa, Tareq K. Khawaji, Amro Ahmed Zalah, Qasem Mohammed Ali Abushushah, Khalid Mohmmed Ghazwani, Abadi M. Mashlawi
摘要中文翻译登革热 是沙特阿拉伯的一种新出现的地方病;然而,在 传播 动态、媒介 分布和高原地区血清型流通方面仍然存在重要差距。本研究主要旨在调查吉赞地区高地省份的 登革病毒 (DENV) 感染和 媒介 的发生情况。一项横断面研究纳入了 182 名发热患者,使用 RT-qPCR、ELISA、基于 RT-PCR 的血清分型和快速诊断测试进行了测试。使用 CDC 光阱同时进行昆虫学 监测,对埃及伊蚊进行形态学和分子鉴定。RT-qPCR 证实 15.9% (29/182) 的患者存在急性 DENV 感染。男性的 患病率 显着高于女性(23.4% vs. 原发感染占 72.4%,而继发感染占 27.6%)。ELISA-NS1 表现出比 RDT-NS1(58.6% 和 99.4%)更高的敏感性(79.3%)和特异性(100%),与 RT-qPCR 的一致性更强(κ = 0.87 vs. IgG 血清阳性) (41.8%) 表明存在大量先前暴露,可能反映了持续流行,而不是孤立的 DENV-2,其次是 DENV-3,与沙特阿拉伯之前报告的血清型模式一致。总共收集了 7,095 只蚊子,其中 20.9% 是埃及伊蚊,全年检测到的蚊子数量在 8 月至 9 月达到峰值,可能与较高的温度和每月的蚊子状况有关。与登革热病例相关(r = 0.770;P = 0.006)。这些发现表明,登革热 传播 和 媒介 的存在延伸到高地地区,这对于加强传统流行区以外的登革热控制至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 是沙特阿拉伯的一种新出现的地方病;然而,在 传播 动态、媒介 分布和高原地区血清型流通方面仍然存在重要差距。本研究主要旨在调查吉赞地区高地省份的 登革病毒 (DENV) 感染和 媒介 的发生情况。
设计与对象
一项横断面研究纳入了 182 名发热患者,使用 RT-qPCR、ELISA、基于 RT-PCR 的血清分型和快速诊断测试进行了测试。使用 CDC 光阱同时进行昆虫学 监测,对埃及伊蚊进行形态学和分子鉴定。
核心方法
RT-qPCR 证实 15.9% (29/182) 的患者存在急性 DENV 感染。ELISA-NS1 表现出比 RDT-NS1(58.6% 和 99.4%)更高的灵敏度(79.3%)和特异性(100%),与 RT-qPCR 的一致性更强(κ = 0.87 vs.
主要结果
男性的 患病率 显着高于女性(23.4% vs. 原发感染占 72.4%,继发感染占 27.6%。DENV-2 占主导地位,其次是 DENV-3,与沙特阿拉伯之前报道的血清型模式一致。
解释与创新
IgG 血清阳性 (41.8%) 表明之前有大量接触,可能反映了持续流行而不是孤立的 暴发。这些发现表明登革热 传播 和 媒介 的存在延伸到高原地区。
科研与公卫意义
将分子诊断与扩展的 媒介 监测 相结合对于加强传统流行区以外的登革热控制至关重要。每月蚊子数量与登革热病例相关(r = 0.770;P = 0.006)。
局限与证据强度
共采集蚊子7095只,其中白伊蚊占20.9%。埃及伊蚊全年都有发现,高峰出现在 8 月至 9 月,可能与较高的气温和降雨条件有关。由于横截面设计和样本量有限,证据强度中等。

Molecular and Serological Surveillance of Dengue Virus and Vector Dynamics in the Highlands of Southwestern Saudi Arabia

Authors: Ahmad Hassn Ghzwani, Mubarak A. Alzubaidi, Hesham M. Al-Mekhlafi, Manal Mohamed Elhassan Taha, Zaki M. Eisa, Tareq K. Khawaji, Amro Ahmed Zalah, Qasem Mohammed Ali Abushushah, Khalid Mohmmed Ghazwani, Abadi M. Mashlawi
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever is an emerging endemic disease in Saudi Arabia; however, important gaps remain regarding transmission dynamics, vector distribution, and serotype circulation in highland areas. This study primarily aimed to investigate dengue virus (DENV) infection and vector occurrence in the highland governorates of the Jazan region. A cross-sectional study enrolled 182 febrile patients tested using RT-qPCR, ELISA, RT-PCR-based serotyping, and rapid diagnostic tests. Concurrent entomological surveillance was conducted using CDC light traps, with morphological and molecular identification of Aedes aegypti. RT-qPCR confirmed acute DENV infection in 15.9% (29/182) of patients. Males showed significantly higher prevalence than females (23.4% vs. Primary infections accounted for 72.4%, while 27.6% were secondary. ELISA-NS1 demonstrated higher sensitivity (79.3%) and specificity (100%) than RDT-NS1 (58.6% and 99.4%), with stronger agreement with RT-qPCR (κ = 0.87 vs. IgG seropositivity (41.8%) suggests substantial prior exposure, likely reflecting ongoing endemicity rather than isolated outbreaks. DENV-2 predominated, followed by DENV-3, consistent with previously reported serotype patterns in Saudi Arabia. A total of 7,095 mosquitoes were collected, of which 20.9% were Ae. aegypti, detected year-round with peaks in August-September, likely associated with higher temperature and rainfall conditions. Monthly mosquito abundance correlated with dengue cases (r = 0.770; P = 0.006). These findings indicate that dengue transmission and vector presence extend into highland areas. Integrating molecular diagnostics with expanded vector surveillance is essential to strengthen dengue control beyond traditional endemic zones.

Question and background
Dengue fever is an emerging endemic disease in Saudi Arabia; however, important gaps remain regarding transmission dynamics, vector distribution, and serotype circulation in highland areas. This study primarily aimed to investigate dengue virus (DENV) infection and vector occurrence in the highland governorates of the Jazan region.

RT-qPCR confirmed acute DENV infection in 15.9% (29/182) of patients. ELISA-NS1 demonstrated higher sensitivity (79.3%) and specificity (100%) than RDT-NS1 (58.6% and 99.4%), with stronger agreement with RT-qPCR (κ = 0.87 vs.
Main results
Males showed significantly higher prevalence than females (23.4% vs. Primary infections accounted for 72.4%, while 27.6% were secondary. DENV-2 predominated, followed by DENV-3, consistent with previously reported serotype patterns in Saudi Arabia.
Interpretation and novelty
IgG seropositivity (41.8%) suggests substantial prior exposure, likely reflecting ongoing endemicity rather than isolated outbreaks. These findings indicate that dengue transmission and vector presence extend into highland areas.
Scientific and public-health significance
Integrating molecular diagnostics with expanded vector surveillance is essential to strengthen dengue control beyond traditional endemic zones. Monthly mosquito abundance correlated with dengue cases (r = 0.770; P = 0.006).
Limitations and evidence strength
A total of 7,095 mosquitoes were collected, of which 20.9% were Ae. aegypti, detected year-round with peaks in August-September, likely associated with higher temperature and rainfall conditions. The evidence strength is moderate due to the cross-sectional design and limited sample size.

针对 登革病毒 的新型基于表位的四价亚基 疫苗 的设计:一种免疫信息学方法

Design of a novel epitope-based tetravalent subunit vaccine against dengue virus: An immunoinformatic approach
作者： Tanbin Jahan Ferdousy, Md Mehedy Hasan Miraz, Tawsif Al Arian, Md Afif Ullah, Md Tahsinul Haque Risat, Most. Afrin Akter, Sukalyan Kumar Kundu, Bidduth Kumar Sarkar, Arghya Prosun Sarkar, Abul Bashar Ripon Khalipha
摘要中文翻译Dengue imposes a profound global impact,with millions affected annually.伊蚊的 传播 给防治带来了重大挑战,城市化和气候变化加剧了这一挑战。Despite efforts,no impeccable 抗病毒药物 治疗 exists to date,highlighting the urgency for a 疫苗. Developing one encounters hurdles like the four distinctive serotypes of the virus and complex 免疫应答.在这项研究中,我们采用免疫信息学方法设计了基于表位的四价亚基 疫苗,旨在对抗所有 DENV 血清型。该研究考虑了表位预测和筛选、理化评估、分子对接、分子动力学(MD)模拟、免疫模拟和计算机克隆。The 疫苗 construct comprises human β-defensin 3 adjuvant and 23 epitopes joined with linkers. Notably,the 疫苗 has an antigenicity of 0.9319 with 97.35% population coverage worldwide.与 Toll 样受体 2 (TLR2) 和 TLR4 的分子对接显示出有希望的相互作用,最低能量分别为 -1240.5 kJ/mol(76 个成员)和 -1393.3 kJ/mol(40 个成员),表现出显着的静电、范达华力和其他相互作用。Molecular dynamics (MD) simulations of 200 nanoseconds revealed the 疫苗 to be stable and flexible with the receptors. The solvent accessibility and radius of gyration were also in suitable ranges.三剂量 疫苗 方案引发了强大且持久的多谱系 免疫应答,其特征是稳定的 B 和 T 细胞记忆、持续的 监测 和高滴度抗体产生 (IgM/IgG) 以及促炎细胞因子谱(IFN-γ 和 IL-2),以实现有效的抗原清除。These findings suggest that our 疫苗 outperforms any other Dengue 疫苗 developed to date.然而,由于这项研究是通过计算机方法进行的,因此需要进行体外和体内验证,以确认 疫苗 作为临床试验的潜在候选者。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热对全球产生了深远的影响,每年有数百万人受到影响,尽管做出了努力,但迄今为止仍不存在完美的 抗病毒药物 治疗,这凸显了 疫苗 的紧迫性。
设计与对象
本研究采用免疫信息学方法设计基于表位的四价亚基 疫苗,旨在对抗所有 DENV 血清型,不涉及特定人群或临床 队列研究。
核心方法
该研究涉及表位预测和筛选、理化评估、分子对接、分子动力学 (MD) 模拟、免疫模拟以及计算机克隆以评估 疫苗 构建体。
主要结果
疫苗 构建体的抗原性为 0.9319,全球人群覆盖率为 97.35%;与 TLR2 和 TLR4 的分子对接揭示了有希望的相互作用,最低能量分别为 -1240.5 kJ/mol 和 -1393.3 kJ/mol,MD 模拟证实了稳定性和灵活性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,疫苗 优于迄今为止开发的任何其他登革热 疫苗,其强大的 疫苗 和高抗原性就证明了这一点。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,通过四价亚基 疫苗 具有广泛的人群覆盖范围和强大的免疫刺激作用,有可能改善登革热预防,但还需要进一步验证。
局限与证据强度
证据强度有限,因为该研究完全通过计算机方法进行,并且需要体外和体内验证来确认 疫苗 作为临床试验的潜在候选者。

Design of a novel epitope-based tetravalent subunit vaccine against dengue virus: An immunoinformatic approach

Authors: Tanbin Jahan Ferdousy, Md Mehedy Hasan Miraz, Tawsif Al Arian, Md Afif Ullah, Md Tahsinul Haque Risat, Most. Afrin Akter, Sukalyan Kumar Kundu, Bidduth Kumar Sarkar, Arghya Prosun Sarkar, Abul Bashar Ripon Khalipha
Original abstract (English)
Dengue imposes a profound global impact, with millions affected annually. Its transmission by Aedes mosquitoes poses significant challenges to combat, aggravated by urbanization and climate change. Despite efforts, no impeccable antiviral treatment exists to date, highlighting the urgency for a vaccine. Developing one encounters hurdles like the four distinctive serotypes of the virus and complex immune responses. In this study, we employed an immunoinformatics approach to design an epitope-based tetravalent subunit vaccine aimed at confronting all DENV serotypes. The study contemplates epitope prediction and screening, physicochemical assessment, molecular docking, molecular dynamics (MD) simulations, immune simulations, and in silico cloning. The vaccine construct comprises human β-defensin 3 adjuvant and 23 epitopes joined with linkers. Notably, the vaccine has an antigenicity of 0.9319 with 97.35% population coverage worldwide. Molecular docking with toll-like receptor 2 (TLR2) and TLR4 showed promising interactions with lowest energies of −1240.5 kJ/mol (76 members) and −1393.3 kJ/mol (40 members), respectively, exhibiting significant electrostatic, van dar Waals, and other interactions. Molecular dynamics (MD) simulations of 200 nanoseconds revealed the vaccine to be stable and flexible with the receptors. The solvent accessibility and radius of gyration were also in suitable ranges. The three-dose vaccine regimen elicited a robust and durable multi-lineage immune response, characterized by stable B and T-cell memory, sustained surveillance, and high-titer antibody production (IgM/IgG) alongside a pro-inflammatory cytokine profile (IFN-γ and IL-2) for effective antigen clearance. These findings suggest that our vaccine outperforms any other Dengue vaccine developed to date. However, since this study was conducted through in silico methods, in vitro and in vivo validations are required to confirm the vaccine as a potential candidate for clinical trials.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue imposes a profound global impact, with millions affected annually, and despite efforts, no impeccable antiviral treatment exists to date, highlighting the urgency for a vaccine.
Design and population
This study employed an immunoinformatics approach to design an epitope-based tetravalent subunit vaccine aimed at confronting all DENV serotypes, with no specific population or clinical cohort involved.
Core methods
The study involved epitope prediction and screening, physicochemical assessment, molecular docking, molecular dynamics (MD) simulations, immune simulations, and in silico cloning to evaluate the vaccine construct.
Main results
The vaccine construct showed an antigenicity of 0.9319 with 97.35% population coverage worldwide; molecular docking with TLR2 and TLR4 revealed promising interactions with lowest energies of −1240.5 kJ/mol and −1393.3 kJ/mol, respectively, and MD simulations confirmed stability and flexibility.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that the vaccine outperforms any other Dengue vaccine developed to date, as evidenced by its robust immune response and high antigenicity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest potential for improved dengue prevention through a tetravalent subunit vaccine with broad population coverage and strong immune stimulation, though further validation is needed.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is limited because the study was conducted entirely through in silico methods, and in vitro and in vivo validations are required to confirm the vaccine as a potential candidate for clinical trials.

索马里 登革热 发病率 预测的机械、统计和混合模型的比较性能。回顾性时间序列分析

Comparative Performance of Mechanistic, Statistical, and Hybrid Models of Forecasting Dengue Fever Incidence in Somalia. A Retrospective Time Series Analysis
作者： Abdiftah Mohamud Abdi, Saralees Nadarajah, Abdisalam Hassan Muse
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景和目标 登革热 对索马里来说是一个日益严重的威胁,索马里是一个气候变化易发地区,医疗保健系统薄弱。公共卫生的当务之急是有效的预测模型。本文将对机械模型、统计模型和混合模型进行详细的比较分析,以便找到在 登革热 数据稀疏的情况下使用的最佳预测模型。方法 我们利用从“我们的数据世界”获得的登革热年度 发病率 原始报告数据(1990-2021 年,N = 32)。由于缺乏标准化病例定义以及索马里 监测 系统固有的高漏报率,预测代表的是预计报告的负担,而不是真实的感染率。数据分为训练集和测试集(分别为 1990-2016 年和 2017-2021 年)。我们使用平均集成策略测试了机械感染-感染-恢复 (SIR) 模型、8 个单一时间序列模型(ARIMA、ETS、TBATS 和 NNAR)以及 12 个混合模型。研究结果 虽然时间序列模型(单独)提供了预测能力的基础知识,但混合 ARIMA-TBATS 模型是最成功的,并且超越了其他模型。它对测试数据进行了最高精度的统计,平均绝对百分比误差(MAPE)为6.49%,均方根误差(RMSE)为663.81,并且优于MAPE为7.14%的最佳单一模型(TBATS)。SIR模型计算的基本再生数(R0)为1.015,表明该病具有地方性流行。结论 本文的结论是,在计划预测索马里登革热时,ARIMA-TBATS 混合工具应该是考虑的工具中经验最有效的工具。现实世界应用:该 ARIMA-TBATS 框架旨在作为索马里国家登革热早期预警系统 (DEWS) 的数学引擎,允许公共卫生官员在 发病率 高峰出现前几个月,通过运送医疗用品、动员熏蒸小组以及向高危地区分发蚊帐来抢占 暴发。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 在索马里这个气候变化易发地区且医疗保健系统薄弱的地区面临着日益严重的威胁,本研究旨在找到在这种数据稀疏的情况下 登革热 的最佳预测模型。
设计与对象
这项回顾性时间序列分析利用了从“我们的数据世界”获得的 1990 年至 2021 年登革热年度 发病率 原始报告数据(N = 32)。
核心方法
我们测试了机械感染-感染-恢复 (SIR) 模型、8 个单时间序列模型(ARIMA、ETS、TBATS 和 NNAR)以及使用平均集成策略的 12 个混合模型,数据分为训练集(1990-2016 年)和测试集(2017-2021 年)。
主要结果
混合 ARIMA-TBATS 模型最为成功,平均绝对百分比误差 (MAPE) 为 6.49%,均方根误差 (RMSE) 为 663.81,优于最佳单一模型 (TBATS),后者的 MAPE 为 7.14%;SIR模型计算出的基本再生数(R0)为1.015,表明是地方病。
解释与创新
作者得出的结论是,ARIMA-TBATS 混合模型是索马里登革热预测中经验最有效的工具,代表了一种针对数据稀疏环境量身定制的新颖方法。
科研与公卫意义
该 ARIMA-TBATS 框架旨在作为索马里国家登革热早期预警系统 (DEWS) 的数学引擎,使公共卫生官员能够在 发病率 高峰出现前几个月,通过运送医疗用品、动员熏蒸小组以及向高危地区分发蚊帐来抢占 暴发。
局限与证据强度
由于缺乏标准化病例定义以及索马里 监测 系统固有的严重漏报情况,预测代表的是预计报告的负担,而不是真实的感染率;鉴于回顾性设计和对报告数据的依赖,证据强度中等。

Comparative Performance of Mechanistic, Statistical, and Hybrid Models of Forecasting Dengue Fever Incidence in Somalia. A Retrospective Time Series Analysis

Authors: Abdiftah Mohamud Abdi, Saralees Nadarajah, Abdisalam Hassan Muse
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background and Aims Dengue fever is a growing menace in Somalia, a climate change prone region with a weak healthcare system. An imperative of public health is effective forecasting models. This paper will provide a detailed comparative analysis comparing mechanistic, statistical, and hybrid models in order to find the best forecasting model to use in this data‐sparse situation of dengue fever. Methods We utilized raw reported annual incidence data of dengue (1990–2021, N = 32) obtained from Our World in Data. Due to the lack of standardized case definitions and high under‐reporting inherent to Somalia's surveillance system, forecasts represent the projected reported burden rather than true infection rates. The data was divided into a training and a testing set (1990–2016 and 2017–2021, respectively). We tested a mechanistic Susceptible‐Infected‐Recovered (SIR) model, 8 single time series models (ARIMA, ETS, TBATS, and NNAR), and 12 hybrids using an averaging ensemble strategy. Findings Although the time series models (individually) offered the basics of predictive power, the hybrid ARIMA‐TBATS model was the most successful and it surpassed the other models. It performed the highest accuracy statistics on the testing data with a Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE) of 6.49% and a Root Mean Squared Error (RMSE) of 663.81 and outperformed the best single model (TBATS) which had a MAPE of 7.14%. The basic reproduction number (R0) calculated by the SIR model was 1.015 which shows that the disease is endemic. Conclusion This paper concludes that the ARIMA‐TBATS hybrid should be the most empirically effective tool out of the ones considered when planning to forecast dengue in Somalia. Real‐World Application: This ARIMA‐TBATS framework is designed to serve as the mathematical engine for a National Dengue Early Warning System (DEWS) in Somalia, allowing public health officials to physically preempt outbreaks by routing medical supplies, mobilizing fumigation teams, and distributing bed nets to high‐risk zones months before peak incidence occurs.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever is a growing menace in Somalia, a climate change prone region with a weak healthcare system, and this study aimed to find the best forecasting model for dengue fever in this data-sparse situation.
Design and population
This retrospective time series analysis utilized raw reported annual incidence data of dengue from 1990 to 2021 (N = 32) obtained from Our World in Data.
Core methods
We tested a mechanistic Susceptible-Infected-Recovered (SIR) model, 8 single time series models (ARIMA, ETS, TBATS, and NNAR), and 12 hybrids using an averaging ensemble strategy, with data divided into training (1990–2016) and testing (2017–2021) sets.
Main results
The hybrid ARIMA-TBATS model was the most successful, with a Mean Absolute Percentage Error (MAPE) of 6.49% and a Root Mean Squared Error (RMSE) of 663.81, outperforming the best single model (TBATS) which had a MAPE of 7.14%; the SIR model calculated a basic reproduction number (R0) of 1.015, indicating endemic disease.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors concluded that the ARIMA-TBATS hybrid is the most empirically effective tool for forecasting dengue in Somalia, representing a novel approach tailored to data-sparse settings.
Scientific and public-health significance
This ARIMA-TBATS framework is designed to serve as the mathematical engine for a National Dengue Early Warning System (DEWS) in Somalia, enabling public health officials to preempt outbreaks by routing medical supplies, mobilizing fumigation teams, and distributing bed nets to high-risk zones months before peak incidence occurs.
Limitations and evidence strength
Due to the lack of standardized case definitions and high under-reporting inherent to Somalia's surveillance system, forecasts represent the projected reported burden rather than true infection rates; the evidence strength is moderate given the retrospective design and reliance on reported data.

儿科登革热的抗体反应表明缅甸 DENV-3 基因型 I 和 III 的出现和共同循环以及正黄病毒交叉反应

Antibody Responses in Pediatric Dengue Indicate Emergence and Co‐Circulation of DENV‐3 Genotypes I and III in Myanmar and Orthoflavivirus Cross‐Reactivity
作者： Jean Claude Balingit, Khine Mya Nwe, Htin Lin, Wah Wah Aung, Ryosuke Suzuki, Yuki Takamatsu, Takeshi Urano, Aye Aye Khin, Kyaw Zin Thant, Hlaing Myat Thu
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革病毒 (DENV) 仍然是东南亚的一个主要公共卫生问题,对儿童的影响尤为严重。在缅甸,DENV-3 基因型 III 在 2017 年之前占主导地位,此后出现了基因型 I。为了评估这种现象对抗体反应的影响,我们分析了 2017 年至 2019 年间收集的 38 名儿童登革热患者的配对急性和恢复期血清样本以及 2004 年存档的样本。针对 DENV-3 基因型 I 和 III 以及其他血清型评估了中和作用和体外抗体依赖性增强 (ADE),并对寨卡病毒和日本脑炎病毒进行了交叉反应性分析。2004 年的样本显示,基因型 I 的中和作用减少,ADE 较高,这与当时不存在的情况一致。相比之下,2017-2019 年的样本对两种基因型表现出相当的中和作用,具有基因型和年份依赖性的 ADE 差异,表明基因型 I 的出现与不断发展的功能性抗体谱相关。因此,为 DENV-3 基因型 I 和 III 在缅甸的持续共循环提供了免疫学证据。未来对循环正黄病毒及其基因型进行连续 监测 的前瞻性儿科 队列研究 研究对于阐明流行地区预先存在的登革热免疫如何影响基因型出现和交叉正黄病毒免疫至关重要。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒 (DENV) 仍然是东南亚的一个主要公共卫生问题,对儿童的影响尤为严重。该研究旨在了解缅甸出现 DENV-3 基因型 I 的免疫学影响。
设计与对象
该研究分析了 2017 年至 2019 年间收集的 38 名儿童登革热患者的急性期和恢复期配对血清样本,以及 2004 年存档的样本。它重点关注 DENV-3 基因型 I 和 III 及其对缅甸抗体反应的影响。
核心方法
为了评估 DENV-3 基因型 I 出现的影响,针对 DENV-3 基因型 I 和 III 以及其他血清型评估了中和和体外 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE)。还分析了与寨卡病毒和日本脑炎病毒的交叉反应。
主要结果
2004 年的样本显示,基因型 I 的中和作用减少,ADE 较高,这与当时不存在的情况一致。相比之下,2017-2019 年的样本对两种基因型表现出相当的中和作用,并具有基因型和年份依赖性的 ADE 差异。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,基因型 I 的出现与功能性抗体谱的演变有关。这为 DENV-3 基因型 I 和 III 在缅甸持续共同循环提供了免疫学证据。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果有助于了解抗体反应如何随着新的 DENV-3 基因型的出现而演变,这对于 监测 和 疫苗 在流行地区的发展至关重要。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其回顾性设计和对存档样本的依赖而受到限制。证据是中等的,因为它是基于抗体谱的横截面分析而不是纵向或实验数据。

Antibody Responses in Pediatric Dengue Indicate Emergence and Co‐Circulation of DENV‐3 Genotypes I and III in Myanmar and Orthoflavivirus Cross‐Reactivity

Authors: Jean Claude Balingit, Khine Mya Nwe, Htin Lin, Wah Wah Aung, Ryosuke Suzuki, Yuki Takamatsu, Takeshi Urano, Aye Aye Khin, Kyaw Zin Thant, Hlaing Myat Thu
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Dengue virus (DENV) remains a major public health concern in Southeast Asia, disproportionately affecting children. In Myanmar, DENV‐3 Genotype III predominated before 2017, with Genotype I emerging thereafter. To assess the impact of this emergence on antibody responses, we analyzed paired acute and convalescent serum samples from 38 pediatric dengue patients collected between 2017 and 2019 alongside archived 2004 samples. Neutralization and in vitro antibody‐dependent enhancement (ADE) were assessed against DENV‐3 Genotypes I and III and other serotypes, with cross‐reactivity analysis to Zika and Japanese encephalitis viruses. Samples from 2004 showed reduced neutralization and higher ADE for Genotype I, consistent with its absence at the time. In contrast, 2017–2019 samples exhibited comparable neutralization for both genotypes, with genotype‐ and year‐dependent ADE differences, indicating that Genotype I emergence was associated with evolving functional antibody profiles. Hence, providing immunological evidence for continued co‐circulation of DENV‐3 Genotypes I and III in Myanmar. Future prospective pediatric cohort studies with continuous surveillance of circulating orthoflaviviruses and their genotypes, are essential to elucidate how pre‐existing dengue immunity shapes genotype emergence and cross‐orthoflavivirus immunity in endemic regions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus (DENV) remains a major public health concern in Southeast Asia, disproportionately affecting children. The study aimed to understand the immunological implications of the emergence of DENV-3 Genotype I in Myanmar.
Design and population
The study analyzed paired acute and convalescent serum samples from 38 pediatric dengue patients collected between 2017 and 2019, alongside archived 2004 samples. It focused on DENV-3 Genotypes I and III and their impact on antibody responses in Myanmar.
Core methods
To assess the impact of the emergence of DENV-3 Genotype I, neutralization and in vitro antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE) were evaluated against DENV-3 Genotypes I and III and other serotypes. Cross-reactivity with Zika and Japanese encephalitis viruses was also analyzed.
Main results
Samples from 2004 showed reduced neutralization and higher ADE for Genotype I, consistent with its absence at the time. In contrast, 2017–2019 samples exhibited comparable neutralization for both genotypes, with genotype- and year-dependent ADE differences.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings indicate that the emergence of Genotype I was associated with evolving functional antibody profiles. This provides immunological evidence for the continued co-circulation of DENV-3 Genotypes I and III in Myanmar.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results contribute to understanding how antibody responses evolve with the emergence of new DENV-3 genotypes, which is critical for surveillance and vaccine development in endemic regions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its retrospective design and reliance on archived samples. The evidence is moderate, as it is based on cross-sectional analysis of antibody profiles rather than longitudinal or experimental data.

线粒体复合物 I 缺乏症患者 重症登革热 出血热的免疫调节疗法:病例报告

Immune Modulatory Therapy for Severe Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever in a Patient With Mitochondrial Complex I Deficiency: A Case Report
作者： Audra N. Iness, Ameya S. Walimbe, Emily R. Strouphauer, Michio Hirano, Alejandro D. Iglesias, Valentina Emmanuele, Caitlin C. Griffin, Sheldon L. Kaplan, William A. Gomes, Karen K. Moeller
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革病毒 (DENV) 构成了严重的全球健康挑战,特别是登革出血热 (DHF) 病例。由于 DENV 对线粒体依赖性细胞过程和免疫功能的特定影响,患有先前存在线粒体疾病的患者可能面临更高的并发症风险。我们描述了一名 16 岁男性,该男性患有已知的线粒体复合物 I 缺陷,该缺陷由 NDUFV1 中的纯合可能致病性变异引起,随后患上 DHF。他的病情迅速恶化,虚弱、呼吸困难、颅内出血和癫痫发作。他的临床病程因脑炎、蛛网膜炎和脊髓炎而进一步复杂化,需要广泛的免疫调节。他接受了皮质类固醇、静脉注射免疫球蛋白 (IVIG) 和血浆置换 (PLEX) 治疗,功能状态得到改善并部分恢复。该病例强调了 登革热 的几种罕见并发症、DENV 对线粒体功能的影响,并强调了治疗患有潜在线粒体疾病的个体传染病的复杂性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒 (DENV) 构成了严重的全球健康挑战,特别是登革出血热 (DHF) 病例。由于 DENV 对线粒体依赖性细胞过程和免疫功能的特定影响,患有先前存在线粒体疾病的患者可能面临更高的并发症风险。
设计与对象
我们描述了一名 16 岁男性,该男性患有已知的线粒体复合物 I 缺陷,该缺陷由 NDUFV1 中的纯合可能致病性变异引起,随后患上 DHF。
核心方法
他的临床病程因脑炎、蛛网膜炎和脊髓炎而进一步复杂化,需要广泛的免疫调节。他接受了皮质类固醇、静脉注射免疫球蛋白 (IVIG) 和血浆置换 (PLEX) 治疗,功能状态得到改善并部分恢复。
主要结果
他的病情迅速恶化,虚弱、呼吸困难、颅内出血和癫痫发作。
解释与创新
该病例强调了 登革热 的几种罕见并发症、DENV 对线粒体功能的影响,并强调了治疗患有潜在线粒体疾病的个体传染病的复杂性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,需要提高对患有线粒体疾病的登革热患者的认识和量身定制的管理策略,强调免疫调节治疗在严重病例中的作用。
局限与证据强度
该证据基于单个 病例报告,限制了普遍性,但为线粒体复合物 I 缺乏症患者的登革热罕见并发症提供了详细的临床见解。

Immune Modulatory Therapy for Severe Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever in a Patient With Mitochondrial Complex I Deficiency: A Case Report

Authors: Audra N. Iness, Ameya S. Walimbe, Emily R. Strouphauer, Michio Hirano, Alejandro D. Iglesias, Valentina Emmanuele, Caitlin C. Griffin, Sheldon L. Kaplan, William A. Gomes, Karen K. Moeller
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Dengue virus (DENV) poses a serious global health challenge, particularly in cases of dengue hemorrhagic fever (DHF). Patients with preexisting mitochondrial disorders may be at increased risk for complications due to the specific impact of DENV on mitochondrial‐dependent cellular processes and immune function. We describe a 16‐year‐old male with known mitochondrial complex I deficiency caused by a homozygous likely pathogenic variant in NDUFV1 who subsequently developed DHF. His illness was marked by rapidly worsening weakness, respiratory distress, intracranial hemorrhage, and seizures. His clinical course was further complicated by encephalitis, arachnoiditis, and myelitis requiring extensive immunomodulation. He received corticosteroids, intravenous immunoglobulin (IVIG), and plasma exchange (PLEX), which led to improvement and partial recovery of functional status. This case highlights several rare complications of dengue fever, the impact of DENV on mitochondrial function, and underscores the complexity of managing infectious diseases in individuals with underlying mitochondrial disorders.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus (DENV) poses a serious global health challenge, particularly in cases of dengue hemorrhagic fever (DHF). Patients with preexisting mitochondrial disorders may be at increased risk for complications due to the specific impact of DENV on mitochondrial‐dependent cellular processes and immune function.
Design and population
We describe a 16‐year‐old male with known mitochondrial complex I deficiency caused by a homozygous likely pathogenic variant in NDUFV1 who subsequently developed DHF.
Core methods
His clinical course was further complicated by encephalitis, arachnoiditis, and myelitis requiring extensive immunomodulation. He received corticosteroids, intravenous immunoglobulin (IVIG), and plasma exchange (PLEX), which led to improvement and partial recovery of functional status.
Main results
His illness was marked by rapidly worsening weakness, respiratory distress, intracranial hemorrhage, and seizures.
Interpretation and novelty
This case highlights several rare complications of dengue fever, the impact of DENV on mitochondrial function, and underscores the complexity of managing infectious diseases in individuals with underlying mitochondrial disorders.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform the need for heightened awareness and tailored management strategies for dengue patients with mitochondrial disorders, emphasizing the role of immunomodulatory therapy in severe cases.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on a single case report, limiting generalizability, but provides detailed clinical insights into a rare complication of dengue in a patient with mitochondrial complex I deficiency.

疫苗 病毒血症的特征、减毒逆转和安全性:TAK-003 登革热 疫苗 临床开发计划的综合结果。

Characterization of vaccine viremia, attenuation reversion, and safety: Combined results from the TAK-003 dengue vaccine clinical development program.
作者： Vianney Tricou, Xavier Saez-Llorens, Pope Kosalaraksa, Humberto Reynales, Chukiat Sirivichayakul, Charissa Borja-Tabora, Jenny G. Low, William B. Messer (180472), Kelley J. Moss, Sanja Mandaric
摘要中文翻译减毒活 疫苗 可以在 疫苗接种 后短暂复制,并可能与类似自然感染的症状相关。四价登革热 疫苗(TAK-003)已接受广泛的临床前评估,以研究 疫苗 RNA 血症、病毒血症、遗传稳定性和传播性。在这里,我们研究了整个临床开发项目参与者中 疫苗 RNAemia 的存在、减毒基因座逆转及其与病毒综合征样不良事件 (AE) 的关联。对 TAK-003 RNA 阳性血清样本进行病毒复制评估和逆转测序。安全性数据(7/14 天内主动发生的 AE;28 天内主动主动发生的 AE)通过汇总 1/2 期试验分析进行评估。在关键的 3 期 DEN-301 试验和 2 期 DEN-313 试验中,在 疫苗接种 后 30 天内出现发热性疾病的参与者接受了 RNA 血症检测并评估了 AE。在汇总分析中,441/805 (54.8%) 名 疫苗 患者(血清阴性:67.0%;血清阳性:33.0%)以及 DEN-301/DEN-313 试验(调查的 1022 例发热性疾病)中的 39 名发热性疾病参与者中检测到 TAK-003 RNA 血症。RNA 血症在 疫苗接种 后第二周达到峰值,并且大多发生在第一次给药后。尽管检测到所有四种 登革病毒 (DENV) 成分,但 RNAemia 最常见的是 DENV-2。检测到 69 个单基因座回复;逆转参与者报告的 AE 一般不严重,并不表明症状严重程度增加。注射部位疼痛(无 RNA 血症 = 42.0%;RNA 血症 = 46.8%)和头痛(无 RNA 血症 = 36.6%;RNA 血症 = 37.6%)是最常报告的引起的 AE。RNA 血症参与者的疼痛、红斑、皮疹、关节痛和疲劳 发病率 较高。患有 RNA 血症的参与者 (62.4%) 中主动出现的 AE 的 发病率 高于未患有 RNA 血症的参与者 (52.0%)。在 DEN-301/DEN-313 分析中,患有发热性疾病的参与者在有或没有 RNA 血症的情况下表现出相似的症状模式。RNA 血症的存在与短暂的轻度至中度症状相关,类似于自然感染,在不存在 RNA 血症的情况下也观察到这种情况。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 TAK-003 登革热 疫苗 临床开发计划参与者中 疫苗 RNAemia 的存在、减毒基因座逆转及其与病毒综合征样不良事件的关联,解决对减毒活 疫苗 可能导致类似于自然感染的短暂症状的担忧。
设计与对象
该分析包括汇总的 1/2 期试验数据以及来自关键 3 期 DEN-301 试验和 2 期 DEN-313 试验的参与者,这些参与者在 疫苗接种 后 30 天内出现发热性疾病,并对 TAK-003 RNA 阳性血清样本进行病毒复制评估和逆转测序。
核心方法
安全性数据(7/14 天内主动不良事件;28 天内主动不良事件)通过汇总 1/2 期试验进行评估,DEN-301/DEN-313 试验中患有发热性疾病的参与者接受了 RNA 血症检测并评估了不良事件。
主要结果
在 疫苗 的 441/805 例 (54.8%) 中检测到 TAK-003 RNA 血症,在 疫苗接种 后第二周达到峰值,大部分发生在第一次剂量后,其中 DENV-2 是检测到的最常见成分;发现了 69 个单基因座逆转,发生逆转的参与者的不良事件通常并不严重。
解释与创新
作者解释说,RNA 血症与类似于自然感染的短暂、轻度至中度症状相关,在没有 RNA 血症的情况下也观察到这种症状,表明衰减逆转并不表明症状严重程度增加。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,TAK-003 疫苗 病毒血症很常见,但一般较轻微,支持其安全性,并有助于在 监测 和临床环境中区分 疫苗 相关症状与自然登革热感染。
局限与证据强度
由于依赖汇总试验数据和发热性疾病病例的观察分析,证据强度中等,尽管大样本量和全面测序提高了可靠性,但可能会引入选择偏差。

Characterization of vaccine viremia, attenuation reversion, and safety: Combined results from the TAK-003 dengue vaccine clinical development program.

Authors: Vianney Tricou, Xavier Saez-Llorens, Pope Kosalaraksa, Humberto Reynales, Chukiat Sirivichayakul, Charissa Borja-Tabora, Jenny G. Low, William B. Messer (180472), Kelley J. Moss, Sanja Mandaric
Original abstract (English)
Live-attenuated vaccines can replicate for a transient period post-vaccination and may be associated with symptoms resembling natural infection. The tetravalent dengue vaccine, TAK-003, has undergone extensive preclinical assessment to investigate vaccine RNAemia, viremia, genetic stability, and transmissibility. Here, we investigated the presence of vaccine RNAemia, attenuation loci reversions, and their association with viral-syndrome-like adverse events (AEs) in participants throughout the clinical development program. TAK-003 RNA positive serum samples were evaluated for viral replication and sequenced for reversions. Safety data (solicited AEs within 7/14 days; unsolicited AEs within 28 days) were assessed from a pooled phase 1/2 trial analysis. Participants who experienced febrile illness within 30 days post vaccination in the pivotal phase 3 DEN-301 trial and the phase 2 DEN-313 trial were tested for RNAemia and evaluated for AEs. TAK-003 RNAemia was detected in 441/805 (54.8%) of vaccinees (seronegative: 67.0%; seropositive: 33.0%) in the pooled analysis and in 39 participants with febrile illness from the DEN-301/DEN-313 trials (from 1022 febrile illnesses investigated). RNAemia peaked during the second week post-vaccination and mostly occurred after first dose. Although all four dengue virus (DENV) components were detected, RNAemia was most commonly due to DENV-2. 69 single locus reversions were detected; AEs reported in participants with reversions were generally non-serious, and not indicative of increased symptom severity. Pain at injection site (without RNAemia = 42.0%; RNAemia = 46.8%) and headache (without RNAemia = 36.6%; RNAemia = 37.6%) were the most frequently reported solicited AEs. Incidences of pain, erythema, rash, arthralgia, and fatigue were higher in participants with RNAemia. The incidence of unsolicited AEs was higher in participants with RNAemia (62.4%) than without (52.0%). In the DEN-301/DEN-313 analysis, participants with febrile illness showed a similar pattern of symptoms with and without RNAemia. The presence of RNAemia was associated with transient, mild-to-moderate symptoms, resembling natural infection, which were also observed in the absence of RNAemia.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the presence of vaccine RNAemia, attenuation loci reversions, and their association with viral-syndrome-like adverse events in participants throughout the TAK-003 dengue vaccine clinical development program, addressing concerns about live-attenuated vaccines potentially causing transient symptoms resembling natural infection.
Design and population
The analysis included pooled phase 1/2 trial data and participants from the pivotal phase 3 DEN-301 trial and phase 2 DEN-313 trial who experienced febrile illness within 30 days post-vaccination, with TAK-003 RNA-positive serum samples evaluated for viral replication and sequenced for reversions.
Core methods
Safety data (solicited adverse events within 7/14 days; unsolicited adverse events within 28 days) were assessed from pooled phase 1/2 trials, and participants with febrile illness in DEN-301/DEN-313 trials were tested for RNAemia and evaluated for adverse events.
Main results
TAK-003 RNAemia was detected in 441/805 (54.8%) of vaccinees, peaking during the second week post-vaccination and mostly occurring after the first dose, with DENV-2 being the most common component detected; 69 single locus reversions were found, and adverse events in participants with reversions were generally non-serious.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that RNAemia was associated with transient, mild-to-moderate symptoms resembling natural infection, which were also observed in the absence of RNAemia, demonstrating that attenuation reversions did not indicate increased symptom severity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform that TAK-003 vaccine viremia is common but generally mild, supporting its safety profile and aiding in distinguishing vaccine-related symptoms from natural dengue infection in surveillance and clinical settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the reliance on pooled trial data and observational analysis of febrile illness cases, which may introduce selection bias, though the large sample size and comprehensive sequencing enhance reliability.

登革病毒 包膜蛋白触发膜融合过程的计算见解

Computational insights into dengue virus envelope protein triggered membrane fusion process
作者： Dwaipayan Chaudhuri, Kalyan Giri
摘要中文翻译由 登革病毒 (DENV) 的包膜 (E) 蛋白启动的膜融合过程是病毒进入 宿主 细胞的关键步骤,但其分子机制仍然只有部分了解。在这里,我们结合使用全原子和粗粒度分子动力学 (MD) 模拟来模拟 登革病毒 包膜蛋白插入和进展到脂质双层的过程,捕获膜融合事件的关键阶段。全原子模拟提供了对初始蛋白质-脂质相互作用、蛋白质-脂质接触的时间 进化 以及 E 蛋白融合环的构象重排的原子洞察,而粗粒度模型可以观察长时间尺度的膜重塑事件,例如曲率生成。结构和动态分析揭示了 E 蛋白如何扰乱膜结构,促进进入部位目标膜的负曲率和类似于早期半融合中间体的相邻区域的正曲率。这些结果支持这样的假设:融合环充当膜锚,并且协调的蛋白质插入和倾斜对于催化膜合并至关重要。这项研究提供了 登革病毒 融合起始的详细分子视图,并证明了多尺度模拟方法在研究复杂膜蛋白相互作用中的价值。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
由 登革病毒 (DENV) 的包膜 (E) 蛋白启动的膜融合过程是病毒进入 宿主 细胞的关键步骤,但其分子机制仍然只有部分了解。
设计与对象
作者采用全原子和粗粒度分子动力学 (MD) 模拟的组合来模拟 登革病毒 包膜蛋白插入和进展到脂质双层的过程,捕获膜融合事件的关键阶段。
核心方法
全原子模拟提供了对初始蛋白质-脂质相互作用、蛋白质-脂质接触的时间进化以及E蛋白融合环的构象重排的原子学洞察,而粗粒度模型使得能够观察长时间尺度的膜重塑事件,例如曲率生成。
主要结果
结构和动力学分析揭示了 E 蛋白如何扰乱膜结构,促进入口部位的负曲率和类似于早期半融合中间体的相邻区域的正曲率。
解释与创新
这些发现支持这样的假设:融合环充当膜锚,协调的蛋白质插入和倾斜对于催化膜合并至关重要,为 登革病毒 融合起始提供了一种新的机制观点。
科研与公卫意义
该研究提供了 登革病毒 融合起始的详细分子视图,并证明了多尺度模拟方法对于研究复杂膜蛋白相互作用的价值,为融合过程的未来治疗靶向提供信息。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据不报告任何限制或证据强度的评估。

Computational insights into dengue virus envelope protein triggered membrane fusion process

Authors: Dwaipayan Chaudhuri, Kalyan Giri
Original abstract (English)
The membrane fusion process initiated by the Envelope (E) protein of the Dengue virus (DENV) is a critical step in viral entry into host cells, yet its molecular mechanism remains only partially understood. Here, we employ a combination of all-atom and coarse-grained molecular dynamics (MD) simulations to model the insertion and progression of the dengue virus Envelope protein into a lipid bilayer, capturing key stages of the membrane fusion event. All-atom simulations provide atomistic insight into initial protein-lipid interactions, time evolution of protein-lipid contacts and conformational rearrangements of the E protein fusion loop, while coarse-grained models enable observation of long-timescale membrane remodeling events, such as curvature generation. Structural and dynamic analyses reveal how the E protein perturbs the membrane architecture, promoting negative curvature in the target membrane at the site of entry and positive curvature in adjoining regions that resemble early hemifusion intermediates. These results support the hypothesis that the fusion loop acts as a membrane anchor, and that coordinated protein insertion and tilting are essential for catalyzing membrane merger. This study offers a detailed molecular view of dengue virus fusion initiation and demonstrates the value of multiscale simulation approaches in studying complex membrane-protein interactions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The membrane fusion process initiated by the Envelope (E) protein of the Dengue virus (DENV) is a critical step in viral entry into host cells, yet its molecular mechanism remains only partially understood.
Design and population
The authors employed a combination of all-atom and coarse-grained molecular dynamics (MD) simulations to model the insertion and progression of the dengue virus Envelope protein into a lipid bilayer, capturing key stages of the membrane fusion event.
Core methods
All-atom simulations provided atomistic insight into initial protein‐lipid interactions, time evolution of protein‐lipid contacts and conformational rearrangements of the E protein fusion loop, while coarse‐grained models enabled observation of long‐timescale membrane remodeling events such as curvature generation.
Main results
Structural and dynamic analyses revealed how the E protein perturbs membrane architecture, promoting negative curvature at the entry site and positive curvature in adjoining regions that resemble early hemifusion intermediates.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings support the hypothesis that the fusion loop acts as a membrane anchor and that coordinated protein insertion and tilting are essential for catalyzing membrane merger, offering a novel mechanistic view of dengue virus fusion initiation.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study provides a detailed molecular view of dengue virus fusion initiation and demonstrates the value of multiscale simulation approaches for investigating complex membrane‐protein interactions, informing future therapeutic targeting of the fusion process.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report any limitations or an assessment of evidence strength.

登革热中的 P 物质和 NK-1R:首次血清学研究

Substance P and NK‐1R in Dengue: A First Serological Investigation
作者： Riffat Mehboob, Imran Shahid, Shahzad Ahmad, Noreen Sarwar, Akash John, Zeeshan Mehboob, Majid Almansouri, Waleed Badoghaish
摘要中文翻译摘要 背景 登革热 缺乏可靠的生物标志物来解释其复杂的病理生理学。P 物质 (SP) 及其受体神经激肽-1 受体 (NK1R) 是病毒感染中炎症和血管通透性的已知调节因子,可能在登革热中发挥作用,但尚未得到探索。目的/目的 检测确诊登革热患者血清样本中 SP 和 NK1R 的水平。方法 使用方便抽样技术从确诊的 登革热 患者中选出参与者 (n = 40),以及健康对照组 (n = 12)。收集血清样本,并使用酶联免疫吸附测定(ELISA)测量 SP 和 NK1R 水平。还评估了全血细胞计数(CBC)参数。结果对照组和病例组的平均 NK1R 水平分别为 2.681 ± 3.147 和 0.57 ± 0.131。与对照组相比,登革热患者的 NK1R 水平显着降低 (p < 0.0001)。男性和女性登革热患者之间的 SP 水平没有显着差异(Mann-Whitney U = 85,p = 0.899)。结论 NK1R 可能通过其潜在参与血管通透性、免疫激活和细胞因子反应而与登革热 发病机制 相关。这些发现表明 NK1R 与登革热感染的病理生理机制之间可能存在联系。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 缺乏可靠的生物标志物来解释其复杂的病理生理学,P 物质 (SP) 及其受体神经激肽-1 受体 (NK1R) 可能在登革热中发挥作用,但仍未得到探索。
设计与对象
采用方便抽样的方式进行了血清学调查,选择了 40 名确诊的 登革热 患者和 12 名健康对照者。
核心方法
收集血清样本,并使用酶联免疫吸附测定(ELISA)测量 SP 和 NK1R 水平,并评估全血细胞计数(CBC)参数。
主要结果
与对照组 (2.681 ± 3.147) 相比,登革热患者的平均 NK1R 水平显着降低 (0.57 ± 0.131) (p < 0.0001),而男性和女性登革热患者之间的 SP 水平没有显着差异 (Mann–Whitney U = 85,p = 0.899)。
解释与创新
研究结果表明 NK1R 与登革热感染的病理生理机制之间可能存在联系,NK1R 可能参与血管通透性、免疫激活和细胞因子反应。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果可能为未来登革热 发病机制 的生物标志物研究提供信息,并有助于了解 NK1R 在血管并发症和免疫失调中的作用。
局限与证据强度
由于样本量较小(n = 40 个病例,n = 12 个对照)并且使用了便利抽样,因此该研究的证据强度中等,这可能会限制普遍性。

Substance P and NK‐1R in Dengue: A First Serological Investigation

Authors: Riffat Mehboob, Imran Shahid, Shahzad Ahmad, Noreen Sarwar, Akash John, Zeeshan Mehboob, Majid Almansouri, Waleed Badoghaish
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Background Dengue fever lacks reliable biomarkers to explain its complex pathophysiology. Substance P (SP) and its receptor, the Neurokinin‐1 receptor (NK1R), known regulators of inflammation and vascular permeability in viral infections, may play a role in dengue but remain unexplored. Aim/Objective To investigate the levels of SP and NK1R in serum samples of diagnosed dengue patients. Methods Participants were selected from a pool of diagnosed dengue fever patients using a convenience sampling technique ( n = 40), along with a control group of healthy individuals ( n = 12). Serum samples were collected, and SP and NK1R levels were measured using enzyme‐linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA). Complete blood count (CBC) parameters were also evaluated. Results The mean NK1R levels in the control and case groups were 2.681 ± 3.147 and 0.57 ± 0.131, respectively. NK1R levels were significantly lower in dengue patients compared with controls ( p < 0.0001). SP levels did not differ significantly between male and female dengue patients (Mann–Whitney U = 85, p = 0.899). Conclusion NK1R may be associated with dengue pathogenesis through its potential involvement in vascular permeability, immune activation, and cytokine responses. These findings suggest a possible connection between NK1R and the pathophysiological mechanisms underlying dengue infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever lacks reliable biomarkers to explain its complex pathophysiology, and Substance P (SP) and its receptor, the Neurokinin‐1 receptor (NK1R), may play a role in dengue but remain unexplored.
Design and population
A serological investigation was conducted using convenience sampling to select 40 diagnosed dengue fever patients and 12 healthy control individuals.
Core methods
Serum samples were collected, and SP and NK1R levels were measured using enzyme‐linked immunosorbent assay (ELISA), with complete blood count (CBC) parameters also evaluated.
Main results
The mean NK1R levels were significantly lower in dengue patients (0.57 ± 0.131) compared to controls (2.681 ± 3.147) (p < 0.0001), while SP levels did not differ significantly between male and female dengue patients (Mann–Whitney U = 85, p = 0.899).
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest a possible connection between NK1R and the pathophysiological mechanisms underlying dengue infection, with NK1R potentially involved in vascular permeability, immune activation, and cytokine responses.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results may inform future biomarker research for dengue pathogenesis and could contribute to understanding the role of NK1R in vascular complications and immune dysregulation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence strength is moderate due to the small sample size (n = 40 cases, n = 12 controls) and the use of convenience sampling, which may limit generalizability.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

登革病毒 2 媒介 暴露于热浪的埃及伊蚊的能力因 5-甲基胞嘧啶 RNA 破坏而降低

Dengue virus 2 vector competence in heatwave-exposed Aedes aegypti mosquitoes is reduced by 5-methylcytosine RNA disruption
作者： Fabiola Claudio-Piedras, Benito Recio-Tótoro, Humberto Lanz-Mendoza, Jorge Cime-Castillo, Humberto Lanz‐Mendoza, Jorge Cime‐Castillo
摘要中文翻译极端天气事件可以通过其主要的 媒介 埃及伊蚊,深刻改变 登革病毒 (DENV) 的 传播 动态。了解蚊子如何应对热应激以及它如何影响病毒 传播 对于为全球公共卫生战略提供信息至关重要。RNA 胞嘧啶甲基化是埃及伊蚊中一种进化上保守的应激反应机制,它将环境扰动与 RNA 代谢和基因表达的变化联系起来。在这里,我们表明,获得 DENV2 时引发的为期 5 天的热浪延长了幸存蚊子的寿命,从而延长了潜在病毒 传播 的窗口期。暴露于热浪的蚊子表现出整体 5-甲基胞嘧啶 RNA (5mC-RNA) 水平升高并抑制 抗病毒药物 基因表达,促进病毒有效复制。用氮胞苷对 5mC-RNA 进行药理学破坏,通过增强 抗病毒药物 转录、将 DENV2 感染 患病率 从 78% 降低至 37%,并将蚊子晚年存活率恢复到与非应激对照组相当的水平,从而抵消热浪引起的 传播 潜力的增加。这种综合方法揭示了一种表观转录组机制,通过该机制,热浪等气候极端情况可以放大登革热 传播 风险,并强调 RNA 甲基化是缓解 DENV 传播 的潜在目标。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
极端天气事件可以通过其主要的 媒介 埃及伊蚊,深刻改变 登革病毒 (DENV) 的 传播 动态。了解蚊子如何应对热应激以及它如何影响病毒 传播 对于为全球公共卫生战略提供信息至关重要。
设计与对象
该研究调查了为期 5 天的热浪对感染 DENV2 的埃及伊蚊的影响,检查了寿命、病毒 传播 潜力和 RNA 甲基化水平的变化。
核心方法
研究人员在获取 DENV2 时将蚊子暴露在热浪中,并测量了整体 5-甲基胞嘧啶 RNA (5mC-RNA) 水平、抗病毒药物 基因表达和病毒复制。使用氮胞苷对 5mC-RNA 进行药理学破坏,以评估其对 传播 潜力的影响。
主要结果
暴露于热浪的蚊子表现出整体 5mC-RNA 水平升高并抑制 抗病毒药物 基因表达,促进病毒有效复制。5mC-RNA 的药理学破坏将 DENV2 感染 患病率 从 78% 降低至 37%,并将蚊子的晚年存活率恢复到与非应激对照组相当的水平。
解释与创新
作者解释说,热浪通过涉及 RNA 甲基化的表观转录组机制放大了登革热 抗病毒药物 风险,RNA 甲基化抑制了 抗病毒药物 反应。这项研究强调 RNA 甲基化是在气候压力下缓解 DENV 传播 的潜在目标。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,针对 RNA 甲基化可能是一种在热浪期间减少登革热 监测 的新策略,为面临极端气候的地区的 监测 和干预计划提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究表明热应激、RNA 甲基化和登革热 传播 之间存在很强的机制联系,但需要进一步的研究来在实地环境中验证这些发现并评估长期生态影响。

Dengue virus 2 vector competence in heatwave-exposed Aedes aegypti mosquitoes is reduced by 5-methylcytosine RNA disruption

Authors: Fabiola Claudio-Piedras, Benito Recio-Tótoro, Humberto Lanz-Mendoza, Jorge Cime-Castillo, Humberto Lanz‐Mendoza, Jorge Cime‐Castillo
Original abstract (English)
Extreme weather events can profoundly alter the transmission dynamics of dengue virus (DENV) by its primary vector, Aedes aegypti. Understanding how mosquitoes respond to heat stress and how this affects viral transmission is critical for informing global public health strategies. RNA cytosine methylation is an evolutionarily conserved stress-response mechanism in A. aegypti that links environmental perturbations to shifts in RNA metabolism and gene expression. Here, we show that a 5-day heatwave initiated at the time of DENV2 acquisition increases the lifespan of surviving mosquitoes, thereby extending the window of potential virus transmission. Heatwave-exposed mosquitoes exhibit elevated global 5-methylcytosine RNA (5mC-RNA) levels and suppressed antiviral gene expression, facilitating efficient viral replication. Pharmacological disruption of 5mC-RNA with azacytidine counteracts heatwave-induced increases in transmission potential by enhancing antiviral transcription, reducing DENV2 infection prevalence from 78% to 37%, and restoring mosquito late-life survival to rates comparable to non-stressed controls. This integrative approach reveals an epitranscriptomic mechanism by which climate extreme scenarios like heatwaves can amplify dengue transmission risk, and highlights RNA methylation as a potential target for mitigating DENV transmission.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Extreme weather events can profoundly alter the transmission dynamics of dengue virus (DENV) by its primary vector, Aedes aegypti. Understanding how mosquitoes respond to heat stress and how this affects viral transmission is critical for informing global public health strategies.
Design and population
The study investigated the effects of a 5-day heatwave on Aedes aegypti mosquitoes infected with DENV2, examining changes in lifespan, viral transmission potential, and RNA methylation levels.
Core methods
Researchers exposed mosquitoes to a heatwave at the time of DENV2 acquisition and measured global 5-methylcytosine RNA (5mC-RNA) levels, antiviral gene expression, and viral replication. Pharmacological disruption of 5mC-RNA was performed using azacytidine to assess its impact on transmission potential.
Main results
Heatwave-exposed mosquitoes exhibited elevated global 5mC-RNA levels and suppressed antiviral gene expression, facilitating efficient viral replication. Pharmacological disruption of 5mC-RNA reduced DENV2 infection prevalence from 78% to 37% and restored mosquito late-life survival to rates comparable to non-stressed controls.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret that heatwaves amplify dengue transmission risk through an epitranscriptomic mechanism involving RNA methylation, which suppresses antiviral responses. This study highlights RNA methylation as a potential target for mitigating DENV transmission under climate stress.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that targeting RNA methylation could be a novel strategy to reduce dengue transmission during heatwaves, informing surveillance and intervention programs in regions facing climate extremes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study demonstrates a strong mechanistic link between heat stress, RNA methylation, and dengue transmission, but further research is needed to validate these findings in field settings and assess long-term ecological impacts.

未成熟血小板分数预测 登革热 中不同的血小板恢复轨迹:纵向分析

Immature platelet fraction predicts differential platelet recovery trajectories in dengue fever: A longitudinal analysis
作者： Santosh Kumar Singh, Sandeep Kumar, P. V. Sreejith, Mehek Khanuja, Abhishek Kumar, Deepanjan Dey, Uday Yanamandra
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革热通常会导致血小板减少症,但仅血小板计数可能无法反映骨髓血小板生成情况。尽管纵向数据有限,但未成熟血小板分数(IPF)是血小板再生的潜在标志。因此,我们的目的是评估第 1 天的 IPF 是否可以预测随后的血小板恢复轨迹,并描述临床、生化和血小板指数的纵向趋势。这是一项在印度西马哈拉施特拉邦一家三级护理医院进行的回顾性纵向分析研究。667 名确诊登革热患者在第一天记录血小板计数、IPF 和世界卫生组织 (WHO) 分类,并在第 3 天和第 5 天进行随访。使用广义最小二乘回归分析第 3 天和第 5 天的对数转换血小板计数。使用弗里德曼检验评估纵向变化。中位年龄为 31 岁,其中 81.4% 为男性。血小板计数从 118 下降至 98 × 10 9 /L ( p = 0.0017),而 IPF 从 9.7% 增加至 12.9% ( p < 0.0001)。较高的基线 IPF 预示着更快的血小板恢复 (p < 0.0001)。高 IPF 组第 5 天的血小板持续下降低于低 IPF 组(28.6% vs. 47.8%;Kruskal-Wallis p = 0.013)。因此,我们得出结论,IPF 是预测登革热血小板恢复轨迹的动态生物标志物。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热通常会导致血小板减少,但仅血小板计数可能无法反映骨髓血小板生成。本研究旨在评估第 1 天未成熟血小板分数 (IPF) 是否可以预测随后的血小板恢复轨迹,并描述临床、生化和血小板指数的纵向趋势。
设计与对象
这是一项在印度西马哈拉施特拉邦一家三级护理医院进行的回顾性纵向分析研究。667 名确诊登革热患者,在第 1 天记录血小板计数、IPF 和世界卫生组织 (WHO) 分类,并在第 3 天和第 5 天进行随访。
核心方法
使用广义最小二乘回归分析第 3 天和第 5 天的对数转换血小板计数。使用弗里德曼检验评估纵向变化。
主要结果
血小板计数从 118 下降至 98 × 10 9 /L ( p = 0.0017),而 IPF 从 9.7% 增加至 12.9% ( p < 0.0001)。较高的基线 IPF 预示着更快的血小板恢复 (p < 0.0001)。高 IPF 组第 5 天的血小板持续下降低于低 IPF 组(28.6% vs. 47.8%;Kruskal-Wallis p = 0.013)。
解释与创新
作者得出结论,IPF 是一种动态生物标志物,可以预测登革热血小板恢复轨迹。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,IPF 可用于监测登革热患者的血小板恢复情况,可能有助于临床决策和资源分配。
局限与证据强度
该研究是回顾性的,基于一家三级医院的数据,这可能会限制普遍性。证据是中等的,因为它基于没有随机化或对照组的观察数据。

Immature platelet fraction predicts differential platelet recovery trajectories in dengue fever: A longitudinal analysis

Authors: Santosh Kumar Singh, Sandeep Kumar, P. V. Sreejith, Mehek Khanuja, Abhishek Kumar, Deepanjan Dey, Uday Yanamandra
Original abstract (English)
Summary Dengue commonly causes thrombocytopenia, but platelet count alone may not reflect marrow thrombopoiesis. Immature platelet fraction (IPF) is a potential marker of platelet regeneration, though longitudinal data are limited. We thus aimed to evaluate whether Day 1 IPF predicts subsequent platelet recovery trajectory and to describe longitudinal trends in clinical, biochemical and platelet indices. This was a retrospective longitudinal analytical study conducted at a tertiary care hospital in Western Maharashtra, India. Six hundred and sixty‐seven patients with confirmed dengue, with platelet count, IPF and World Health Organization (WHO) classification recorded on Day 1, with follow‐up at Day 3 and 5. Log‐transformed platelet counts at Day 3 and Day 5 were analysed using generalized least squares regression. Longitudinal changes were assessed using the Friedman test. Median age was 31 years, with 81.4% males. Platelet counts declined from 118 to 98 × 10 9 /L ( p = 0.0017), while IPF increased from 9.7% to 12.9% ( p < 0.0001). Higher baseline IPF predicted faster platelet recovery ( p < 0.0001). Continued platelet decline at Day 5 was lower in the high‐IPF group than the low‐IPF group (28.6% vs. 47.8%; Kruskal–Wallis p = 0.013). We thus conclude that IPF is a dynamic biomarker that predicts platelet recovery trajectory in dengue.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue commonly causes thrombocytopenia, but platelet count alone may not reflect marrow thrombopoiesis. This study aimed to evaluate whether Day 1 immature platelet fraction (IPF) predicts subsequent platelet recovery trajectory and to describe longitudinal trends in clinical, biochemical and platelet indices.
Design and population
This was a retrospective longitudinal analytical study conducted at a tertiary care hospital in Western Maharashtra, India. Six hundred and sixty‐seven patients with confirmed dengue, with platelet count, IPF and World Health Organization (WHO) classification recorded on Day 1, with follow‐up at Day 3 and 5.
Core methods
Log‐transformed platelet counts at Day 3 and Day 5 were analysed using generalized least squares regression. Longitudinal changes were assessed using the Friedman test.
Main results
Platelet counts declined from 118 to 98 × 10 9 /L ( p = 0.0017), while IPF increased from 9.7% to 12.9% ( p < 0.0001). Higher baseline IPF predicted faster platelet recovery ( p < 0.0001). Continued platelet decline at Day 5 was lower in the high‐IPF group than the low‐IPF group (28.6% vs. 47.8%; Kruskal–Wallis p = 0.013).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors conclude that IPF is a dynamic biomarker that predicts platelet recovery trajectory in dengue.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that IPF could be used to monitor platelet recovery in dengue patients, potentially aiding in clinical decision-making and resource allocation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was retrospective and based on data from a single tertiary care hospital, which may limit generalisability. The evidence is moderate as it is based on observational data without randomization or control groups.

XPO1依赖性核输出调节NS3定位并通过线粒体重塑和干扰素抑制促进DENV-2复制

XPO1-dependent nuclear export regulates NS3 localization and promotes DENV-2 replication through mitochondrial remodeling and interferon suppression
作者： Selvin Noé Palacios-Rápalo, Jonathan Hernández-Castillo, Luis Adrián De Jesús-González, Daniel Talamas-Lara, Carlos Daniel Cordero-Rivera, Bulmaro Cisneros-Vega, Jose Manuel Reyes-Ruiz, Rosa María del Ángel, Luis Adrián De Jesús‐González, Carlos Daniel Cordero‐Rivera
摘要中文翻译核细胞质运输对于细胞稳态至关重要,并且在感染过程中经常被病毒利用。尽管 登革病毒 (DENV) 非结构蛋白 3 (NS3) 经历核运输,但核输出途径在其定位和病毒复制中的作用仍知之甚少。在这里,我们发现输出蛋白 1 (XPO1) 的药理学抑制可促进受感染 Huh-7 细胞的细胞核和线粒体中 DENV-2 NS3 的积累。XPO1 抑制还会诱导线粒体形态变化,类似于 DENV 感染期间观察到的变化。此外,核输出的阻断通过减少I型干扰素的产生来增强干扰素刺激的细胞中的DENV-2复制,这表明促病毒细胞环境的建立。我们的研究结果表明,XPO1 介导的核输出有助于 NS3 定位的调节,并将核输出与 DENV 感染期间线粒体重塑和 抗病毒药物 信号传导抑制联系起来。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查核输出途径在 DENV-2 NS3 定位中的作用及其对病毒复制的影响。登革病毒 属于黄病毒科,利用核细胞质运输进行复制和 发病机制。
设计与对象
该研究是使用感染 DENV-2 的 Huh-7 细胞进行的。应用 XPO1 的药理学抑制来检查其对 NS3 定位和病毒复制的影响。
核心方法
研究人员利用 XPO1 的药理学抑制来评估其对 DENV-2 NS3 定位和复制的影响。他们观察到受感染细胞中线粒体形态和干扰素产生的变化。
主要结果
XPO1 的药理学抑制导致 DENV-2 NS3 在受感染 Huh-7 细胞的细胞核和线粒体中积累。它还诱导线粒体形态改变并减少 I 型干扰素的产生。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,在 DENV 感染期间,XPO1 介导的核输出在调节 NS3 定位、将核输出与线粒体重塑和 抗病毒药物 信号抑制联系起来方面发挥着关键作用。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果提供了对 DENV 复制机制的深入了解,并强调了 抗病毒药物 策略通过调节核输出途径的潜在治疗靶点。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于使用 Huh-7 细胞的体外实验,可能无法完全代表体内条件。证据是中等的,因为它是基于药理学抑制和细胞观察。

XPO1-dependent nuclear export regulates NS3 localization and promotes DENV-2 replication through mitochondrial remodeling and interferon suppression

Authors: Selvin Noé Palacios-Rápalo, Jonathan Hernández-Castillo, Luis Adrián De Jesús-González, Daniel Talamas-Lara, Carlos Daniel Cordero-Rivera, Bulmaro Cisneros-Vega, Jose Manuel Reyes-Ruiz, Rosa María del Ángel, Luis Adrián De Jesús‐González, Carlos Daniel Cordero‐Rivera
Original abstract (English)
Nucleocytoplasmic transport is essential for cellular homeostasis and is frequently exploited by viruses during infection. Although dengue virus (DENV) non-structural protein 3 (NS3) undergoes nuclear trafficking, the role of nuclear export pathways in its localization and in viral replication remains poorly understood. Here, we show that pharmacological inhibition of exportin 1 (XPO1) promotes the accumulation of DENV-2 NS3 in both the nucleus and mitochondria of infected Huh-7 cells. XPO1 inhibition also induces mitochondrial morphological alterations resembling those observed during DENV infection. Moreover, blockade of nuclear export enhances DENV-2 replication in interferon-stimulated cells by reducing type I interferon production, suggesting the establishment of a pro-viral cellular environment. Our findings reveal that XPO1-mediated nuclear export contributes to the regulation of NS3 localization and links nuclear export to mitochondrial remodeling and suppression of antiviral signaling during DENV infection.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the role of nuclear export pathways in the localization of DENV-2 NS3 and its impact on viral replication. Dengue virus, which belongs to the flavivirus family, exploits nucleocytoplasmic transport for its replication and pathogenesis.
Design and population
The study was conducted using Huh-7 cells infected with DENV-2. Pharmacological inhibition of XPO1 was applied to examine its effects on NS3 localization and viral replication.
Core methods
The researchers used pharmacological inhibition of XPO1 to assess its impact on DENV-2 NS3 localization and replication. They observed changes in mitochondrial morphology and interferon production in infected cells.
Main results
Pharmacological inhibition of XPO1 led to the accumulation of DENV-2 NS3 in both the nucleus and mitochondria of infected Huh-7 cells. It also induced mitochondrial morphological alterations and reduced type I interferon production.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that XPO1-mediated nuclear export plays a critical role in regulating NS3 localization and linking nuclear export to mitochondrial remodeling and suppression of antiviral signaling during DENV infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results provide insights into the mechanisms of DENV replication and highlight potential therapeutic targets for antiviral strategies by modulating nuclear export pathways.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to in vitro experiments using Huh-7 cells, which may not fully represent in vivo conditions. The evidence is moderate as it is based on pharmacological inhibition and cellular observations.

塞内加尔的分层分子 监测 框架将快速登革热检测与基因组 流行病学 连接起来。

A tiered molecular surveillance framework linking rapid dengue detection to genomic epidemiology in Senegal.
作者： Amy Gaye, Varun Vaidya, Mariama Toure, Ibrahima Mbaye Ndiaye, Sebastian Gallon, Mamadou Samb Yade, Bassirou Ngom, Djiby Sow, Ndeye Coumba Diop, Ousmane Kebe
摘要中文翻译背景:非洲的虫媒病毒 监测 受到诊断能力分散以及分子检测与基因组 流行病学 整合不足的限制,特别是在登革热 (DENV)、寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 和基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 病毒共同传播并出现重叠临床综合征的环境中。方法:我们在塞内加尔多个地区进行了一项分子 监测 研究,包括从 367 名发热或非发热疾病患者收集的样本。使用针对 DENV、ZIKV 和 CHIKV 的多重逆转录定量聚合酶链反应 (RT-qPCR) 筛选来实施分层工作流程,对单独测试的样本 (n=43) 和合并样本(每个池三个个体)进行组合。通过逆转录重组酶聚合酶扩增 (RT-RPA) 血清分型和基因组测序进一步表征 DENV RT-qPCR 阳性样本。结果:多重 RT-qPCR 显示多种虫媒病毒同时循环。在单独检测的样本中,DENV 阳性率为 20·9% (9/43),ZIKV 阳性率为 9·3% (4/43),CHIKV 阳性率为 11·6% (5/43)。在所有中心(108 个池)的汇总筛查中,DENV 阳性率为 18·5% (20/108),ZIKV 阳性率为 5·6% (6/108),CHIKV 阳性率为 10·2% (11/108),表明虫媒病毒 传播 广泛存在。RT-RPA 进行的 DENV-1-4 血清分型与 RT-qPCR 完全一致,并确定了 DENV-2 的专属循环,从而能够对样本进行分类以进行下游基因组测序。与宏基因组测序相比,基于扩增子的测序显着提高了 基因组 回收率,在 77·8% (14/18) 的 RT-RPA 阳性样本中产生接近完整的 基因组。系统发育分析表明,所有序列都聚集在 DENV-2 世界基因型(基因型 II)、谱系 II-F.1.1 内,与来自西非和亚洲的最新毒株密切相关。时间分辨重建表明最近出现(∼2022-2023)并快速扩张,与正在进行的 传播 和区域传播一致。解释:这些发现证明 DENV、ZIKV 和 CHIKV 在塞内加尔共同传播,并提供了 DENV-2 最近在全球连接的 传播 网络中扩张的证据。将汇总分子筛选与 RT-RPA 分类和基因组测序相结合的分层策略为资源有限环境中的虫媒病毒 监测 提供了可扩展的框架。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
非洲的虫媒病毒 监测 受到诊断能力分散以及分子检测与基因组 流行病学 整合不足的限制,特别是在登革热 (DENV)、寨卡病毒 (ZIKV) 和基孔肯雅病毒 (CHIKV) 病毒共同传播并出现重叠临床综合征的环境中。
设计与对象
我们在塞内加尔的多个地区进行了一项分子 监测 研究,包括从 367 名发热或非发热疾病患者收集的样本。
核心方法
使用针对 DENV、ZIKV 和 CHIKV 的多重逆转录定量聚合酶链反应 (RT-qPCR) 筛选来实施分层工作流程,对单独测试的样本 (n=43) 和合并样本(每个池三个个体)进行组合。通过逆转录重组酶聚合酶扩增 (RT-RPA) 血清分型和基因组测序进一步表征 DENV RT-qPCR 阳性样本。
主要结果
多重 RT-qPCR 显示多种虫媒病毒同时循环。在单独检测的样本中,DENV 阳性率为 20·9% (9/43),ZIKV 阳性率为 9·3% (4/43),CHIKV 阳性率为 11·6% (5/43)。在所有中心(108 个池)的汇总筛查中,DENV 阳性率为 18·5% (20/108),ZIKV 阳性率为 5·6% (6/108),CHIKV 阳性率为 10·2% (11/108),表明虫媒病毒 传播 广泛存在。
解释与创新
这些发现证明了 DENV、ZIKV 和 CHIKV 在塞内加尔的共同传播,并提供了 DENV-2 最近在全球连接的 传播 网络中扩张的证据。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提供了DENV-2最近出现和快速扩张的证据,这可以为塞内加尔和其他具有类似虫媒病毒动态的地区的有针对性的监测、诊断和公共卫生干预措施提供信息。
局限与证据强度
将汇总分子筛选与 RT-RPA 分类和基因组测序相结合的分层策略为资源有限环境中的虫媒病毒 监测 提供了可扩展的框架。

A tiered molecular surveillance framework linking rapid dengue detection to genomic epidemiology in Senegal.

Authors: Amy Gaye, Varun Vaidya, Mariama Toure, Ibrahima Mbaye Ndiaye, Sebastian Gallon, Mamadou Samb Yade, Bassirou Ngom, Djiby Sow, Ndeye Coumba Diop, Ousmane Kebe
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Arboviral surveillance in Africa is limited by fragmented diagnostic capacity and insufficient integration of molecular detection with genomic epidemiology, particularly in settings where dengue (DENV), Zika (ZIKV), and chikungunya (CHIKV) viruses co-circulate and present with overlapping clinical syndromes. METHODS: We conducted a molecular surveillance study across multiple regions in Senegal, including samples collected from 367 individuals with febrile or non-febrile illness. A tiered workflow was implemented using multiplex reverse transcription quantitative polymerase chain reaction (RT-qPCR) screening for DENV, ZIKV, and CHIKV, performed on a combination of individually tested samples (n=43) and pooled samples (three individuals per pool). DENV RT-qPCR-positive samples were further characterized by reverse transcription recombinase polymerase amplification (RT-RPA) serotyping and genomic sequencing. FINDINGS: Multiplex RT-qPCR revealed concurrent circulation of multiple arboviruses. Among individually tested samples, positivity rates were 20·9% (9/43) for DENV, 9·3% (4/43) for ZIKV, and 11·6% (5/43) for CHIKV. In pooled screening across all sites (108 pools), positivity rates were 18·5% for DENV (20/108), 5·6% for ZIKV (6/108), and 10·2% for CHIKV (11/108), indicating widespread arboviral transmission. DENV-1-4 serotyping by RT-RPA demonstrated complete concordance with RT-qPCR and identified exclusive circulation of DENV-2, enabling triage of samples for downstream genomic sequencing. Amplicon-based sequencing substantially improved genome recovery compared with metagenomic sequencing, yielding near-complete genomes in 77·8% (14/18) of RT-RPA-positive samples. Phylogenetic analyses demonstrated that all sequences clustered within the DENV-2 cosmopolitan genotype (genotype II), lineage II-F.1.1, closely related to recent strains from West Africa and Asia. Time-resolved reconstruction suggested recent emergence (∼2022-2023) and rapid expansion, consistent with ongoing transmission and regional dissemination. INTERPRETATION: These findings demonstrate co-circulation of DENV, ZIKV, and CHIKV in Senegal and provide evidence of recent expansion of DENV-2 within a globally connected transmission network. A tiered strategy integrating pooled molecular screening with RT-RPA triage and genomic sequencing offers a scalable framework for arboviral surveillance in resource-limited settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Arboviral surveillance in Africa is limited by fragmented diagnostic capacity and insufficient integration of molecular detection with genomic epidemiology, particularly in settings where dengue (DENV), Zika (ZIKV), and chikungunya (CHIKV) viruses co-circulate and present with overlapping clinical syndromes.
Design and population
We conducted a molecular surveillance study across multiple regions in Senegal, including samples collected from 367 individuals with febrile or non-febrile illness.
Core methods
A tiered workflow was implemented using multiplex reverse transcription quantitative polymerase chain reaction (RT-qPCR) screening for DENV, ZIKV, and CHIKV, performed on a combination of individually tested samples (n=43) and pooled samples (three individuals per pool). DENV RT-qPCR-positive samples were further characterized by reverse transcription recombinase polymerase amplification (RT-RPA) serotyping and genomic sequencing.
Main results
Multiplex RT-qPCR revealed concurrent circulation of multiple arboviruses. Among individually tested samples, positivity rates were 20·9% (9/43) for DENV, 9·3% (4/43) for ZIKV, and 11·6% (5/43) for CHIKV. In pooled screening across all sites (108 pools), positivity rates were 18·5% for DENV (20/108), 5·6% for ZIKV (6/108), and 10·2% for CHIKV (11/108), indicating widespread arboviral transmission.

These findings demonstrate co-circulation of DENV, ZIKV, and CHIKV in Senegal and provide evidence of recent expansion of DENV-2 within a globally connected transmission network.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide evidence of recent emergence and rapid expansion of DENV-2, which can inform targeted surveillance, diagnosis, and public health interventions in Senegal and other regions with similar arboviral dynamics.
Limitations and evidence strength
A tiered strategy integrating pooled molecular screening with RT-RPA triage and genomic sequencing offers a scalable framework for arboviral surveillance in resource-limited settings.

在阿拉伯联合酋长国迪拜的埃及伊蚊中检测到 登革病毒

Detection of Dengue Virus in Aedes aegypti Mosquitoes, Dubai, United Arab Emirates
作者： Norbert Nowotny, Noushad Karuvantevida, Elmahi El Tilib Gubran, Naseem Mohammed Rafee, Adel Abdulla AlKarrani, Ghanim Al Hammadi, Nasna Nassir, Dia Advani, Mohammed J Uddin, Jeremy V. Camp
摘要中文翻译继 2024 年 登革病毒 暴发 后,我们在阿拉伯联合酋长国迪拜进行了为期 1 年的蚊子调查。我们收集了 1,598 只埃及伊蚊,并在 5 个蚊子数量少于 20 只的池中检测到了 登革病毒。我们的研究结果强调了持续 媒介 监测 对于 登革病毒 在干旱城市环境中的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 2024 年在阿拉伯联合酋长国迪拜发生 登革病毒 暴发 后埃及伊蚊中是否存在 登革病毒。
设计与对象
在阿拉伯联合酋长国迪拜进行了一项为期1年的蚊子调查,以收集和分析埃及伊蚊(Aedes aegypti)进行登革病毒检测。
核心方法
该研究收集了 1,598 只埃及伊蚊,并通过合并方法对它们进行 登革病毒 检测。
主要结果
调查期间,在 5 个蚊子数量少于 20 只的蚊子中检测到了 登革病毒。
解释与创新
研究结果强调需要在城市地区持续实施 媒介 监测 来监测 登革病毒 活动。
科研与公卫意义
结果强调了在干旱城市环境中监测 登革病毒 以支持公共卫生干预措施的重要性。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于摘要报告,证据基于单一调查,这可能会影响研究结果的普遍性。

Detection of Dengue Virus in Aedes aegypti Mosquitoes, Dubai, United Arab Emirates

Authors: Norbert Nowotny, Noushad Karuvantevida, Elmahi El Tilib Gubran, Naseem Mohammed Rafee, Adel Abdulla AlKarrani, Ghanim Al Hammadi, Nasna Nassir, Dia Advani, Mohammed J Uddin, Jeremy V. Camp
Original abstract (English)
We performed a 1-year mosquito survey in Dubai, United Arab Emirates, after a dengue virus outbreak in 2024. We collected 1,598 Aedes aegypti mosquitoes and detected dengue virus in 5 pools of <20 mosquitoes. Our findings underscore the importance of sustained vector surveillance for dengue virus in arid urban environments.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the presence of dengue virus in Aedes aegypti mosquitoes following a dengue virus outbreak in Dubai, United Arab Emirates, in 2024.
Design and population
A 1-year mosquito survey was conducted in Dubai, United Arab Emirates, to collect and analyze Aedes aegypti mosquitoes for dengue virus detection.
Core methods
The study involved collecting 1,598 Aedes aegypti mosquitoes and testing them for dengue virus through pooling methods.
Main results
Dengue virus was detected in 5 pools of <20 mosquitoes during the survey.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings highlight the need for ongoing vector surveillance in urban areas to monitor dengue virus activity.
Scientific and public-health significance
The results emphasize the importance of monitoring dengue virus in arid urban environments to support public health interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to an abstract-only report, and the evidence is based on a single survey, which may affect the generalizability of the findings.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

广泛交叉反应中和并增强越南儿童登革热病例血清中对登革热、日本脑炎病毒和寨卡病毒的抗体反应。

Broadly cross-reactive neutralizing and enhancing antibody responses to dengue, Japanese encephalitis viruses and Zika in sera from Vietnamese pediatric dengue cases.
作者： Muhareva Raekiansyah, Mya Myat Ngwe Tun, Jean Claude Balingit, Thi Thanh Ngan Nguyen, Merveille Kapandji, Yuki Takamatsu, Takeshi Urano, Thi Quynh Mai Le, Futoshi Hasebe, Kouichi Morita
摘要中文翻译目的:了解保护性抗体反应和致病性抗体反应之间的平衡对于登革热免疫学和黄病毒 疫苗 开发至关重要。方法:对越南南部(2015-2017 年)儿科登革热患者的 50 对急性和恢复期血清样本进行了分析,包括各种疾病严重程度的原发性和继发性感染。使用内部验证的 DENV IgG ELISA 对原发性和继发性 DENV 感染进行分类。使用单轮感染颗粒(SRIP)针对 登革病毒 四种血清型(DENV)、日本脑炎病毒(JEV)和 ZIKA(ZIKV)评估中和抗体(NAb)滴度和 抗体依赖性增强(ADE)活性。结果:检测到针对所有 DENV 血清型的中和抗体 (NAb) 反应,其中针对 DENV-1 的中和抗体反应最高。Nab 滴度在恢复期增加,继发感染时的滴度通常高于原发感染时的滴度。与其他临床组相比,DSS 病例的恢复期血清中也观察到了更高的 NAB 滴度。ADE 活性针对 DENV-2 最明显,针对 DENV-4 最低,而峰值增强滴度 (PET) 在恢复期样本、继发感染和 DSS 病例中较高,并且与 NAb 滴度呈正相关。此外,DSS 病例的血清中观察到的增强活性高于较轻疾病类别的血清。还检测到针对 JEV 和 ZIKV 的交叉反应中和和增强抗体,其中针对 ZIKV 观察到更强的增强作用。结论:继发性和 重症登革热 中和免疫力的增强伴随着增强潜力的扩大,表明感染时免疫激活驱动的交叉反应性扩大的 重症登革热。保护性抗体和增强性抗体的共存可能有助于病毒控制和随后黄病毒感染期间的免疫发病机制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
了解保护性抗体反应和致病性抗体反应之间的平衡对于登革热免疫学和黄病毒 疫苗 开发至关重要。
设计与对象
对越南南部(2015-2017 年)儿科登革热患者的 50 对急性和恢复期血清样本进行了分析,包括各种疾病严重程度的原发性和继发性感染。
核心方法
使用内部验证的 DENV IgG ELISA 对原发性和继发性 DENV 感染进行分类。使用单轮感染颗粒(SRIP)针对 登革病毒 四种血清型(DENV)、日本脑炎病毒(JEV)和 ZIKA(ZIKV)评估中和抗体(NAb)滴度和 抗体依赖性增强(ADE)活性。
主要结果
针对所有 DENV 血清型均检测到中和抗体 (NAb) 反应,其中针对 DENV-1 的中和抗体反应最高。Nab 滴度在恢复期增加,继发感染时的滴度通常高于原发感染时的滴度。与其他临床组相比,DSS 病例的恢复期血清中也观察到了更高的 NAB 滴度。ADE 活性针对 DENV-2 最明显,针对 DENV-4 最低,而峰值增强滴度 (PET) 在恢复期样本、继发感染和 DSS 病例中较高,并且与 NAb 滴度呈正相关。在 DSS 病例的血清中观察到的增强活性高于较轻疾病类别的血清。
解释与创新
继发性和 重症登革热 中中和免疫力的增强伴随着增强潜力的扩大,表明感染时免疫激活驱动的扩大的交叉反应性 重症登革热。保护性抗体和增强性抗体的共存可能有助于病毒控制和随后黄病毒感染期间的免疫发病机制。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可能为开发更有效的登革热 疫苗 提供信息,并强调监测 重症登革热 患者抗体反应的重要性,以更好地了解免疫发病机制和改进 治疗 策略。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于越南南部儿童登革热患者的血清样本,这可能会影响普遍性。证据是中等的,因为它基于仅抽象的分析。

Broadly cross-reactive neutralizing and enhancing antibody responses to dengue, Japanese encephalitis viruses and Zika in sera from Vietnamese pediatric dengue cases.

Authors: Muhareva Raekiansyah, Mya Myat Ngwe Tun, Jean Claude Balingit, Thi Thanh Ngan Nguyen, Merveille Kapandji, Yuki Takamatsu, Takeshi Urano, Thi Quynh Mai Le, Futoshi Hasebe, Kouichi Morita
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVES: Understanding the balance between protective and pathogenic antibody responses is central to dengue immunology and flavivirus vaccine development. METHODS: Fifty paired acute and convalescent serum samples from pediatric dengue patients in southern Vietnam (2015-2017) were analyzed, including primary and secondary infections across a spectrum of disease severity. Primary and secondary DENV infections were classified using an in-house validated DENV IgG ELISA. Neutralizing antibody (NAb) titers and antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE) activities were evaluated against dengue virus the four serotypes (DENV), Japanese encephalitis virus (JEV) and ZIKA (ZIKV) using single-round infectious particles (SRIPs). RESULTS: Neutralizing antibody (NAb) responses were detected against all DENV serotypes and were highest against DENV-1. NAb titers increased during convalescence and were generally higher in secondary than primary infections. Higher NAb titers were also observed in convalescent sera from DSS cases compared with other clinical groups. ADE activity was most pronounced against DENV-2 and was lowest against DENV-4, while Peak enhancement titers (PET) were higher in convalescent samples, secondary infections, and DSS cases, and were positively correlated with NAb titers. Additionally, higher enhancement activity was observed in sera from DSS cases than in those from milder disease categories. Cross-reactive neutralizing and enhancing antibodies against JEV and ZIKV were also detected, with stronger enhancement observed against ZIKV. CONCLUSION: Heightened neutralizing immunity in secondary and severe dengue is accompanied by expanded enhancement potential, indicating an expanded, cross-reactive immune response driven by immune activation upon infection. The coexistence of protective and enhancing antibodies may contribute both to viral control and to immunopathogenesis during subsequent flavivirus infections.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Understanding the balance between protective and pathogenic antibody responses is central to dengue immunology and flavivirus vaccine development.
Design and population
Fifty paired acute and convalescent serum samples from pediatric dengue patients in southern Vietnam (2015-2017) were analyzed, including primary and secondary infections across a spectrum of disease severity.
Core methods
Primary and secondary DENV infections were classified using an in-house validated DENV IgG ELISA. Neutralizing antibody (NAb) titers and antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE) activities were evaluated against dengue virus the four serotypes (DENV), Japanese encephalitis virus (JEV) and ZIKA (ZIKV) using single-round infectious particles (SRIPs).
Main results
Neutralizing antibody (NAb) responses were detected against all DENV serotypes and were highest against DENV-1. NAb titers increased during convalescence and were generally higher in secondary than primary infections. Higher NAb titers were also observed in convalescent sera from DSS cases compared with other clinical groups. ADE activity was most pronounced against DENV-2 and was lowest against DENV-4, while Peak enhancement titers (PET) were higher in convalescent samples, secondary infections, and DSS cases, and were positively correlated with NAb titers. Higher enhancement activity was observed in sera from DSS cases than in those from milder disease categories.
Interpretation and novelty
Heightened neutralizing immunity in secondary and severe dengue is accompanied by expanded enhancement potential, indicating an expanded, cross-reactive immune response driven by immune activation upon infection. The coexistence of protective and enhancing antibodies may contribute both to viral control and to immunopathogenesis during subsequent flavivirus infections.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings may inform the development of more effective dengue vaccines and highlight the importance of monitoring antibody responses in patients with severe dengue to better understand immunopathogenesis and improve treatment strategies.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to serum samples from pediatric dengue patients in southern Vietnam, which may affect generalizability. The evidence is moderate as it is based on an abstract-only analysis.

现实世界中的登革热 疫苗:为什么长期 监测 很重要。

Dengue vaccines in the real world: why long-term surveillance matters.
作者： Maíra Aguiar
摘要中文翻译2026 年 6 月 8 日,当正在调查 疫苗接种 后的罕见事件时,巴西暂时停止了目前的 疫苗接种 策略,即单剂量登革热 疫苗 Butantan-Dengue 疫苗 (Butantan-DV)。1-3 这种暂停既不应被解释为 疫苗 失败的证据,也不应削弱登革热预防方面关键进展的重要性。相反,这一暂停凸显了一个更广泛的教训:对于登革热 疫苗,有关功效、安全性和公共卫生益处的结论不应仅停留在监管部门的批准上。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了登革热 监测 的长期 监测 的需要,以评估其现实世界的影响和安全性,强调监测 疫苗接种 后可能出现的罕见不良事件的重要性。
设计与对象
该研究调查了巴西于 2026 年 6 月 8 日暂时停止使用单剂量登革热 疫苗 Butantan-DV,原因是 疫苗接种 接受调查后发生罕见事件。该事件作为案例研究,强调对 疫苗 安全和公共卫生的更广泛影响。
核心方法
这些方法涉及分析 Butantan-DV 疫苗 暂时停用的背景,包括对正在调查的罕见事件的监管和公共卫生反应。
主要结果
主要结果包括暂时暂停使用 Butantan-DV 的 疫苗接种 策略,该策略是由于对 疫苗接种 后罕见事件的调查而启动的。
解释与创新
作者将暂停解释为提醒,有关 疫苗 功效、安全性和公共卫生益处的结论不应仅限于监管部门的批准,强调需要进行持续的现实世界监测。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了长期 监测 对于登革热 疫苗 确保其在现实环境中的安全性和有效性的重要性,这可以为未来的公共卫生策略和 疫苗 部署提供信息。
局限与证据强度
证据基于仅摘要的报告,这限制了分析的深度。然而,该研究为 疫苗 的暂时终止及其对公共卫生的影响提供了明确的背景。

Dengue vaccines in the real world: why long-term surveillance matters.

Authors: Maíra Aguiar
Original abstract (English)
On June 8, 2026, Brazil temporarily discontinued its current vaccination strategy with the single-dose dengue vaccine Butantan-Dengue Vaccine (Butantan-DV) when rare events after vaccination were under investigation.1–3 This pause should neither be interpreted as evidence of vaccine failure nor diminish the importance of a key advancement in dengue prevention. Rather, the pause highlights a broader lesson: for dengue vaccines, conclusions regarding efficacy, safety, and public-health benefit should not stop at regulatory approval.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the need for long-term surveillance of dengue vaccines to assess their real-world impact and safety, highlighting the importance of monitoring rare adverse events that may arise post-vaccination.
Design and population
The study examines Brazil's temporary discontinuation of its single-dose dengue vaccine Butantan-DV on June 8, 2026, due to rare events after vaccination being under investigation. This event serves as a case study to emphasize the broader implications for vaccine safety and public health.
Core methods
The methods involve analyzing the context of the temporary discontinuation of the Butantan-DV vaccine, including the regulatory and public health responses to the rare events under investigation.
Main results
The main results include the temporary pause in the vaccination strategy with Butantan-DV, which was initiated due to the investigation of rare events following vaccination.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the pause as a reminder that conclusions about vaccine efficacy, safety, and public health benefit should not be limited to regulatory approval, emphasizing the need for ongoing real-world monitoring.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the importance of long-term surveillance for dengue vaccines to ensure their safety and effectiveness in real-world settings, which can inform future public health strategies and vaccine deployment.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence is based on an abstract-only report, which limits the depth of analysis. However, the study provides a clear context for the temporary discontinuation of the vaccine and its implications for public health.
