苏丹东部的 患病率 和与 登革热 血清型相关的因素:基于医院的横断面研究

Prevalence and factors associated with dengue fever serotypes in Eastern Sudan: a hospital-based cross-sectional study
作者： Abdualmoniem O. Musa, Abdel Rhman S. Hajnour, Asma Mohammed Almuatasim, Elddai H. Elnaem, Amal Huseen Karar, Ahmed A. Hassan, Tajeldin M. Abdallah
摘要中文翻译登革热是苏丹的一个严重的公共卫生威胁,快速城市化和气候因素加剧了这一威胁。2024 年卡萨拉州的 暴发 发生在激烈的内部冲突和大规模人口流离失所的情况下,协同加速了 传播 并使临床管理复杂化。本研究旨在确定 登革病毒 (DENV) 血清型的 患病率,并确定 2024 年流行期间卡萨拉居民和境内流离失所者 (IDP) 中与特定血清型相关的社会人口学和临床因素。卡萨拉教学医院进行了一项以医院为基础的横断面研究(2024 年 10 月至 12 月)。使用一步实时 PCR 分析 227 名确诊登革热患者(NS1、IgM 或两者均呈 RDT 阳性)的血清样本进行血清分型。进行单变量和多变量逻辑回归分析以确定与最流行血清型相关的因素。在已确诊的227例登革热中,DENV-2是主要血清型(59.9%),其次是DENV-3(35.2%)和DENV-1(4.8%)。值得注意的是,52% 的患者是国内流离失所者,30.4% 的患者患有严重形式 (DHF/DSS)。总体 死亡率 率为 6.6%,记录的 15 例死亡中有 13 例发生在流离失所者中。虽然一小部分 DENV-1 患者(11 例中有 4 例)的死亡率很高,但由于样本量有限,必须极其谨慎地解释这一发现。多变量分析显示,不使用蚊帐是与 DENV-2 感染相关的唯一独立因素(调整优势比,AOR = 2.20;95% CI:1.23–3.90;P = 0.007)。在年龄、性别或流离失所状况方面,血清型之间没有发现显着差异。2024 年登革热 暴发 标志着从 DENV-3 到 DENV-2 主导地位的重大转变,由于系统性流离失所因素而不是毒株主导,高 死亡率 负担严重集中在易受伤害的国内流离失所者中。不使用蚊帐与 DENV-2 之间的密切关联凸显了在流离失所者营地中迫切需要有针对性地分发长效杀虫蚊帐 (LLIN) 和 媒介 控制。此外,DENV-1小亚群的高死亡率强调了加强分子监测来监测冲突地区的毒力菌株的必要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热是苏丹的一个严重的公共卫生威胁,快速城市化和气候因素加剧了这一威胁。本研究旨在确定 登革病毒 (DENV) 血清型的 患病率,并确定 2024 年流行期间卡萨拉居民和境内流离失所者 (IDP) 中与特定血清型相关的社会人口学和临床因素。
设计与对象
卡萨拉教学医院进行了一项以医院为基础的横断面研究(2024 年 10 月至 12 月)。在确诊的 227 例 登革热 病例中,52% 的患者为境内流离失所者,30.4% 为重症(DHF/DSS)。
核心方法
使用一步实时 PCR 分析 227 名确诊登革热患者(NS1、IgM 或两者均呈 RDT 阳性)的血清样本进行血清分型。进行单变量和多变量逻辑回归分析以确定与最流行血清型相关的因素。
主要结果
在确诊的227例登革热病例中,DENV-2是主要血清型(59.9%),其次是DENV-3(35.2%)和DENV-1(4.8%)。总体 死亡率 率为 6.6%,记录的 15 例死亡中有 13 例发生在流离失所者中。多变量分析显示,不使用蚊帐是与 DENV-2 感染相关的唯一独立因素(调整优势比,AOR = 2.20;95% CI:1.23–3.90;P = 0.007)。
解释与创新
2024 年登革热 暴发 标志着从 DENV-3 到 DENV-2 主导地位的重大转变,由于系统性流离失所因素而不是毒株主导,高 死亡率 负担严重集中在易受伤害的国内流离失所者中。不使用蚊帐与 DENV-2 之间的密切关联凸显了在流离失所者营地中迫切需要有针对性地分发长效杀虫蚊帐 (LLIN) 和 媒介 控制。
科研与公卫意义
DENV-1 小型亚群的高死亡率强调了加强分子 监测 来监测冲突地区毒力菌株的必要性。此外,研究结果强调需要采取有针对性的 媒介 控制措施,以减少高危人群中的登革热 传播。
局限与证据强度
由于样本量有限,必须极其谨慎地解释一小部分 DENV-1 患者的高死亡率。证据强度中等,因为该研究为 患病率 血清型和相关因素提供了有价值的见解,但受到其横断面设计和某些血清型样本量较小的限制。

Prevalence and factors associated with dengue fever serotypes in Eastern Sudan: a hospital-based cross-sectional study

Authors: Abdualmoniem O. Musa, Abdel Rhman S. Hajnour, Asma Mohammed Almuatasim, Elddai H. Elnaem, Amal Huseen Karar, Ahmed A. Hassan, Tajeldin M. Abdallah
Original abstract (English)
Dengue is a critical public health threat in Sudan, exacerbated by rapid urbanization and climatic factors. The 2024 outbreak in Kassala State occurred amidst a violent internal conflict and massive population displacement, synergistically accelerating transmission and complicating clinical management. This study aimed to determine the prevalence of dengue virus (DENV) serotypes and identify sociodemographic and clinical factors associated with specific serotypes among residents and internally displaced persons (IDPs) in Kassala during the 2024 epidemic. A hospital-based cross-sectional study was conducted at Kassala Teaching Hospital (October–December 2024). Serum samples from 227 confirmed dengue patients (RDT-positive for NS1, IgM, or both) were analyzed using one-step Real-Time PCR for serotyping. Univariate and multivariate logistic regression analyses were performed to identify factors associated with the most prevalent serotypes. Among the confirmed 227 dengue fever, DENV-2 was the dominant serotype (59.9%), followed by DENV-3 (35.2%) and DENV-1 (4.8%). Notably, 52% of patients were IDPs, and 30.4% presented with severe forms (DHF/DSS). The overall mortality rate was 6.6%, with 13 out of the 15 recorded deaths occurring among the displaced population. While a high fatality rate was noted among the small subset of DENV-1 patients (4 out of 11), this finding must be interpreted with extreme caution due to the limited sample size. Multivariate analysis revealed that the non-use of mosquito nets was the only independent factor associated with DENV-2 infection (adjusted odds ratio, AOR = 2.20; 95% CI: 1.23–3.90; P = 0.007). No significant differences were found between serotypes regarding age, gender, or displacement status. The 2024 dengue outbreak marked a significant shift from DENV-3 to DENV-2 dominance, with a high mortality burden clustering heavily within vulnerable IDPs due to systemic displacement factors rather than strain dominance. The strong association between net non-use and DENV-2 highlights the urgent need for targeted long-lasting insecticidal nets (LLINs) distribution and vector control in displacement camps. Additionally, the high fatality rate in the small DENV-1 subset underscores the necessity of strengthened molecular surveillance to monitor virulent strains in conflict zones.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue is a critical public health threat in Sudan, exacerbated by rapid urbanization and climatic factors. This study aimed to determine the prevalence of dengue virus (DENV) serotypes and identify sociodemographic and clinical factors associated with specific serotypes among residents and internally displaced persons (IDPs) in Kassala during the 2024 epidemic.
Design and population
A hospital-based cross-sectional study was conducted at Kassala Teaching Hospital (October–December 2024). Among the confirmed 227 dengue fever cases, 52% of patients were IDPs, and 30.4% presented with severe forms (DHF/DSS).
Core methods
Serum samples from 227 confirmed dengue patients (RDT-positive for NS1, IgM, or both) were analyzed using one-step Real-Time PCR for serotyping. Univariate and multivariate logistic regression analyses were performed to identify factors associated with the most prevalent serotypes.
Main results
Among the confirmed 227 dengue fever cases, DENV-2 was the dominant serotype (59.9%), followed by DENV-3 (35.2%) and DENV-1 (4.8%). The overall mortality rate was 6.6%, with 13 out of the 15 recorded deaths occurring among the displaced population. Multivariate analysis revealed that the non-use of mosquito nets was the only independent factor associated with DENV-2 infection (adjusted odds ratio, AOR = 2.20; 95% CI: 1.23–3.90; P = 0.007).
Interpretation and novelty
The 2024 dengue outbreak marked a significant shift from DENV-3 to DENV-2 dominance, with a high mortality burden clustering heavily within vulnerable IDPs due to systemic displacement factors rather than strain dominance. The strong association between net non-use and DENV-2 highlights the urgent need for targeted long-lasting insecticidal nets (LLINs) distribution and vector control in displacement camps.
Scientific and public-health significance
The high fatality rate in the small DENV-1 subset underscores the necessity of strengthened molecular surveillance to monitor virulent strains in conflict zones. Additionally, the findings emphasize the need for targeted vector control measures to reduce dengue transmission in high-risk populations.
Limitations and evidence strength
The high fatality rate among the small subset of DENV-1 patients must be interpreted with extreme caution due to the limited sample size. The evidence strength is moderate, as the study provides valuable insights into serotype prevalence and associated factors but is limited by its cross-sectional design and small sample size for certain serotypes.

最佳控制分析和成本 – 登革热 传播 模型的有效性以及应用控制干预措施的实施时间效应

Optimal Control Analysis and Cost – Effectiveness of Dengue Transmission Model Along With Implementation Time Effects in Applying the Control Interventions
作者： Manisha, Anuj Kumar, Sunmi ‍Lee, Prashant K. Srivastava
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革热 对全球公共卫生构成重大威胁,其有效管理需要采取综合方法来控制登革热 患病率。本研究提出了登革热 传播 的最优控制问题,结合了三个关键控制措施:信息诱导的自我保护、饱和 治疗 和用于 媒介 控制的农药使用,并使用庞特里亚金极大值原理进行了分析。重要的是,在成本结构中纳入了一种新颖的更高非线性。通过数值模拟设计和评估了七种不同的控制策略,结合了不同的控制措施组合。结果表明,所有控制的组合策略(策略)是最有效的,可在大约 60 天内将感染 患病率 降低至最低水平,同时在此后保持持续控制。相比之下,信息和治疗(政策)的结合使用取得了类似的结果,但需要近120天才能稳定感染。在单一控制策略中,单独应用时,信息诱导的自我保护(策略)优于 治疗()和农药施用()。使用 CCER、IAR 和 ICER 进行的成本效益分析表明,政策是最具成本效益的干预措施,CCER 值最低,而政策避免的感染数量最多。此外,分析表明,控制的有效性在很大程度上取决于流行病的严重程度:对于轻度暴发(),组合策略(例如治疗和农药)可以有效发挥作用,而对于严重的暴发(),综合控制()变得至关重要。此外,控制措施的延迟实施会显着恶化疾病结果,增加感染高峰和经济负担。总体而言,该研究强调了及时和综合干预策略的重要性,并为登革热控制中的最佳资源分配提供了定量见解。

Optimal Control Analysis and Cost – Effectiveness of Dengue Transmission Model Along With Implementation Time Effects in Applying the Control Interventions

Authors: Manisha, Anuj Kumar, Sunmi ‍Lee, Prashant K. Srivastava
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT Dengue fever poses a significant global public health threat, and its effective management requires a comprehensive approach to control the dengue prevalence. This study proposes an optimal control problem for the dengue transmission, incorporating three key control measures: information‐induced self‐protection, saturated treatment, and pesticide use for vector control, and is analyzed using Pontryagin's Maximum Principle. Importantly, a novel higher nonlinearity in the cost construction is incorporated. Seven distinct control policies () incorporating different combinations of control measures are designed and evaluated through numerical simulations. The results demonstrate that the combined strategy of all controls (Policy ) is the most effective, reducing infection prevalence to minimal levels within approximately 60 days, while maintaining sustained control thereafter. In comparison, the combined use of information and treatment (Policy ) achieves a similar outcome but requires nearly 120 days to stabilize the infection. Among single‐control strategies, information‐induced self‐protection (Policy ) outperforms treatment () and pesticide application () when applied alone. Cost‐effectiveness analysis using CCER, IAR, and ICER reveals that Policy is the most cost‐effective intervention, with the lowest CCER value, while Policy averts the highest number of infections. Furthermore, the analysis shows that the control's effectiveness depends strongly on epidemic severity: for mild outbreaks (), combined strategies such as and (treatment and pesticide) perform efficiently, whereas for severe outbreaks (), comprehensive control () becomes essential. Additionally, delayed implementation of control measures significantly worsens disease outcomes, increasing infection peaks and economic burden. Overall, the study highlights the importance of timely and integrated intervention strategies and provides quantitative insights for optimal resource allocation in dengue control.

Limitations and evidence strength

苏丹医生登革热管理实践的知识与实践差距、态度和决定因素:横断面研究,2026 年

Knowledge–Practice Gap, Attitudes, and Determinants of Dengue Management Practices Among Physicians in Sudan: A Cross-Sectional Study, 2026
作者： Mohannad Abdalfdeel Almahie Shaban, Thoraya Salah Murtada Sidahmed, Mohamed Yassin, Hamza Essam Mohamed Abbas, Marwa Imad Awad Ali, Omnia Abdelmonim Mohamed Babiker, Fatima Alamin Mohammed AbdAlrhman, Al-Romaysa M. Osman Khalafalla El-Haj, Altayeb Abayazeed Altayeb Yusuf, Osama Shaban
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 是苏丹的一种新出现的公共卫生威胁,该国脆弱且受冲突影响的卫生系统内经常发生 登革热,而关于登革热相关知识是否能转化为适当的临床实践的证据有限。
设计与对象
2026 年 2 月至 4 月期间,对在苏丹执业的医生进行了一项在线横断面调查。
核心方法
使用根据经过验证的登革热知识、态度和实践 (KAP) 工具改编的结构化调查问卷来收集数据,并进行多变量逻辑回归来确定与高登革热管理实践独立相关的因素。
主要结果
共有633名医师参加;只有3.8%的人表现出较高的疾病知识,28.8%的人具有较高的媒介知识,40.0%的人获得较高的实践分数;只有 36.2% 的人表示熟悉世卫组织登革热指南,57.7% 的人表示资源不足。
解释与创新
苏丹治疗登革热的医生之间存在巨大的知识与实践差距,临床实践受指南熟悉程度、指南有用性和态度的影响比单纯的疾病知识影响更大。
科研与公卫意义
通过实践培训、实施世卫组织登革热指南和改善卫生系统的准备情况来加强医生的准备,可以加强受冲突影响地区的登革热管理。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其横断面设计和对自我报告数据的依赖而受到限制;由于样本量较大,但证据强度中等,但可能存在回忆偏差且缺乏客观的实践评估。

Knowledge–Practice Gap, Attitudes, and Determinants of Dengue Management Practices Among Physicians in Sudan: A Cross-Sectional Study, 2026

Authors: Mohannad Abdalfdeel Almahie Shaban, Thoraya Salah Murtada Sidahmed, Mohamed Yassin, Hamza Essam Mohamed Abbas, Marwa Imad Awad Ali, Omnia Abdelmonim Mohamed Babiker, Fatima Alamin Mohammed AbdAlrhman, Al-Romaysa M. Osman Khalafalla El-Haj, Altayeb Abayazeed Altayeb Yusuf, Osama Shaban
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Background Dengue fever is an emerging public health threat in Sudan, where recurrent outbreaks occur within a fragile and conflict-affected health system. Physicians play a central role in dengue diagnosis, management, and outbreak response. However, limited evidence exists regarding whether dengue-related knowledge translates into appropriate clinical practice. This study assessed dengue-related knowledge, attitudes, practices, and clinical readiness among physicians in Sudan and examined determinants of high dengue management practice. Methods An online cross-sectional survey was conducted among physicians practicing in Sudan between February and April 2026. A structured questionnaire adapted from a validated dengue knowledge, attitude, and practice (KAP) instrument was used to collect data on dengue disease knowledge, vector knowledge, attitudes, clinical practices, WHO guideline familiarity, resource availability, and healthcare-system readiness. Knowledge and practice scores were classified using Bloom’s cut-off criteria. Multivariable logistic regression was performed to identify factors independently associated with high dengue management practice. Results A total of 633 physicians participated. Only 3.8% demonstrated high disease knowledge, while 48.8% had low disease knowledge. High vector knowledge was observed in 28.8% of participants, whereas 40.0% achieved high practice scores. Important knowledge gaps were identified regarding dengue etiology, vector behavior, diagnostic testing, and breeding sites. Only 36.2% of physicians reported familiarity with WHO dengue guidelines, and 57.7% reported inadequate resources for dengue management. Correlation analysis demonstrated weak relationships between disease knowledge and practice (r = 0.077) and between vector knowledge and practice (r = 0.026), suggesting a knowledge–practice gap. In multivariable analysis, familiarity with WHO dengue guidelines (AOR = 2.66, 95% CI: 1.82–3.89), belief in the usefulness of WHO guidelines (AOR = 4.06, 95% CI: 1.94–8.51), and higher attitude scores (AOR = 1.07, 95% CI: 1.03–1.11) were independently associated with high practice, whereas disease knowledge was not. Conclusions A substantial knowledge–practice gap exists among physicians managing dengue in Sudan. Clinical practice was influenced more strongly by guideline familiarity, perceived guideline usefulness, and attitudes than by disease knowledge alone. Strengthening physician preparedness through practical training, implementation of WHO dengue guidelines, and improved health-system readiness may enhance dengue management in conflict-affected settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever is an emerging public health threat in Sudan, where recurrent outbreaks occur within a fragile and conflict-affected health system, and limited evidence exists regarding whether dengue-related knowledge translates into appropriate clinical practice.
Design and population
An online cross-sectional survey was conducted among physicians practicing in Sudan between February and April 2026.
Core methods
A structured questionnaire adapted from a validated dengue knowledge, attitude, and practice (KAP) instrument was used to collect data, and multivariable logistic regression was performed to identify factors independently associated with high dengue management practice.
Main results
A total of 633 physicians participated; only 3.8% demonstrated high disease knowledge, 28.8% had high vector knowledge, and 40.0% achieved high practice scores; only 36.2% reported familiarity with WHO dengue guidelines, and 57.7% reported inadequate resources.
Interpretation and novelty
A substantial knowledge–practice gap exists among physicians managing dengue in Sudan, and clinical practice was influenced more strongly by guideline familiarity, perceived guideline usefulness, and attitudes than by disease knowledge alone.
Scientific and public-health significance
Strengthening physician preparedness through practical training, implementation of WHO dengue guidelines, and improved health-system readiness may enhance dengue management in conflict-affected settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by its cross-sectional design and reliance on self-reported data; evidence strength is moderate due to the large sample size but potential for recall bias and lack of objective practice assessment.

贝宁南部阿波美-卡拉维公社埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊的 登革病毒 和 Kdr 突变 血清型(F1534C、S989P、V410L、V1016I 和 V1016G)

Serotypes of dengue virus and Kdr mutations (F1534C, S989P, V410L, V1016I, and V1016G) in Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus mosquitoes from the commune of Abomey-Calavi, Southern Benin
作者： Tatchémè Filémon Tokponnon, Oloubo Djibril Amoussa, Zoulkifilou Sare Dabou, Nicodème Kpemasse, Minassou Juvenal Ahouandjinou, Linda Towakinou, Brunelle Agassounon, Olivier Sègla Tandjekpon, Fabrice Comlan Gandaho, Mistourath Issa
摘要中文翻译摘要 登革热是一种由伊蚊属蚊子传播的病毒感染,每年造成数百万例病例。在贝宁,已报告了几起 暴发,特别是在阿波美-卡拉维,该地区于 2019 年记录了首例与登革热相关的死亡。在没有特定 治疗 的情况下,媒介 控制仍然是主要预防措施。然而,杀虫剂抗药性的出现,特别是通过 kdr 突变,可能会损害其有效性。本研究旨在鉴定在 Abomey-Calavi 公社流行的 登革病毒 血清型,并评估埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊中 kdr 突变 V410L 、 V1016G 、 V1016I 、 F1534C 和 S989P 的频率。2024 年 8 月,通过上午和下午的喷洒,从托格巴、卡拉维、阿卡萨托和戈多美四个区收集了蚊子。形态学鉴定后,进行分子分析,检测病毒血清型和kdr 突变。总共收集了 218 只伊蚊,其中埃及伊蚊 (68.8%) 占主导地位,而白纹伊蚊 (31.2%) 则占主导地位。总体登革热阳性率为 23.39%(95% CI:17.9–29.6)。最常见的血清型是 DENV-3 (12.4%),其次是 DENV-2 (6.9%)、DENV-1 (5%) 和 DENV-4 (0.09%)。六只蚊子同时感染两种血清型。Kdr 突变 在两个物种中均检测到,V1016G 的频率为 45%,S989P 的频率为 44%,F1534C 的频率为 40%,V410L 的频率为 22%,V1016I 的频率为 19%。四只蚊子同时携带三个 突变,而二十二只蚊子同时携带两个。这些结果强调了 登革病毒 的活跃循环以及阿波美-卡拉维伊蚊中 kdr 突变 的存在。然而,没有观察到登革热感染和 kdr 突变 之间存在显着关联,并且它们的分布与病毒血清型无关。这些发现强调需要定期监测 kdr 突变,以适应 媒介 控制策略并限制登革热的传播。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在鉴定在阿波美-卡拉维流行的 登革病毒 血清型,并评估 kdr 突变 在伊蚊中的频率,因为登革热是贝宁报告的 暴发 伊蚊传播的主要病毒感染。
设计与对象
2024 年 8 月,我们从阿波美-卡拉维的四个区收集了蚊子,共分析了 218 只伊蚊。
核心方法
对形态学鉴定的蚊子进行分子分析,以检测 登革病毒 血清型和 kdr 突变(V410L、V1016G、V1016I、F1534C 和 S989P)。
主要结果
登革热阳性率为23.39%,其中DENV-3(12.4%)是最常见的血清型,其次是DENV-2(6.9%)、DENV-1(5%)和DENV-4(0.09%)。V1016G 的 Kdr 突变 频率为 45%,S989P 为 44%,F1534C 为 40%,V410L 为 22%,V1016I 为 19%。
解释与创新
作者强调了伊蚊中 登革病毒 的活跃循环和 kdr 突变 的存在,并指出登革热感染与 kdr 突变 之间没有显着关联。
科研与公卫意义
需要对 kdr 突变 进行定期监测,以适应 媒介 控制策略并限制登革热传播,因为杀虫剂耐药性可能会损害 媒介 控制效果。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单一时间点和地点,但由于全面的分子分析以及血清型和 突变 频率的清晰报告,证据强度中等。

Serotypes of dengue virus and Kdr mutations (F1534C, S989P, V410L, V1016I, and V1016G) in Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus mosquitoes from the commune of Abomey-Calavi, Southern Benin

Authors: Tatchémè Filémon Tokponnon, Oloubo Djibril Amoussa, Zoulkifilou Sare Dabou, Nicodème Kpemasse, Minassou Juvenal Ahouandjinou, Linda Towakinou, Brunelle Agassounon, Olivier Sègla Tandjekpon, Fabrice Comlan Gandaho, Mistourath Issa
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Dengue is a viral infection transmitted by mosquitoes of the Aedes genus, responsible for millions of cases each year. In Benin, several outbreaks have been reported, particularly in Abomey-Calavi, where the first dengue-related death was recorded in 2019. In the absence of a specific treatment, vector control remains the primary preventive measure. However, the emergence of insecticide resistance, notably through kdr mutations, could compromise its effectiveness. This study aims to identify the dengue virus serotypes circulating in the commune of Abomey-Calavi and to assess the frequency of kdr mutations V410L , V1016G , V1016I , F1534C , and S989P in Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus mosquitoes. Mosquitoes were collected in August 2024 from four arrondissement Togba, Calavi, Akassato, and Godomey through morning and afternoon spraying. After morphological identification, molecular analyses were conducted to detect the viral serotypes and kdr mutations. A total of 218 Aedes mosquitoes were collected, with a predominance of Aedes aegypti (68.8%) compared to Aedes albopictus (31.2%). The overall dengue positivity rate was 23.39% (95% CI: 17.9–29.6). The most frequent serotype was DENV-3 (12.4%), followed by DENV-2 (6.9%), DENV-1 (5%), and DENV-4 (0.09%). Six mosquitoes were co-infected with two serotypes. Kdr mutations were detected in both species, with frequencies of 45% for V1016G , 44% for S989P , 40% for F1534C , 22% for V410L , and 19% for V1016I. Four mosquitoes carried three simultaneous mutations, while twenty-two carried two. These results highlight the active circulation of the dengue virus and the presence of kdr mutations in Aedes mosquitoes in Abomey-Calavi. However, no significant association was observed between dengue infection and kdr mutations, and their distribution was independent of the viral serotype. These findings emphasize the need for regular monitoring of kdr mutations to adapt vector control strategies and limit the spread of dengue.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to identify dengue virus serotypes circulating in Abomey-Calavi and assess the frequency of kdr mutations in Aedes mosquitoes, as dengue is a major viral infection transmitted by Aedes mosquitoes with outbreaks reported in Benin.
Design and population
Mosquitoes were collected in August 2024 from four arrondissements in Abomey-Calavi, with a total of 218 Aedes mosquitoes analyzed.
Core methods
Molecular analyses were conducted on morphologically identified mosquitoes to detect dengue virus serotypes and kdr mutations (V410L, V1016G, V1016I, F1534C, and S989P).
Main results
The dengue positivity rate was 23.39%, with DENV-3 (12.4%) being the most frequent serotype, followed by DENV-2 (6.9%), DENV-1 (5%), and DENV-4 (0.09%). Kdr mutation frequencies were 45% for V1016G, 44% for S989P, 40% for F1534C, 22% for V410L, and 19% for V1016I.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors highlighted the active circulation of dengue virus and presence of kdr mutations in Aedes mosquitoes, noting no significant association between dengue infection and kdr mutations.
Scientific and public-health significance
Regular monitoring of kdr mutations is needed to adapt vector control strategies and limit dengue spread, as insecticide resistance could compromise vector control effectiveness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to a single time point and location, but the evidence strength is moderate due to the comprehensive molecular analysis and clear reporting of serotype and mutation frequencies.

登革热 揭示 JAK2 突变 相关真性红细胞增多症,表现为广泛的内脏静脉血栓形成和出血并发症:诊断和治疗挑战

Dengue Fever Unmasking JAK2 Mutation-Related Polycythemia Vera Presenting With Extensive Splanchnic Venous Thrombosis and Hemorrhagic Complications: A Diagnostic and Therapeutic Challenge
作者： Rabbia Ayesha, Fizza Sikandar, Noor ul ain Sikandar, Ayshah Tabasum
摘要中文翻译登革热是一种常见的虫媒病毒感染,以发热、血小板减少和出血表现为特征,而血栓并发症相对罕见。我们报告了一例 重症登革热 感染病例,揭示了先前未诊断的 JAK2 相关真性红细胞增多症 (PV) 并发广泛内脏静脉血栓形成。一名58岁男性患者,有糖尿病和高血压病史,有5天发热、厌食和腹痛病史。初步实验室检查显示进行性血小板减少、血红蛋白升高和血细胞比容增加。住院期间,血小板计数持续下降,登革热血清学恢复阳性。腹部和骨盆的计算机断层扫描显示广泛的内脏静脉血栓形成,累及肠系膜上、脾和门静脉系统,并伴有急性胰腺炎。患者随后出现粘膜出血。进一步评估显示脾肿大和 JAK2 V617F 突变 阳性,证实潜在的真性红细胞增多症。由于并发出血和血栓形成,最初推迟抗凝治疗直至血液学稳定。随后患者接受抗凝和羟基脲治疗,临床康复。该病例强调了 登革热 中罕见的出血性和血栓性并发症同时存在,并强调了在出现非典型血栓形成、红细胞增多或脾肿大的患者中考虑潜在的骨髓增殖性肿瘤的重要性。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热是一种常见的虫媒病毒感染,以发热、血小板减少和出血表现为特征,而血栓并发症相对罕见。本研究报告了一例 重症登革热 感染病例,揭示了先前未诊断的 JAK2 相关真性红细胞增多症 (PV) 并发广泛内脏静脉血栓形成。
设计与对象
该研究报告了一名 58 岁男性患者,有糖尿病和高血压病史,有 5 天发烧、厌食和腹痛病史。
核心方法
初步实验室检查显示进行性血小板减少、血红蛋白升高和血细胞比容增加。腹部和骨盆的计算机断层扫描显示广泛的内脏静脉血栓形成,累及肠系膜上、脾和门静脉系统,并伴有急性胰腺炎。
主要结果
住院期间,血小板计数持续下降,登革热血清学恢复阳性。进一步评估显示脾肿大和 JAK2 V617F 突变 阳性,证实潜在的真性红细胞增多症。
解释与创新
该患者随后出现粘膜出血,并接受抗凝和羟基脲治疗,最终临床康复。该病例强调了 登革热 中罕见的出血性和血栓性并发症同时存在,并强调了在出现非典型血栓形成、红细胞增多或脾肿大的患者中考虑潜在的骨髓增殖性肿瘤的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调,需要对 登革热 患者的潜在骨髓增殖性疾病进行警惕监测和早期 诊断,以预防严重并发症。
局限与证据强度
该研究因其单一案例的性质而受到限制,可能无法推广到更广泛的人群。然而,详细的临床和实验室研究结果为了解登革热感染和骨髓增殖性疾病之间复杂的相互作用提供了宝贵的见解。

Dengue Fever Unmasking JAK2 Mutation-Related Polycythemia Vera Presenting With Extensive Splanchnic Venous Thrombosis and Hemorrhagic Complications: A Diagnostic and Therapeutic Challenge

Authors: Rabbia Ayesha, Fizza Sikandar, Noor ul ain Sikandar, Ayshah Tabasum
Original abstract (English)
Dengue fever is a common arboviral infection characterized by fever, thrombocytopenia, and hemorrhagic manifestations, whereas thrombotic complications are relatively uncommon. We report a case of severe dengue infection revealing previously undiagnosed JAK2-related polycythemia vera (PV) complicated by extensive splanchnic venous thrombosis. A 58-year-old male patient with a history of diabetes mellitus and hypertension presented with a five-day history of fever, anorexia, and abdominal pain. Initial laboratory investigations demonstrated progressive thrombocytopenia, elevated hemoglobin, and increased hematocrit. During hospitalization, platelet counts continued to decline, and dengue serology returned positive. Computed tomography of the abdomen and pelvis revealed extensive splanchnic venous thrombosis involving the superior mesenteric, splenic, and portal venous systems, along with acute pancreatitis. The patient subsequently developed mucosal bleeding. Further evaluation revealed splenomegaly and a positive JAK2 V617F mutation, confirming underlying polycythemia vera. Due to concurrent bleeding and thrombosis, anticoagulation was initially deferred until hematologic stabilization was achieved. The patient was subsequently treated with anticoagulation and hydroxyurea, resulting in clinical recovery. This case highlights the rare coexistence of hemorrhagic and thrombotic complications in dengue fever and emphasizes the importance of considering underlying myeloproliferative neoplasms in patients presenting with atypical thrombosis, erythrocytosis, or splenomegaly.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue fever is a common arboviral infection characterized by fever, thrombocytopenia, and hemorrhagic manifestations, whereas thrombotic complications are relatively uncommon. This study reports a case of severe dengue infection revealing previously undiagnosed JAK2-related polycythemia vera (PV) complicated by extensive splanchnic venous thrombosis.
Design and population
The study reports a case of a 58-year-old male patient with a history of diabetes mellitus and hypertension who presented with a five-day history of fever, anorexia, and abdominal pain.
Core methods
Initial laboratory investigations demonstrated progressive thrombocytopenia, elevated hemoglobin, and increased hematocrit. Computed tomography of the abdomen and pelvis revealed extensive splanchnic venous thrombosis involving the superior mesenteric, splenic, and portal venous systems, along with acute pancreatitis.
Main results
During hospitalization, platelet counts continued to decline, and dengue serology returned positive. Further evaluation revealed splenomegaly and a positive JAK2 V617F mutation, confirming underlying polycythemia vera.
Interpretation and novelty
The patient subsequently developed mucosal bleeding and was treated with anticoagulation and hydroxyurea, resulting in clinical recovery. This case highlights the rare coexistence of hemorrhagic and thrombotic complications in dengue fever and emphasizes the importance of considering underlying myeloproliferative neoplasms in patients presenting with atypical thrombosis, erythrocytosis, or splenomegaly.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings underscore the need for vigilant monitoring and early diagnosis of underlying myeloproliferative disorders in patients with dengue fever to prevent severe complications.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its single-case nature, which may not be generalizable to a broader population. However, the detailed clinical and laboratory findings provide valuable insights into the complex interplay between dengue infection and myeloproliferative disorders.

伊朗的 登革热 暴发:紧急警告和解决方案

Dengue fever outbreak in Iran: Urgent warnings and solutions
作者： Mohammad Javad Mousavi, Emad Behboudi
摘要中文翻译尊敬的编辑,继 COVID-19 大流行之后,登革热 作为最重要的蚊媒病毒性疾病之一,近年来已成为世界许多国家面临的严重公共卫生挑战。该病由埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊传播,流行于热带和亚热带地区。尽管伊朗位于干旱和半干旱气候地区,但也面临传播这种疾病的风险。最近霍尔木兹甘省,特别是班达尔伦格市的 登革热 当地病例报告给该国的卫生系统敲响了警钟。登革热 是对伊朗公共卫生的新威胁,需要采取紧急和协调的行动。控制这种疾病需要社会各界的参与,包括政府、卫生机构和公众。通过实施科学和可持续的战略并与政府机构协调,可以防止伊朗成为 登革热 的流行区。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了 登革热 带来的日益严重的公共卫生挑战,登革热 在 COVID-19 大流行后已成为许多国家的严重威胁。该研究旨在强调解决伊朗这一新威胁的紧迫性。
设计与对象
该研究是对 登革热 对伊朗公共卫生影响的一般观察分析,重点关注霍尔木兹甘省最近的 暴发,特别是在 Bandar Lengeh 市。它强调社会各部门采取协调行动的必要性。
核心方法
这些方法包括对伊朗 登革热 的流行病学背景和公共卫生反应进行总体审查,重点是社会参与和政府协调在疾病控制中的作用。
主要结果
该研究报告了霍尔木兹甘省最近发生的 登革热 当地病例,特别是在班达尔伦盖市,这是该疾病在伊朗潜在传播的重要指标。
解释与创新
作者将这些最近的病例解读为对该国卫生系统的警钟,强调需要采取紧急和协调行动,以防止 登革热 流行。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果强调了与政府机构协调实施科学和可持续战略的重要性,以降低 登革热 成为伊朗地方病的风险。
局限与证据强度
该研究基于一般观察和报告,可能缺乏详细的统计或严谨的方法。然而,证据足够充分,需要采取紧急公共卫生行动。

Dengue fever outbreak in Iran: Urgent warnings and solutions

Authors: Mohammad Javad Mousavi, Emad Behboudi
Original abstract (English)
Dear Editor, Following the COVID-19 pandemic, dengue fever, one of the most important mosquito-borne viral diseases, has become a serious public health challenge in many countries around the world in recent years. Transmitted by Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus mosquitoes, this disease is prevalent in tropical and subtropical regions. Iran is also at risk of spreading this disease despite being located in a region with a predominantly arid and semi-arid climate. Recent reports of local cases of dengue fever in Hormozgan province, especially in Bandar Lengeh city, are a wake-up call for the country’s health system. Dengue fever is an emerging threat to public health in Iran that requires urgent and coordinated action. Controlling this disease requires the participation of all sectors of society, including the government, health institutions, and the public. By implementing scientific and sustainable strategies and in coordination with government agencies, Iran can be prevented from becoming an endemic area for dengue fever.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the growing public health challenge posed by dengue fever, which has become a serious threat in many countries following the COVID-19 pandemic. The research aims to highlight the urgency of addressing this emerging threat in Iran.
Design and population
The study is a general observational analysis of dengue fever's public health implications in Iran, focusing on recent outbreaks in Hormozgan province, particularly in Bandar Lengeh city. It emphasizes the need for coordinated action across all sectors of society.
Core methods
The methods involve a general review of the epidemiological context and public health response to dengue fever in Iran, with an emphasis on the role of societal participation and government coordination in disease control.
Main results
The study reports recent local cases of dengue fever in Hormozgan province, especially in Bandar Lengeh city, as a significant indicator of the disease's potential spread in Iran.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret these recent cases as a wake-up call for the country’s health system, emphasizing the need for urgent and coordinated action to prevent dengue fever from becoming endemic.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings highlight the importance of implementing scientific and sustainable strategies in coordination with government agencies to mitigate the risk of dengue fever becoming an endemic disease in Iran.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is based on general observations and reports, which may lack detailed statistical or methodological rigor. However, the evidence is strong enough to warrant urgent public health action.

机器学习揭示温度是泰国各省登革热风险的关键预测因素:20 年分析。

Machine learning reveals temperature as a key predictor of dengue risk across Thailand's provinces: A 20-year analysis.
作者： Pikkanet Suttirat, Sudarat Chadsuthi, Supassorn Aekthong, Joacim Rocklöv, Dominique J. Bicout, Peter Haddawy, Myat Su Yin, Saranath Lawpoolsri, Charin Modchang
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 仍然是泰国的一项重大公共卫生挑战,传播 动态是由环境和社会经济因素之间复杂的相互作用驱动的。了解这些预测因素对于开发强大的预测系统至关重要。
设计与对象
我们分析了 77 个省份 20 年来每月的登革出血热 监测 数据(2003-2022 年),整合了 54 个环境、气候和社会经济特征。
核心方法
我们开发了一个机器学习框架来对时空登革热风险进行分类并确定整个泰国的关键预测因素,对四个候选分类器进行基准测试并根据六个指标的性能选择 XGBoost。SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) 用于解释特征贡献,数据集分为训练期 (2003-2016) 和测试期,后者又细分为 COVID-19 前、COVID-19 和 COVID-19 后阶段。
主要结果
XGBoost模型在疫情前测试中达到0.80的AUC值,在疫情期间和疫情后合并期间为0.74。温度在特征重要性排名中占主导地位,1个月滞后最低温度在21°C附近,3个月滞后最高温度在32°C附近存在非线性阈值,而降水特征贡献最小。模型在COVID-19疫情期间性能显著下降(2021年AUC为0.62),系统性的过度预测表明在此期间非环境因素主导了登革热的动态变化。
解释与创新
温度是泰国登革热风险的主要预测因素,我们恢复的阈值与 媒介 能力的已知生物限制密切相关。该研究表明,环境驱动的预测在稳定条件下是可靠的,但在重大社会混乱时期会大幅退化。
科研与公卫意义
环境驱动的预测在静止条件下是可靠的,但在重大社会混乱期间会大幅退化,这强调了在实时应用此类模型时需要将行为和 监测 覆盖指标与环境预测因素结合起来。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为模型性能在 COVID-19 大流行期间显着恶化,表明模型之外的非环境因素主导了这一时期的登革热动态。所提供的证据并未报告超出观察到的性能下降的具体设计限制或偏差。

Machine learning reveals temperature as a key predictor of dengue risk across Thailand's provinces: A 20-year analysis.

Authors: Pikkanet Suttirat, Sudarat Chadsuthi, Supassorn Aekthong, Joacim Rocklöv, Dominique J. Bicout, Peter Haddawy, Myat Su Yin, Saranath Lawpoolsri, Charin Modchang
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: Dengue fever remains a critical public health challenge in Thailand, with transmission dynamics driven by complex interactions between environmental and socioeconomic factors. Understanding these predictive factors is essential for developing robust forecasting systems. METHODS: We developed a machine learning framework to classify spatiotemporal dengue risk and identify key predictive factors across Thailand. We analyzed 20 years of monthly dengue hemorrhagic fever surveillance data (2003-2022) from 77 provinces, integrating 54 environmental, climatic, and socioeconomic features. We benchmarked four candidate classifiers - logistic regression, support vector machines, random forests, and eXtreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) - and selected XGBoost on the basis of performance across six metrics. SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) were used to interpret feature contributions. The dataset was stratified into training (2003-2016) and testing periods, with the latter subdivided into pre-COVID-19 (2017-2019), COVID-19 (2020-2021), and post-COVID-19 (2022) phases. RESULTS: The XGBoost model achieved an AUC of 0.80 in pre-pandemic testing and 0.74 across the combined during- and post-pandemic period. Temperature dominated the feature-importance ranking, comprising seven of the top ten features, with non-linear thresholds near 21°C for 1-month lagged minimum temperature and near 32°C for 3-month lagged maximum temperature - values that align with established biological constraints on Aedes aegypti-mediated transmission. Precipitation features contributed minimally to model predictions, while a higher Gross Provincial Product was associated with increased dengue risk, consistent with predominantly urban transmission patterns. Model performance deteriorated significantly during the COVID-19 pandemic (AUC = 0.62 in 2021), with systematic overprediction indicating that non-environmental factors operating outside the model dominated dengue dynamics during this period. CONCLUSIONS: Temperature is the dominant predictor of dengue risk in Thailand, and the thresholds we recover correspond closely to known biological constraints on vector competence. Environmentally driven prediction is reliable under stationary conditions but degrades substantially during periods of major societal disruption, underscoring the need to integrate behavioral and surveillance-coverage indicators alongside environmental predictors when applying such models in real time.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background

The supplied evidence does not report specific design limitations or biases beyond the observed performance degradation.

泛正黄病毒 RT-qPCR 检测的无比对 k 聚体引导设计

Alignment-free k-mer-guided design of a pan-Orthoflavivirus RT-qPCR assay
作者： Khanate Sayasit, Chutikarn Chaimayo, Warinya Nuwong, Tharathip Boondouylan, Nattaya Tanlieng, Kamol Suwannakarn, Intawat Nookaew, Navin Horthongkham
摘要中文翻译摘要 正黄病毒属的共同传播和快速扩张,包括 登革病毒 (DENV)、寨卡病毒 (ZIKV)、西尼罗河病毒 (WNV)、黄热病病毒 (YFV) 和日本脑炎病毒 (JEV),构成了重大的全球健康挑战。高核苷酸分歧(>25-30%)和传统多序列比对在检测大型数据集中的保守基序时的计算限制阻碍了包容性泛属分子诊断的发展。为了克服这些限制,我们开发了一种系统的免对齐设计流程,该流程使用严格的 k 聚体分析和压缩的 De Bruijn 图。我们分析了 11,846 个 RefSeq 病毒 基因组,以识别正黄病毒属内系统发育保守、功能相关的特征作为案例研究。该管道在非结构蛋白 5 基因内鉴定出一个保守的 600 bp 区域,有助于广谱 TaqMan RT-qPCR 检测的设计。分析验证表明,DENV1-4、ZIKV、JEV 和 YFV 的检测限 (LOD) 为 1-10 个拷贝/μL,且与非目标病原体没有交叉反应;在初步实验中,WNV 的检测浓度始终为 1,000 拷贝/μL。在对存档样本的临床评估中,该检测的总体准确率达到 97.33%。它对 DENV 血清型表现出 100% 的敏感性和特异性,与标准商业试剂盒相比,产生显着更早的循环阈值 (Ct) 值,而 ZIKV 检测显示出 100% 的特异性和 71.43% 的敏感性。这项研究验证了一种免比对、k-mer 引导的策略,用于在高度可变的病毒属中发现保守的诊断靶点,然后进行局部 MSA 辅助的引物探针设计。由此产生的检测为广泛的前线 监测 提供了强大的工具,并且计算框架为未来的大流行防范提供了可扩展的解决方案。重要性 登革热、寨卡病毒和西尼罗河等蚊媒病毒对全球健康构成巨大威胁。然而,由于这些病毒高度多样化且突变迅速,创建单一诊断测试来检测所有病毒一直是一个挑战。传统软件很难在这些不同的病毒中找到共享的基因目标。为了解决这个问题,我们开发了一种新的计算方法。通过分析 11,000 多个病毒 基因组,我们成功识别了一组蚊媒病毒的共同基因特征。我们利用这一发现创建了一种单一的、高度准确的测试,能够同时检测多种危险的蚊媒病毒。这一突破为一线临床 诊断 和全球 暴发 监测 提供了一个关键的、即用型工具。更重要的是,我们的策略可以快速调整,为其他快速变异的病毒设计广谱测试,从而显着增强我们准备和应对未来流行病的能力。为了解决这个问题,我们开发了一种新的计算方法。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在解决开发针对 登革病毒 和其他相关黄病毒的广谱诊断测试的挑战,因为它们具有高遗传多样性和快速的 突变 率。正黄病毒(包括 登革病毒、寨卡病毒、西尼罗河病毒、黄热病病毒和日本脑炎病毒)的共同循环和扩张给全球健康带来了重大挑战。
设计与对象
该研究涉及使用 k-mer 分析和 De Bruijn 图来识别正黄病毒属内保守遗传特征的系统性免比对设计流程。它包括分析验证阶段和对存档样本的临床评估。
核心方法
该流程利用 11,846 个 RefSeq 病毒 基因组 来识别系统发育保守、功能相关的特征。它还涉及基于这些特征的 TaqMan RT-qPCR 测定的设计以及评估检测限和交叉反应性的分析验证。
主要结果
分析验证显示 DENV1-4、ZIKV、JEV 和 YFV 的检测限 (LOD) 为 1-10 拷贝/μL,与非目标病原体没有交叉反应。在初步实验中,WNV 的检测浓度始终为 1,000 拷贝/μL。该测定在存档样本的临床评估中总体准确率达到 97.33%。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为证明了无比对、k-mer 引导策略在高度变异的病毒属中识别保守诊断靶点的有效性。这种方法允许设计广谱诊断工具,而不依赖于传统的多序列比对。
科研与公卫意义
该研究结果为诊断和登革病毒及相关黄病毒的全球暴发 监测和相关黄病毒的一线临床提供了强大的工具。该策略可以适用于其他快速突变的病毒,从而增强大流行的防范能力。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于抽象证据,可能缺乏详细的实验验证。证据强度中等,因为结果基于对存档样本的分析验证和临床评估,没有实时数据。

Alignment-free k-mer-guided design of a pan-Orthoflavivirus RT-qPCR assay

Authors: Khanate Sayasit, Chutikarn Chaimayo, Warinya Nuwong, Tharathip Boondouylan, Nattaya Tanlieng, Kamol Suwannakarn, Intawat Nookaew, Navin Horthongkham
Original abstract (English)
ABSTRACT The co-circulation and rapid expansion of the genus Orthoflavivirus, including dengue virus (DENV), Zika virus (ZIKV), West Nile virus (WNV), Yellow fever virus (YFV), and Japanese encephalitis virus (JEV), pose significant global health challenges. Developing inclusive pan-genus molecular diagnostics is hindered by high nucleotide divergence (>25–30%) and the computational limitations of traditional multiple sequence alignment in detecting conserved motifs across large data sets. To overcome these limitations, we developed a systematic alignment-free design pipeline that uses rigorous k-mer analysis and compacted De Bruijn graphs. We analyzed 11,846 RefSeq viral genomes to identify phylogenetically conserved, functionally relevant signatures within the Orthoflavivirus genus as a case study. The pipeline identified a conserved 600 bp region within the non-structural protein 5 gene, facilitating the design of a broad-spectrum TaqMan RT-qPCR assay. Analytical validation demonstrated limits of detection (LODs) of 1–10 copies/μL for DENV1–4, ZIKV, JEV, and YFV, with no cross-reactivity against non-target pathogens; WNV was consistently detected at 1,000 copies/μL in pilot experiments. In a clinical evaluation of archived samples, the assay achieved 97.33% overall accuracy. It demonstrated 100% sensitivity and specificity for DENV serotypes, yielding significantly earlier cycle threshold (Ct) values compared to a standard commercial kit, while ZIKV detection showed 100% specificity with 71.43% sensitivity. This study validates an alignment-free, k-mer-guided strategy for uncovering conserved diagnostic targets in highly variable viral genera, followed by local MSA-assisted primer-probe design. The resulting assay offers a robust tool for broad frontline surveillance, and the computational framework provides a scalable solution for future pandemic preparedness. IMPORTANCE Mosquito-borne viruses like dengue, Zika, and West Nile pose a massive threat to global health. However, because these viruses are highly diverse and mutate rapidly, creating a single diagnostic test to detect all of them has been a challenge. Traditional software struggles to find shared genetic targets across such different viruses. To solve this, we developed a new computational approach. By analyzing over 11,000 viral genomes, we successfully identified a shared genetic signature of a group of mosquito-borne viruses. We used this discovery to create a single, highly accurate test capable of detecting multiple dangerous mosquito-borne viruses at once. This breakthrough provides a crucial, ready-to-use tool for frontline clinical diagnosis and global outbreak surveillance. More importantly, our strategy can be quickly adapted to design broad-spectrum tests for other rapidly mutating viruses, significantly strengthening our ability to prepare for and respond to future pandemics. To solve this, we developed a new computational approach.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to address the challenges of developing a broad-spectrum diagnostic test for dengue virus and other related flaviviruses due to their high genetic diversity and rapid mutation rates. The co-circulation and expansion of Orthoflavivirus, including dengue virus, Zika virus, West Nile virus, Yellow fever virus, and Japanese encephalitis virus, present significant global health challenges.
Design and population
The study involved a systematic alignment-free design pipeline using k-mer analysis and De Bruijn graphs to identify conserved genetic signatures within the Orthoflavivirus genus. It included an analytical validation phase and a clinical evaluation of archived samples.
Core methods
The pipeline utilized 11,846 RefSeq viral genomes to identify phylogenetically conserved, functionally relevant signatures. It also involved the design of a TaqMan RT-qPCR assay based on these signatures and an analytical validation to assess detection limits and cross-reactivity.
Main results
Analytical validation showed limits of detection (LODs) of 1–10 copies/μL for DENV1–4, ZIKV, JEV, and YFV, with no cross-reactivity against non-target pathogens. WNV was consistently detected at 1,000 copies/μL in pilot experiments. The assay achieved 97.33% overall accuracy in a clinical evaluation of archived samples.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating the effectiveness of an alignment-free, k-mer-guided strategy for identifying conserved diagnostic targets in highly variable viral genera. This approach allows for the design of broad-spectrum diagnostic tools without relying on traditional multiple sequence alignment.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings provide a robust tool for frontline clinical diagnosis and global outbreak surveillance of dengue virus and related flaviviruses. The strategy can be adapted for other rapidly mutating viruses, enhancing pandemic preparedness.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to abstract-only evidence, which may lack detailed experimental validation. The evidence strength is moderate, as the results are based on analytical validation and clinical evaluation of archived samples without real-time data.

登革热特异性 T 细胞免疫受不同水平的预先存在的免疫的影响 2267557

Dengue-specific T cell immunity influenced by various levels of pre-existing immunity 2267557
作者： Daniela Weiskopf, Amparo Martinez Perez, Rosemary Aogo, Ysabelle Broderson, Kelsey Lowman, Camila Odio, Leah Katzelnick
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 登革病毒 (DENV) 是一种蚊媒黄病毒,导致全球高发病率和 死亡率。先前接触不同的 DENV 血清型可能会影响随后的 免疫应答 并影响保护和风险。这对开发普遍有效且安全的登革热 疫苗 提出了挑战,该 疫苗 能够诱导针对所有四种血清型的平衡且持久的免疫力。T 细胞构成登革热免疫的重要组成部分,独立于抗体发挥作用。在这项工作中,我们评估了减毒单价 DENV3 疫苗 在预先存在不同水平 DENV 暴露的参与者中引发的登革热特异性 T 细胞反应。方法 使用实验定义的 DENV3 表位大库刺激来自不同登革热血清状态(血清阴性、单型和多型暴露)的 疫苗 受体的外周血单核细胞 (PBMC),以获取 DENV 结构和非结构表位。我们在高参数流式细胞术中使用一组 30 多个标记物来分析 T 细胞激活标记物、记忆表型和细胞因子分泌。结果在 疫苗接种 之前以及 疫苗接种 后 15、28 和 90 天纵向分析 T 细胞反应。DENV-3 挑战诱导了结构和非结构成分的可测量的 DENV 特异性 T 细胞反应。我们观察到 T 细胞反应的个体差异受到其基线血清状态和病毒血症负担的影响。结论 我们的研究为塑造针对新型 DENV 挑战的细胞免疫的因素提供了宝贵的见解 资金来源 2P01AI106695-11 主题类别 病毒免疫学 (VIR)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒 (DENV) 是一种蚊媒黄病毒,导致全球重大发病率和 死亡率。先前接触不同的 DENV 血清型可能会影响随后的 免疫应答 并影响保护和风险。
设计与对象
这项研究评估了减毒单价 DENV3 疫苗 在预先存在不同程度 DENV 暴露的参与者中引发的登革热特异性 T 细胞反应。该研究是在对照环境中进行的,疫苗 接受者按血清状态(血清阴性、单型和多型暴露)进行分类。
核心方法
使用实验定义的 DENV3 表位大库刺激来自不同登革热血清状态的 疫苗 受体的外周血单核细胞 (PBMC),以获取 DENV 结构和非结构表位。在高参数流式细胞术中使用一组 30 多个标记物对 T 细胞激活标记物、记忆表型和细胞因子分泌进行分析。
主要结果
在 疫苗接种 之前以及 疫苗接种 后 15、28 和 90 天纵向分析 T 细胞反应。我们观察到 T 细胞反应的个体差异受到其基线血清状态和病毒血症负担的影响。
解释与创新
我们的研究为塑造细胞免疫以应对新的 DENV 挑战的因素提供了宝贵的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现可以为开发更有效、更安全的登革热 疫苗 提供信息,该 疫苗 能够诱导针对所有四种血清型的平衡且持久的免疫力。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于评估特定 疫苗 背景下的 T 细胞反应,并未涉及更广泛的影响。由于关注单一 疫苗 和有限的群体多样性,证据强度适中。

Dengue-specific T cell immunity influenced by various levels of pre-existing immunity 2267557

Authors: Daniela Weiskopf, Amparo Martinez Perez, Rosemary Aogo, Ysabelle Broderson, Kelsey Lowman, Camila Odio, Leah Katzelnick
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Dengue virus (DENV) is a mosquito-borne flavivirus responsible for significant global morbidity and mortality. Prior exposure to different DENV serotypes can shape subsequent immune responses and influence both protection and risk. This challenges the development of a universally effective and safe dengue vaccine, capable of inducing balanced and durable immunity against all four serotypes. T cells constitute a critical arm of dengue immunity, acting independently of antibodies. In this work, we have evaluated the dengue-specific T-cell responses elicited by an attenuated monovalent DENV3 vaccine in participants with various levels of pre-existing DENV exposure. Methods Peripheral blood mononuclear cells (PBMCs) from vaccine recipients of different dengue serostatus (seronegative, monotypic, and multitypic-exposure) were stimulated with experimentally defined DENV3 epitope megapools for DENV structural and nonstructural epitopes. We profiled T cell activation markers, memory phenotypes, and cytokine secretion using a panel of 30+ markers in high-parameter flow cytometry. Results T cells responses were analyzed longitudinally pre-vaccination, and 15, 28 and 90 days after vaccination. The DENV-3 challenge induced measurable DENV-specific T-cell responses for both structural and non-structural components. We observed individual variability in T cell responses that are influenced by their baseline serostatus and viremia burden. Conclusion Our study provides valuable insights into the factors shaping cellular immunity to a novel DENV challenge Funding Source 2P01AI106695-11 Topic Categories Viral Immunology (VIR)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus (DENV) is a mosquito-borne flavivirus responsible for significant global morbidity and mortality. Prior exposure to different DENV serotypes can shape subsequent immune responses and influence both protection and risk.
Design and population
This study evaluated dengue-specific T-cell responses elicited by an attenuated monovalent DENV3 vaccine in participants with various levels of pre-existing DENV exposure. The study was conducted in a controlled setting with vaccine recipients categorized by their serostatus (seronegative, monotypic, and multitypic-exposure).
Core methods
Peripheral blood mononuclear cells (PBMCs) from vaccine recipients of different dengue serostatus were stimulated with experimentally defined DENV3 epitope megapools for DENV structural and nonstructural epitopes. T cell activation markers, memory phenotypes, and cytokine secretion were profiled using a panel of 30+ markers in high-parameter flow cytometry.
Main results
T cells responses were analyzed longitudinally pre-vaccination, and 15, 28 and 90 days after vaccination. We observed individual variability in T cell responses that are influenced by their baseline serostatus and viremia burden.
Interpretation and novelty
Our study provides valuable insights into the factors shaping cellular immunity to a novel DENV challenge.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the development of more effective and safer dengue vaccines capable of inducing balanced and durable immunity against all four serotypes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to evaluating T cell responses in a specific vaccine context and does not address broader implications. The evidence is moderate in strength due to the focus on a single vaccine and limited population diversity.

中性粒细胞激活与人类受体中登革热 疫苗 复制减少相关 2307750

Neutrophil Activation is Associated with Decreased Dengue Vaccine Replication in Human Recipients 2307750
作者： Ysabelle Broderson, Heather Teague, Keerthi Konda, Lauren Knabe, Raquel Santana Da Cruz, Melissa Law, Leah Katzelnick, Rosemary Aogo, Jeffrey Strich, Camila Odio
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 登革病毒 (DENV) 是一种蚊媒黄病毒,影响全球 50% 以上的人口。需要更好地理解 DENV 特定的 免疫应答 才能改进 疫苗。抗体依赖性中性粒细胞吞噬作用与预防出血热有关,但过度的中性粒细胞激活会导致 重症登革热,并且保护性中性粒细胞的表型仍不清楚。为了表征中性粒细胞对 DENV 的反应,我们评估了登革热 疫苗 受体和体外的中性粒细胞表型。方法 10 名具有不同 DENV 暴露史的参与者接种了单价 DENV3 疫苗 疫苗。在基线(第 0 天)以及 疫苗接种 后第 1 天和第 9 天通过流式细胞术测量细胞中性粒细胞标记。用不同的 DENV 血清型在体外刺激来自健康供体的中性粒细胞,并通过流式细胞术评估其表型。结果 DENV3 疫苗接种 后,所有受体的中性粒细胞总数均显示出 SYK 升高和 CD33 表达降低,表明激活和成熟。与高 疫苗 病毒血症参与者 (N = 7) 相比,低病毒血症参与者 (N = 3) 在第 1 天的 CD16 和 CD15 表达增加,CD62L 降低。此外,低病毒血症参与者在第 9 天 SYK 阳性中性粒细胞中 CD63 增加。在体外,暴露于 DENV4 的中性粒细胞在刺激后存活长达 96 小时,没有病毒感染的证据不同感染复数(MOI 1-17)。结论 低病毒血症参与者的中性粒细胞活化、成熟和脱颗粒增加,支持其在登革热中的保护作用。健康供体中性粒细胞在体外对 登革病毒 做出反应,将用于表征具有高或低 疫苗 复制的参与者的血清如何改变中性粒细胞活性。总体而言,更好地了解中性粒细胞对 DENV 的反应可以为治疗选择提供信息。资金来源 美国国立卫生研究院校内研究计划、NIH 实验室到床边计划基金编号 994875、NIH 主任挑战创新奖、ReVAMPP 赠款 1 U19 AI181960-01 和 P01 AI106695。该项目还获得了 f 主题类别病毒免疫学 (VIR) 的部分资助
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒 (DENV) 影响全球 50% 以上的人口,需要更好地了解 DENV 特定的 免疫应答 才能改进 疫苗。抗体依赖性中性粒细胞吞噬作用与预防出血热有关,但过度的中性粒细胞激活会导致 重症登革热,并且保护性中性粒细胞的表型仍不清楚。
设计与对象
该研究评估了登革热 疫苗 受体和体外的中性粒细胞表型。10 名具有不同 DENV 暴露史的参与者接种了单价 DENV3 疫苗 疫苗,并在基线(第 0 天)以及 疫苗接种 后第 1 天和第 9 天通过流式细胞术测量细胞中性粒细胞标记物。
核心方法
用不同的 DENV 血清型在体外刺激来自健康供体的中性粒细胞,并通过流式细胞术评估其表型。
主要结果
DENV3 疫苗接种 之后,中性粒细胞总数显示 SYK 升高,CD33 表达降低。与高病毒血症参与者 (N = 7) 相比,低病毒血症参与者 (N = 3) 在第 1 天的 CD16 和 CD15 表达增加,CD62L 表达较低。低病毒血症参与者在第 9 天的 SYK 阳性中性粒细胞中 CD63 也有所增加。
解释与创新
低病毒血症参与者的中性粒细胞显示出更高的活化、成熟和脱粒能力,支持其在登革热中的保护作用。研究结果表明,中性粒细胞对 DENV 的反应可以为治疗选择提供信息。
科研与公卫意义
更好地了解中性粒细胞对 DENV 的反应可以为治疗选择提供信息,并通过识别保护性中性粒细胞表型来改进 疫苗 设计。
局限与证据强度
该研究以小样本量(N = 10)进行,重点关注单价 DENV3 疫苗,这可能会限制普遍性。由于样本量小和体外限制,证据强度中等。

Neutrophil Activation is Associated with Decreased Dengue Vaccine Replication in Human Recipients 2307750

Authors: Ysabelle Broderson, Heather Teague, Keerthi Konda, Lauren Knabe, Raquel Santana Da Cruz, Melissa Law, Leah Katzelnick, Rosemary Aogo, Jeffrey Strich, Camila Odio
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Dengue virus (DENV) is a mosquito-borne flavivirus that impacts over 50% of the global population. A better understanding of DENV-specific immune responses is needed to improve vaccines. Antibody-dependent neutrophil phagocytosis has been associated with protection against hemorrhagic fever, yet excessive neutrophil activation contributes to severe dengue, and the phenotypes of protective neutrophils remain undefined. To characterize how neutrophils respond to DENV, we evaluated neutrophil phenotypes in dengue vaccine recipients and in vitro. Methods 10 participants, with distinct DENV exposure histories, were vaccinated with a monovalent DENV3 vaccine. Cellular neutrophil markers were measured by flow cytometry at baseline (day 0) and on days 1 and 9 post-vaccination. Neutrophils from healthy donors were stimulated in vitro with different DENV serotypes, and their phenotypes were evaluated by flow cytometry. Results Following DENV3 vaccination, the total neutrophil population from all recipients showed elevated SYK and decreased CD33 expression indicating activation and maturation. When compared to participants with high vaccine viremia (N = 7), those with low viremia (N = 3) had increased expressions of CD16 and CD15 and lower CD62L on day 1. Furthermore, low viremic participants had increased CD63 in SYK positive neutrophils on day 9. In-vitro, neutrophils exposed to DENV4 survived up to 96 hours post-stimulation with no evidence of viral infection at various multiplicities of infection (MOI 1-17). Conclusion Neutrophils from participants with low viremia show increased activation, maturation and degranulation supporting their protective role in dengue. Healthy donor neutrophils responding to dengue virus in vitro will be used to characterize how sera from participants with high or low vaccine replication alters neutrophil activity. Overall, a better understanding of neutrophil responses to DENV can inform therapeutic options. Funding Source Intramural Research Program of the National Institutes of Health, NIH Bench-to-Bedside Program Funds No. 994875, NIH Director’s Challenge Innovation Award, ReVAMPP grant 1 U19 AI181960-01, and P01 AI106695. This project has also been funded in part with f Topic Categories Viral Immunology (VIR)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus (DENV) impacts over 50% of the global population, and a better understanding of DENV-specific immune responses is needed to improve vaccines. Antibody-dependent neutrophil phagocytosis has been associated with protection against hemorrhagic fever, yet excessive neutrophil activation contributes to severe dengue, and the phenotypes of protective neutrophils remain undefined.
Design and population
The study evaluated neutrophil phenotypes in dengue vaccine recipients and in vitro. Ten participants with distinct DENV exposure histories were vaccinated with a monovalent DENV3 vaccine, and cellular neutrophil markers were measured by flow cytometry at baseline (day 0) and on days 1 and 9 post-vaccination.
Core methods
Neutrophils from healthy donors were stimulated in vitro with different DENV serotypes, and their phenotypes were evaluated by flow cytometry.
Main results
Following DENV3 vaccination, the total neutrophil population showed elevated SYK and decreased CD33 expression. Participants with low viremia (N = 3) had increased expressions of CD16 and CD15 and lower CD62L on day 1 compared to those with high viremia (N = 7). Low viremic participants also had increased CD63 in SYK positive neutrophils on day 9.
Interpretation and novelty
Neutrophils from participants with low viremia show increased activation, maturation and degranulation supporting their protective role in dengue. The findings suggest that neutrophil responses to DENV can inform therapeutic options.
Scientific and public-health significance
A better understanding of neutrophil responses to DENV can inform therapeutic options and improve vaccine design by identifying protective neutrophil phenotypes.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in a small sample size (N = 10) and focused on a monovalent DENV3 vaccine, which may limit generalizability. The evidence strength is moderate due to the small sample size and in vitro limitations.

快速启动标准体积血浆置换治疗登革热感染引起的急性肝功能衰竭。

Rapid initiation of standard-volume plasma exchange for acute liver failure from dengue infection.
作者： Elliot Yeung Chong, Samuel Jun Ming Lim, Chanda Kendra Ho, Rajneesh Kumar
摘要中文翻译登革热 (DF) 的 发病率 正在迅速上升,急性肝衰竭 (ALF) 是一种罕见但危及生命的相关并发症。我们介绍了一名成年患者,在 DF 相关 ALF 发生 诊断 后立即接受了标准容量血浆置换 (PLEX)。在 ALF 诊断 的同一天开始 1.5 倍血浆量的 PLEX,置换液包含 2.0 L 新鲜冰冻血浆和 1.0 L 5% 白蛋白。第二天,患者的肝性脑病得到缓解,生化指标显着改善,无需进行额外的 PLEX 周期,并且顺利出院。该 病例报告 描述了在肝性脑病发病后数小时内快速启动 PLEX,仅使用标准容量 PLEX 的单周期,而不受其他治疗混杂因素的影响。该案例有助于不断发展有关 DF-ALF 中 PLEX 最佳时间和体积的文献。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究通过探索血浆置换(PLEX)作为 登革热(DF)(一种快速上升的 发病率 疾病)的潜力,解决了罕见但危及生命的急性肝衰竭(ALF)并发症。
设计与对象
该 病例报告 描述了一位患有 DF 相关 ALF 的成年患者,他在 诊断 后立即接受了标准容量血浆置换。
核心方法
在 ALF 诊断 的同一天开始使用 1.5 倍血浆量的 PLEX,使用包含 2.0 L 新鲜冰冻血浆和 1.0 L 5% 白蛋白的置换液进行单周期。
主要结果
第二天,患者的肝性脑病得到缓解,并且他的生化特征显着改善,无需额外的 PLEX 周期,出院时状况良好。
解释与创新
作者将症状的快速缓解和生化改善解释为支持 DF-ALF 及时启动 PLEX 的证据,为有关这种情况的 PLEX 最佳时机和剂量的文献做出了贡献。
科研与公卫意义
该病例表明,即时标准容量 PLEX 可能是 DF 相关 ALF 的有效干预措施,可能为临床实践和 治疗 方案的未来研究提供信息。
局限与证据强度
证据强度受到病例报告设计的限制,该设计缺乏对照组,可能无法推广;然而,没有混杂的治疗干预措施加强了观察到的结果。

Rapid initiation of standard-volume plasma exchange for acute liver failure from dengue infection.

Authors: Elliot Yeung Chong, Samuel Jun Ming Lim, Chanda Kendra Ho, Rajneesh Kumar
Original abstract (English)
The incidence of dengue fever (DF) is rapidly rising and acute liver failure (ALF) is a rare but life-threatening associated complication. We present an adult patient who underwent standard-volume plasma exchange (PLEX) promptly after diagnosis of DF-associated ALF. PLEX of 1.5 times plasma volume was started on the same day of ALF diagnosis with the replacement fluid comprising 2.0 L of fresh frozen plasma and 1.0 L of 5% albumin. The patient's hepatic encephalopathy resolved the following day, and his biochemical profile significantly improved without additional cycles of PLEX and he was discharged well. This case report describes the rapid initiation of PLEX within hours of hepatic encephalopathy onset, using a single cycle of standard-volume PLEX alone without the influence of other therapeutic confounders. This case contributes to the evolving literature on the optimal timing and volume of PLEX in DF-ALF.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addresses the rare but life-threatening complication of acute liver failure (ALF) in dengue fever (DF), a rapidly rising incidence disease, by exploring the potential of plasma exchange (PLEX) as a treatment.
Design and population
This case report describes an adult patient with DF-associated ALF who underwent standard-volume plasma exchange promptly after diagnosis.
Core methods
PLEX of 1.5 times plasma volume was initiated on the same day of ALF diagnosis, using a single cycle with replacement fluid comprising 2.0 L of fresh frozen plasma and 1.0 L of 5% albumin.
Main results
The patient's hepatic encephalopathy resolved the following day, and his biochemical profile significantly improved without additional cycles of PLEX, leading to discharge in good condition.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the rapid resolution of symptoms and biochemical improvement as evidence supporting the timely initiation of PLEX in DF-ALF, contributing to the literature on optimal timing and volume of PLEX for this condition.
Scientific and public-health significance
This case suggests that prompt standard-volume PLEX may be an effective intervention for DF-associated ALF, potentially informing clinical practice and future research on treatment protocols.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is limited by the case report design, which lacks a control group and may not be generalizable; however, the absence of confounding therapeutic interventions strengthens the observed outcome.

开发基于 NS1 的登革热 疫苗,通过非中和抗体提供保护并规避 抗体依赖性增强 2249046

Development of an NS1-based dengue vaccine that protects via non-neutralizing antibodies and evades antibody-dependent enhancement 2249046
作者： Jordan Polster, Fahima Akther, David Martinez, E. De Buck, Norbert Pardi, David Martínez
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 登革热是一种由蚊子传播的病毒,威胁着全球一半的人口。虽然原发感染通常是轻微或无症状的,但由于一种称为 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE) 的现象,四种血清型中另一种的继发感染会增加患严重疾病的风险。通过 ADE,针对登革热结构蛋白的非中和抗体会促进病毒进入和复制,并使疾病严重程度和结果恶化。目前的登革热 疫苗 是减毒活疫苗,并不能消除突破性感染后发生 ADE 的可能性。因此需要不诱导 ADE 的新型 疫苗。NS1 是一种分泌型非结构蛋白,通过介导血管渗漏而导致登革热 发病机制。NS1 是一个有前途的 疫苗 靶点,因为抗 NS1 抗体具有非中和性,从而可以逃避 ADE,而且它们也被证明可以保护小鼠免受疾病侵害。方法在这里,我们正在开发一种安全、高免疫原性且对小鼠有效的 DENV2 NS1 mRNA-LNP 疫苗。结果该 DENV2 NS1 mRNA-LNP 疫苗 诱导非中和抗体,可在致命攻击模型中预防严重疾病和 死亡率。结论 在未来的方向上,这项工作将研究非中和性抗 NS1 保护的机制,包括亲和力成熟和 Fc 效应子功能的参与。我们共同提出了一种有前途的登革热 疫苗 策略,该策略可以逃避 ADE 并预防致命疾病。资金来源 Jordan Polster 得到了 HHMI Gilliam 研究员计划的支持。大卫·马丁内斯得到了 HHMI 汉娜·格雷研究员计划的支持。主题类别 疫苗 和免疫疗法 (VAC)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热是一种由蚊子传播的病毒,威胁着全球一半人口。虽然原发感染通常是轻微或无症状的,但由于一种称为 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE) 的现象,四种血清型中另一种的继发感染会增加患严重疾病的风险。
设计与对象
本研究开发了一种 DENV2 NS1 mRNA-LNP 疫苗,它在小鼠中安全、具有高免疫原性且有效。NS1 是一种分泌型非结构蛋白,通过介导血管渗漏而导致登革热 发病机制。
核心方法
该研究涉及开发 DENV2 NS1 mRNA-LNP 疫苗 并测试其在小鼠中的免疫原性和功效。NS1 是一个有前途的 疫苗 靶点,因为抗 NS1 抗体具有非中和性,从而可以逃避 ADE,而且它们也被证明可以保护小鼠免受疾病侵害。
主要结果
这种 DENV2 NS1 mRNA-LNP 疫苗 诱导非中和抗体,可在致命挑战模型中预防严重疾病和 死亡率。
解释与创新
因此需要不诱导 ADE 的新型 疫苗。我们共同提出了一种有前途的登革热 疫苗 策略,该策略可以逃避 ADE 并预防致命疾病。
科研与公卫意义
该 疫苗 策略可以通过提供一种逃避 ADE 和预防严重疾病的方法,为未来登革热 疫苗 的发展提供信息。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告局限性或证据强度。该研究基于小鼠模型的结果,不包括人体试验或长期随访。

Development of an NS1-based dengue vaccine that protects via non-neutralizing antibodies and evades antibody-dependent enhancement 2249046

Authors: Jordan Polster, Fahima Akther, David Martinez, E. De Buck, Norbert Pardi, David Martínez
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Dengue is a mosquito-borne virus that threatens one half of the global population. While primary infection is usually mild or asymptomatic, secondary infection with another of the four serotypes increases the risk for severe disease due to a phenomenon called antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE). Through ADE, non-neutralizing antibodies that target dengue structural proteins facilitate viral entry and replication and worsen disease severity and outcomes. Current dengue vaccines are live-attenuated and do not eliminate the possibility of ADE upon breakthrough infection. Novel vaccines that do not induce ADE are consequently needed. NS1 is a secreted non-structural protein that contributes to dengue pathogenesis by mediating vascular leakage. NS1 is a promising vaccine target because anti-NS1 antibodies are non-neutralizing, thereby evading ADE, and they have also been shown to protect mice from disease. Methods Here, we are developing a DENV2 NS1 mRNA-LNP vaccine that is safe, highly immunogenic, and effective in mice. Results This DENV2 NS1 mRNA-LNP vaccine induces non-neutralizing antibodies that protect against severe disease and mortality in a lethal challenge model. Conclusion In future directions, this work will investigate the mechanisms of non-neutralizing anti-NS1-based protection, including the involvement of affinity maturation and Fc effector functions. Together, we present a promising dengue vaccine strategy that evades ADE and protects against lethal disease. Funding Source Jordan Polster is supported by the HHMI Gilliam Fellows Program. David Martinez is supported by the HHMI Hanna Gray Fellows Program. Topic Categories Vaccines and Immunotherapy (VAC)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue is a mosquito-borne virus that threatens one half of the global population. While primary infection is usually mild or asymptomatic, secondary infection with another of the four serotypes increases the risk for severe disease due to a phenomenon called antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE).
Design and population
This study develops a DENV2 NS1 mRNA-LNP vaccine that is safe, highly immunogenic, and effective in mice. NS1 is a secreted non-structural protein that contributes to dengue pathogenesis by mediating vascular leakage.
Core methods
The study involves developing a DENV2 NS1 mRNA-LNP vaccine and testing its immunogenicity and efficacy in mice. NS1 is a promising vaccine target because anti-NS1 antibodies are non-neutralizing, thereby evading ADE, and they have also been shown to protect mice from disease.
Main results
This DENV2 NS1 mRNA-LNP vaccine induces non-neutralizing antibodies that protect against severe disease and mortality in a lethal challenge model.
Interpretation and novelty
Novel vaccines that do not induce ADE are consequently needed. Together, we present a promising dengue vaccine strategy that evades ADE and protects against lethal disease.
Scientific and public-health significance
This vaccine strategy could inform future dengue vaccine development by providing a method to evade ADE and protect against severe disease.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report limitations or evidence strength. The study is based on results from a mouse model and does not include human trials or long-term follow-up.

四价登革热 prM-E mRNA-LNP 疫苗 通过抗体对易感小鼠提供保护 2255808

A Tetravalent Dengue prM-E mRNA-LNP Vaccine Protects via Antibodies in Susceptible Mice 2255808
作者： Fahima Akther, E. De Buck, Xiaoyang Wang, Norbert Pardi, David Martinez, David Martínez
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 登革病毒 (DENV) 仍然是一个主要的全球健康威胁,目前还没有针对其四种不同血清型:DENV1、DENV2、DENV3 和 DENV4 的最佳 疫苗。登革热 疫苗 开发的一个核心挑战是实现针对所有血清型的平衡且持久的免疫力,因为部分保护可能导致 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE),增加继发感染时发生严重疾病的风险。方法我们开发了一种四价 mRNA 疫苗,编码来自每种 DENV 血清型的 prM-E 蛋白,配制在脂质纳米颗粒 (LNP) 中。将 疫苗 肌肉注射给 C57BL/6J 小鼠。每剂总共含有 5 μg mRNA(每种血清型 1.25 μg),每 4 周间隔一次,进行 3 次免疫。与明矾配制的重组包膜 (rE) 蛋白相比,分析了生发中心 (GC) B 细胞反应和组织驻留记忆 T (Trm) 细胞诱导。结果四价 prM-E mRNA-LNP 疫苗 引发针对所有四种血清型的强烈中和抗体反应,产生的 DENV1-4 效价分别为 5481、4632、12,179 和 5006。来自接种疫苗的小鼠的血清被动转移为受到致命 DENV2 攻击的 AG129 小鼠 (IFN-α/β/γR-/-) 提供了完全保护,导致 100% 存活。到第14天,prM-E mRNA-LNP组表现出CD38^lowGL7+ GC B细胞的频率比rE + Alum组高两倍,表明GC B细胞活性增强。与肌内注射相比,皮内 (ID) 疫苗接种 在皮肤中诱导显着更高的 Trm 细胞形成,在 ID 初免-加强免疫后显示出大约四倍的增加。结论 这些研究结果表明,四价 prM-E mRNA-LNP 疫苗 可引发针对所有 DENV 血清型的强烈体液和组织驻留细胞免疫。结果强调了其强大的免疫原性和保护功效,支持进一步开发基于 mRNA 的登革热 疫苗,以提供广泛而持久的保护。资金来源 不适用 主题类别 疫苗 和免疫疗法 (VAC)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革病毒 (DENV) 仍然是一个主要的全球健康威胁,目前还没有针对其四种不同血清型(DENV1、DENV2、DENV3 和 DENV4)的最佳 疫苗。登革热 疫苗 开发的一个核心挑战是实现针对所有血清型的平衡且持久的免疫力,因为部分保护可能导致 抗体依赖性增强 (ADE),增加继发感染时发生严重疾病的风险。
设计与对象
将编码来自每种 DENV 血清型的 prM-E 蛋白的四价 mRNA 疫苗 配制在脂质纳米颗粒 (LNP) 中,对 C57BL/6J 小鼠进行肌内注射。每剂总共含有 5 μg mRNA(每种血清型 1.25 μg),每 4 周间隔一次,进行 3 次免疫。
核心方法
与明矾配制的重组包膜 (rE) 蛋白相比,分析了生发中心 (GC) B 细胞反应和组织驻留记忆 T (Trm) 细胞诱导。还评估了皮内 (ID) 疫苗接种 皮肤中 Trm 细胞的形成。
主要结果
四价 prM-E mRNA-LNP 疫苗 引发针对所有四种血清型的强烈中和抗体反应,产生的 DENV1-4 效价分别为 5481、4632、12,179 和 5006。来自接种疫苗的小鼠的血清被动转移为受到致命 DENV2 攻击的 AG129 小鼠提供了完全保护,导致 100% 存活。到第 14 天,prM-E mRNA-LNP 组表现出的 CD38^lowGL7+ GC B 细胞频率是 rE + Alum 组的两倍,并且与肌内递送相比,ID 疫苗接种 在皮肤中诱导的 Trm 细胞形成大约高四倍。
解释与创新
这些发现表明,四价 prM-E mRNA-LNP 疫苗 能引发针对所有 DENV 血清型的强烈体液和组织驻留细胞免疫。结果强调了其强大的免疫原性和保护功效,支持进一步开发基于 mRNA 的登革热 疫苗,以提供广泛而持久的保护。
科研与公卫意义
疫苗 能够针对所有四种血清型引发平衡免疫,解决了登革热预防的关键挑战,有可能降低 抗体依赖性增强 和严重疾病的风险。它在动物模型中的功效为将基于 mRNA 的登革热 疫苗 推向临床开发提供了强有力的理由。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在小鼠身上进行的,限制了对人类的直接外推,并且证据仅基于抽象数据,无法完全获取方法或结果。尽管存在这些限制,但在动物模型中观察到的强免疫原性和保护功效为研究结果提供了一定的可信度。

A Tetravalent Dengue prM-E mRNA-LNP Vaccine Protects via Antibodies in Susceptible Mice 2255808

Authors: Fahima Akther, E. De Buck, Xiaoyang Wang, Norbert Pardi, David Martinez, David Martínez
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Dengue virus (DENV) remains a major global health threat, with no optimal vaccine available against its four distinct serotypes: DENV1, DENV2, DENV3, and DENV4. A central challenge in dengue vaccine development is achieving balanced and durable immunity against all serotypes, as partial protection can lead to antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE), increasing the risk of severe disease upon secondary infection. Methods We developed a tetravalent mRNA vaccine encoding the prM-E proteins from each DENV serotype, formulated in lipid nanoparticles (LNPs). The vaccine was administered intramuscularly to C57BL/6J mice. Each dose contained a total of 5 μg mRNA (1.25 μg per serotype) and was given at 4-week intervals for three immunizations. Germinal center (GC) B cell responses and tissue-resident memory T (Trm) cell induction were analyzed in comparison with recombinant Envelope (rE) protein formulated with Alum. Results The tetravalent prM-E mRNA-LNP vaccine elicited strong neutralizing antibody responses against all four serotypes, producing titers of 5481, 4632, 12,179, and 5006 for DENV1-4, respectively. Passive transfer of sera from vaccinated mice conferred complete protection in AG129 mice (IFN-α/β/γR-/-) challenged with lethal DENV2, resulting in 100% survival. By day 14, the prM-E mRNA-LNP group exhibited a two-fold higher frequency of CD38^lowGL7+ GC B cells than the rE + Alum group, indicating enhanced GC B cell activity. Intradermal (ID) vaccination induced significantly higher Trm cell formation in the skin compared to intramuscular delivery, showing approximately four-fold increases following ID prime-boost immunization. Conclusion These findings show that the tetravalent prM-E mRNA-LNP vaccine elicits strong humoral and tissue-resident cellular immunity against all DENV serotypes. The results underscore its robust immunogenicity and protective efficacy, supporting further development of mRNA-based dengue vaccines for broad and durable protection. Funding Source n/a Topic Categories Vaccines and Immunotherapy (VAC)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus (DENV) remains a major global health threat, with no optimal vaccine available against its four distinct serotypes: DENV1, DENV2, DENV3, and DENV4. A central challenge in dengue vaccine development is achieving balanced and durable immunity against all serotypes, as partial protection can lead to antibody-dependent enhancement (ADE), increasing the risk of severe disease upon secondary infection.
Design and population
A tetravalent mRNA vaccine encoding the prM-E proteins from each DENV serotype, formulated in lipid nanoparticles (LNPs), was administered intramuscularly to C57BL/6J mice. Each dose contained a total of 5 μg mRNA (1.25 μg per serotype) and was given at 4-week intervals for three immunizations.
Core methods
Germinal center (GC) B cell responses and tissue-resident memory T (Trm) cell induction were analyzed in comparison with recombinant Envelope (rE) protein formulated with Alum. Intradermal (ID) vaccination was also evaluated for Trm cell formation in the skin.
Main results
The tetravalent prM-E mRNA-LNP vaccine elicited strong neutralizing antibody responses against all four serotypes, producing titers of 5481, 4632, 12,179, and 5006 for DENV1-4, respectively. Passive transfer of sera from vaccinated mice conferred complete protection in AG129 mice challenged with lethal DENV2, resulting in 100% survival. By day 14, the prM-E mRNA-LNP group exhibited a two-fold higher frequency of CD38^lowGL7+ GC B cells than the rE + Alum group, and ID vaccination induced approximately four-fold higher Trm cell formation in the skin compared to intramuscular delivery.
Interpretation and novelty
These findings show that the tetravalent prM-E mRNA-LNP vaccine elicits strong humoral and tissue-resident cellular immunity against all DENV serotypes. The results underscore its robust immunogenicity and protective efficacy, supporting further development of mRNA-based dengue vaccines for broad and durable protection.
Scientific and public-health significance
The vaccine's ability to elicit balanced immunity against all four serotypes addresses a critical challenge in dengue prevention, potentially reducing the risk of antibody-dependent enhancement and severe disease. Its efficacy in animal models provides a strong rationale for advancing mRNA-based dengue vaccines to clinical development.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in mice, limiting direct extrapolation to humans, and the evidence is based solely on abstract data without full access to methods or results. Despite these limitations, the strong immunogenicity and protective efficacy observed in animal models provide moderate confidence in the findings.

利用红外热成像确定尼泊尔生态区登革热 媒介 栖息地的温度参数:试点可行性研究。

I﻿nfrared thermal imaging to determine temperature parameters of dengue vector habitats across ecological regions of Nepal: a pilot feasibility study.
作者： Louise A Kelly-Hope, Ajit Kumar Karna, Bijay Bajracharya, Satya Raj Paudel, Srijana Kc, Pooja Shah, Matthew Baylis, Gokarna Dahal, Deena Shrestha, Louise A. Kelly‐Hope
摘要中文翻译登革热是尼泊尔一种主要的 媒介 传播疾病,由在家庭和城市环境中繁殖的容器繁殖伊蚊驱动。由于城市化和全球变暖,从低地特莱到丘陵和山区,其风险不断增加。需要实用和创新的方法来更好地了解对伊蚊栖息地的影响,而伊蚊栖息地是 媒介 控制的主要目标。我们进行了一项试点可行性研究,以描述埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊繁殖地的特征,检查物理化学参数(总溶解固体、pH、盐度、溶解氧),并探索红外热成像作为测量不同生态区域栖息地温度参数的新工具的效用。我们发现塑料容器、锡/金属物品和橡胶轮胎是常见的繁殖地。登革热 媒介 Ae。在大多数地点的各种类型的集装箱中都发现了白纹伊蚊。总体而言,热像仪和标准温度计之间存在合理的一致性,尽管热像仪记录的温度较低约 1°C,并且在较高温度下显示出差异。大多数水生栖息地的温度都在蚊子的最佳繁殖范围内(20 摄氏度到 30 摄氏度以下),并且低于非繁殖地温度。特莱地区的繁殖地水温比中山和山区高约 4°C。还发现水生栖息地温度与溶解氧显着相关。热成像案例研究为复杂的伊蚊微气候中的不同温度提供了关键的视觉见解。值得注意的是,尽管外部温度波动较大且外部轮胎温度超过 50°C,但内部橡胶轮胎温度仍保持相对稳定。热成像显示了蚊子繁殖地现场表征的前景,但在未经资格认证(包括校准和不确定性分析)的情况下,它不能与标准测温法互换。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热是尼泊尔一个日益严重的公共卫生问题,城市化和气候变化推动 媒介 栖息地不断扩大,但描述这些栖息地特征的实用工具却很有限。该研究询问红外热成像是否可以提供伊蚊繁殖地的新颖、可现场使用的温度数据,以便为 媒介 控制提供信息。
设计与对象
在尼泊尔不同的生态区域(特莱、丘陵、山区)进行了一项试点可行性研究,以检查埃及伊蚊和白纹伊蚊的繁殖地点及其理化参数。
核心方法
该研究描述了繁殖地点的特征,测量了物理化学参数(总溶解固体、pH、盐度、溶解氧),并根据测量栖息地温度的标准测温法评估了红外热成像。
主要结果
塑料容器、锡/金属物品和橡胶轮胎是常见的繁殖地。热成像与标准温度计相当一致,但记录温度较低约 1°C,在较高温度下出现偏差。大多数水生栖息地的温度都在蚊子的最佳繁殖范围内(20 摄氏度到 30 摄氏度以下),并且低于非繁殖地。
解释与创新
热成像提供了对伊蚊微气候的视觉洞察,并显示尽管外部波动,橡胶轮胎内部仍保持稳定的温度。作者得出的结论是,热成像对于现场表征很有希望,但在没有校准和不确定性分析的情况下不能与标准测温法互换。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,热成像可以通过绘制温度变化图来增强伊蚊栖息地的 监测,从而有可能指导尼泊尔不同生态区的针对性 媒介 控制。
局限与证据强度
作为一项试点可行性研究,证据是初步的,并且由于规模小、缺乏校准协议以及与高温下标准测温法的差异而受到限制,因此需要适度的信心。

I﻿nfrared thermal imaging to determine temperature parameters of dengue vector habitats across ecological regions of Nepal: a pilot feasibility study.

Authors: Louise A Kelly-Hope, Ajit Kumar Karna, Bijay Bajracharya, Satya Raj Paudel, Srijana Kc, Pooja Shah, Matthew Baylis, Gokarna Dahal, Deena Shrestha, Louise A. Kelly‐Hope
Original abstract (English)
Dengue is a major vector-borne disease in Nepal, driven by container-breeding Aedes mosquitoes that thrive in domestic, urban settings. Its risk is increasing from the lowland Terai to the Hill and Mountain regions due to urbanisation and global warming. Practical and innovative approaches are needed to better understand the impact on Aedes mosquito habitats that are the main targets for vector control. We conducted a pilot feasibility study to characterise Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus breeding sites, examine physicochemical parameters (total dissolved solids, pH, salinity, dissolved oxygen), and explore the utility of infrared thermal imaging as a novel tool for measuring temperature parameters of habitats across distinct ecological regions. We found that plastic containers, tin/metal items, and rubber tyres were common breeding sites. The dengue vectors Ae. albopictus were identified in a variety of container types across most locations. Overall, there was reasonable agreement between the thermal camera and standard thermometer, although the camera recorded approximately 1 °C lower and showed divergence at higher temperatures. Most aquatic habitat temperatures were within an optimal mosquito breeding range (mid-20s to low 30s °C) and lower than non-breeding site temperatures. The breeding site water temperatures in the Terai region were ~ 4 °C higher than the Mid-Hill and Mountain regions. Aquatic habitat temperatures were also found to be significantly correlated with dissolved oxygen. Thermal imaging case studies provided key visual insights into varying temperatures in complex Aedes microclimates. Notably, inner rubber tyre temperatures remained relatively stable despite significant external temperature fluctuations and external tyre temperatures exceeding 50 °C. Thermal imaging shows promise for the field characterisation of mosquito breeding sites, but it is not interchangeable with standard thermometry without qualification, including calibration and uncertainty analysis.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue is a growing public-health problem in Nepal, with expanding vector habitats driven by urbanisation and climate change, yet practical tools to characterise these habitats are limited. The study asked whether infrared thermal imaging could provide novel, field-usable temperature data on Aedes mosquito breeding sites to inform vector control.
Design and population
A pilot feasibility study was conducted across distinct ecological regions of Nepal (Terai, Hill, Mountain) to examine Aedes aegypti and Aedes albopictus breeding sites and their physicochemical parameters.
Core methods
The study characterised breeding sites, measured physicochemical parameters (total dissolved solids, pH, salinity, dissolved oxygen), and evaluated infrared thermal imaging against standard thermometry for measuring habitat temperatures.
Main results
Plastic containers, tin/metal items, and rubber tyres were common breeding sites. Thermal imaging agreed reasonably with standard thermometers but recorded about 1 °C lower and diverged at higher temperatures. Most aquatic habitat temperatures fell within the optimal mosquito breeding range (mid-20s to low 30s °C) and were lower than non-breeding sites.
Interpretation and novelty
Thermal imaging provided visual insights into Aedes microclimates and showed that rubber tyre interiors maintained stable temperatures despite external fluctuations. The authors concluded that thermal imaging is promising for field characterisation but not interchangeable with standard thermometry without calibration and uncertainty analysis.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest thermal imaging could enhance surveillance of Aedes habitats by mapping temperature variability, potentially guiding targeted vector control in Nepal’s diverse ecological zones.
Limitations and evidence strength
As a pilot feasibility study, the evidence is preliminary and limited by small scale, lack of calibration protocols, and divergence from standard thermometry at high temperatures, warranting moderate confidence.
