关于法国海外领土 登革热 医院和经济负担的真实世界研究。

A real-world study on hospital and economic burden of dengue fever in French overseas territories.
作者： Julie Cornebise, Solenne Tauty, Anouar Salhi, Cédric Strub, Franck Favre-Besse, Françoise Bugnard, Cinira Lefèvre, Samuel Markowicz
摘要中文翻译简介:登革热 发病率 近年来显着增加,对法国海外领土的公共卫生构成威胁。地区/年龄组缺乏有关其负担的现实数据。目标:描述法国海外领土登革热的医院负担和相关费用。方法:利用法国国家医院数据库 (PMSI),确定了 2017 年至 2024 年马提尼克岛、瓜德罗普岛、法属圭亚那 (FG) 和留尼汪岛的登革热住院情况。计算了总体和流行期间每 100,000 例全因住院的住院率 (EP)。在 EP 中,报告了患者和住院特征以及费用。按年龄组/地区进行分析。结果:2017 年至 2024 年期间,8,399 名患者住院,其中 95.7% 在 EP 期间住院。EP 期间的住院率比总体住院率高 2.6 倍,其中 5-19 岁患者的住院率最高。住院患者大多数为女性,平均年龄从 28.2 岁(FG)到 51.3 岁(留尼汪岛)不等。平均住院时间从 3.4 天(FG)到 5.1 天(留尼汪岛)不等,留尼汪岛通过急诊室入院的频率最高(91.8%)。每次住院费用中位数(第一季度至第三季度)范围从 1,403 欧元(846-2,876 欧元)(FG)到 3,385 欧元(989-7,119 欧元)(留尼汪岛)。结论:这项研究强调了登革热医院的巨大负担,尤其是 5-19 岁的儿童,而且法国海外领土之间存在差异。包括 疫苗接种 在内的预防策略可以减轻这种负担。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革热 发病率 在法国海外领土上大幅上升,但按领土和年龄组划分的医院和经济负担的真实数据仍然很少。该研究旨在量化这些地区因登革热而造成的医院负担和相关费用。
设计与对象
这项回顾性研究使用法国国家医院数据库 (PMSI) 来确定 2017 年至 2024 年马提尼克岛、瓜德罗普岛、法属圭亚那和留尼汪岛的登革热住院情况。按年龄组和地区进行分析。
核心方法
Hospitalization rates per 100,000 all-cause hospitalizations were calculated overall and during epidemic periods. Patient and stay characteristics and costs were reported specifically within epidemic periods.
主要结果
2017年至2024年间,有8,399名患者住院,其中95.7%发生在流行期间。流行病期间的住院率是总体住院率的 2.6 倍,其中 5-19 岁患者的住院率最高。平均住院时间为 3.4 天(法属圭亚那)至 5.1 天(留尼汪岛),中位住院费用为 1,403 欧元(法属圭亚那)至 3,385 欧元(留尼汪岛)。
解释与创新
该研究强调了登革热医院的巨大负担,特别是对于 5-19 岁的儿童,其持续时间和费用存在明显的地域差异。这一现实世界的证据强调了有针对性的预防策略的必要性。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果支持在高负担地区和年龄组优先考虑 疫苗接种 和其他预防措施,以减少住院治疗和相关费用。
局限与证据强度
该研究依赖于医院的行政数据,这些数据可能缺乏临床细节并低估非住院病例,限制了普遍性和因果推断。由于这些限制,证据强度中等。

A real-world study on hospital and economic burden of dengue fever in French overseas territories.

Authors: Julie Cornebise, Solenne Tauty, Anouar Salhi, Cédric Strub, Franck Favre-Besse, Françoise Bugnard, Cinira Lefèvre, Samuel Markowicz
Original abstract (English)
INTRODUCTION: Dengue incidence significantly increased in recent years, representing a public health threat in French overseas territories. Real-world data on its burden is lacking by territory/age groups. OBJECTIVES: To describe the hospital burden and associated costs of dengue in French overseas territories. METHODS: Using the French national hospital database (PMSI), dengue hospitalizations in Martinique, Guadeloupe, French Guiana (FG), and La Réunion were identified over 2017-2024. Hospitalization rates per 100,000 all-cause hospitalizations were calculated overall and during epidemic periods (EPs). Within EPs, patients and stays characteristics and costs were reported. Analyses were conducted by age groups/territories. RESULTS: Over 2017-2024, 8,399 patients were hospitalized, 95.7% during EPs. Hospitalization rates were 2.6 times higher during EPs than overall and were the highest in patients aged 5-19. Hospitalizations mostly involved women and mean age ranged from 28.2 (FG) to 51.3 (La Réunion) years. Mean hospitalization duration ranged from 3.4 (FG) to 5.1 (La Réunion) days, and admissions via emergency room were most frequent in La Réunion (91.8%). Median (Q1-Q3) cost per hospitalization ranged from €1,403 (€846-€2,876) (FG) to €3,385 (€989- €7,119) (La Réunion). CONCLUSIONS: This study highlights the substantial dengue hospital burden, particularly in 5-19-year-old, with variations across French overseas territories. Preventive strategies, including vaccination could reduce this burden.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue incidence has risen substantially in French overseas territories, yet real-world data on its hospital and economic burden by territory and age group remain scarce. The study aimed to quantify the hospital burden and associated costs of dengue in these regions.
Design and population
This retrospective study used the French national hospital database (PMSI) to identify dengue hospitalizations in Martinique, Guadeloupe, French Guiana, and La Réunion from 2017 to 2024. Analyses were conducted by age groups and territories.
Core methods
Hospitalization rates per 100,000 all-cause hospitalizations were calculated overall and during epidemic periods. Patient and stay characteristics and costs were reported specifically within epidemic periods.
Main results
Over 2017–2024, 8,399 patients were hospitalized, with 95.7% occurring during epidemic periods. Hospitalization rates were 2.6 times higher during epidemic periods than overall and were highest in patients aged 5–19 years. Mean hospitalization duration ranged from 3.4 days (French Guiana) to 5.1 days (La Réunion), and median hospitalization cost ranged from €1,403 (French Guiana) to €3,385 (La Réunion).
Interpretation and novelty
The study highlights a substantial dengue hospital burden, particularly among children aged 5–19 years, with marked territorial variation in duration and cost. This real-world evidence underscores the need for targeted preventive strategies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings support prioritizing vaccination and other preventive measures in high-burden territories and age groups to reduce hospitalizations and associated costs.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study relies on administrative hospital data, which may lack clinical detail and underestimate non-hospitalized cases, limiting generalizability and causal inference. The evidence strength is moderate due to these limitations.

温度变化作为沃尔巴克氏体释放功效的驱动因素:对气候变暖中登革热控制的影响

Temperature variation as a driver of Wolbachia release efficacy: implications for dengue control in warming climates
作者： Juanita Prada-Mora, Santiago Villamil-Chacon, Mauricio Santos-Vega, Mauricio Santos‐Vega
摘要中文翻译背景 基于沃尔巴克氏体的控制方法通过抑制埃及伊蚊种群或阻断病毒复制来减少登革热 传播,但其在流行地区气候条件下的有效性仍知之甚少。季节性和年际温度变化影响着蚊子的动态,但沃尔巴克氏体在厄尔尼诺或热浪等异常事件期间的释放情况在很大程度上尚不清楚,这一差距限制了我们优化释放策略和预测不同气候下干预成功的能力。方法/主要发现为了解决这一差距,我们建立了一个数学模型框架,明确地将温度依赖性沃尔巴克氏体参数与登革热流行地区的季节性和年际气候变化特征结合在一起,将详细的沃尔巴克氏体动力学模型与易感感染恢复(SIR)流行病学模型相结合,以评估沃尔巴克氏体在不同热状况下的建立、持久性和稳定性,并追踪其对疾病传播的下游影响。较高的温度会侵蚀沃尔巴克氏体的建立和长期持续性,当条件接近 30°C 时,有效释放策略的范围急剧缩小。季节性又增加了一层复杂性:与沃尔巴克氏体频率相关的热应激时间,而不仅仅是其大小,决定了种群更替是否成功。年际变化、逐渐变暖、季节性波动扩大和热峰移位,每一个都侵蚀着沃尔巴克氏体 患病率 和稳定性,其影响在连续几年中加剧。登革热 传播 密切跟踪这些动态,温暖的条件会产生更大、更早的 暴发,干预的成功取决于释放时间、频率和目标如何与当地的热条件相匹配。结论/意义 随着气候变化导致极端高温事件变得更加频繁,忽视当地高温条件的发布计划可能会在最需要控制登革热的地方出现不足。通过阐明温度、沃尔巴克氏体和登革热之间的机制相互作用,我们的研究结果有助于完善沃尔巴克氏体释放计划以适应不同的气候条件,从而在气候变化加剧时加强登革热控制。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究是必要的,因为基于沃尔巴克氏体的登革热控制方法在流行地区的气候条件下的有效性仍然知之甚少,特别是在厄尔尼诺或热浪等异常事件期间。
设计与对象
该研究使用了一个数学模型框架,该框架结合了温度依赖性沃尔巴克氏体参数和登革热流行地区的气候变化特征,将沃尔巴克氏体动力学模型与易感感染恢复(SIR)流行病学模型相结合。
核心方法
该方法涉及建立一个数学模型,明确地将与温度相关的沃尔巴克氏体参数、季节性和年际气候变化结合起来,并将沃尔巴克氏体动力学与 SIR 流行病学模型结合起来,以评估不同热状况下沃尔巴克氏体的建立、持久性和稳定性。
主要结果
较高的温度会侵蚀沃尔巴克氏体的建立和长期持久性,当条件接近 30°C 时,会缩小有效释放策略的范围;季节性增加了复杂性,因为与沃尔巴克氏体频率相关的热应激时间决定了种群更替的成功;年际变化、逐渐变暖和热峰偏移连续几年加剧了影响。
解释与创新
作者解释说,气候变化下的极端高温事件可能会降低最需要控制登革热的沃尔巴克氏体释放计划的有效性,而新颖之处在于阐明温度、沃尔巴克氏体和登革热动力学之间的机制相互作用。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为完善沃尔巴克氏体释放计划以适应不同的气候条件提供信息,从而在气候变化加剧时加强登革热控制,并强调需要将释放时间、频率和目标与当地的热条件相匹配。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为该研究依赖于未经实证验证的数学模型,并且局限性包括缺乏真实世界数据来证实实际气候条件下的模型预测。

Temperature variation as a driver of Wolbachia release efficacy: implications for dengue control in warming climates

Authors: Juanita Prada-Mora, Santiago Villamil-Chacon, Mauricio Santos-Vega, Mauricio Santos‐Vega
Original abstract (English)
Background Wolbachia-based control methods reduce dengue transmission by suppressing Aedes aegypti populations or blocking viral replication, yet their effectiveness across the climatic conditions of endemic regions remains poorly understood. Seasonal and interannual temperature changes shape mosquito dynamics, but how Wolbachia releases perform during anomalous events such as El Nino or heatwaves is largely unknown a gap that limits our ability to optimize release strategies and predict intervention success across different climates. Methodology/Principal Findings To address this gap, we built a mathematical modeling framework that explicitly incorporates temperature-dependent Wolbachia parameters together with the seasonal and interannual climate variability characteristic of dengue-endemic regions, coupling a detailed Wolbachia dynamics model with a susceptible infectious recovered (SIR) epidemiological model to evaluate Wolbachia establishment, persistence, and stability under different thermal regimes and trace their downstream impact on disease spread. Higher temperatures eroded both Wolbachia establishment and long-term persistence, sharply narrowing the range of effective release strategies as conditions approached 30 C. Seasonality added a further layer of complexity: the timing of thermal stress relative to Wolbachia frequency, not merely its magnitude, determined whether population replacement succeeded. Interannual shifts, progressive warming, widening seasonal swings, and displaced thermal peaks, each eroded Wolbachia prevalence and stability, with effects that compounded over successive years. Dengue transmission tracked these dynamics closely, with warmer conditions producing larger, earlier outbreaks, and intervention success hinging on how release timing, frequency, and targeting were matched to local thermal conditions. Conclusions/Significance As extreme heat events become more frequent under climate change, release programs that ignore local thermal conditions risk falling short where dengue control is most needed. By elucidating the mechanistic interplay among temperature, Wolbachia, and dengue, our findings help refine Wolbachia release programs to suit different climatic conditions, thereby strengthening dengue control as climate variability intensifies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study was needed because the effectiveness of Wolbachia-based dengue control methods across climatic conditions in endemic regions remains poorly understood, particularly during anomalous events like El Nino or heatwaves.
Design and population
The study used a mathematical modeling framework that incorporated temperature-dependent Wolbachia parameters and climate variability characteristic of dengue-endemic regions, coupling a Wolbachia dynamics model with a susceptible infectious recovered (SIR) epidemiological model.
Core methods
The methodology involved building a mathematical model that explicitly incorporates temperature-dependent Wolbachia parameters, seasonal and interannual climate variability, and couples Wolbachia dynamics with an SIR epidemiological model to evaluate Wolbachia establishment, persistence, and stability under different thermal regimes.
Main results
Higher temperatures eroded Wolbachia establishment and long-term persistence, narrowing the range of effective release strategies as conditions approached 30°C; seasonality added complexity as the timing of thermal stress relative to Wolbachia frequency determined population replacement success; interannual shifts, progressive warming, and displaced thermal peaks compounded effects over successive years.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that extreme heat events under climate change risk reducing the effectiveness of Wolbachia release programs where dengue control is most needed, and the novelty lies in elucidating the mechanistic interplay among temperature, Wolbachia, and dengue dynamics.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform the refinement of Wolbachia release programs to suit different climatic conditions, thereby strengthening dengue control as climate variability intensifies, and highlight the need to match release timing, frequency, and targeting to local thermal conditions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate as the study relies on a mathematical model without empirical validation, and limitations include the lack of real-world data to confirm model predictions under actual climatic conditions.

将孕妇作为参考人群来估计登革热漏报:巴西圣保罗州的方法建议和应用。

Pregnant women as a reference population to estimate dengue under-reporting: methodological proposal and application in São Paulo State, Brazil.
作者： Leandro Cesar Pompeu, Fredi Alexander Diaz-Quijano
摘要中文翻译目的:以孕妇 发病率 作为参考,提出并应用一种方法来估计非孕妇中可能的登革热病例的漏报情况。方法:使用巴西圣保罗州 2024 年 1 月至 6 月期间报告的登革热疑似病例数据进行生态研究。假设孕妇因产前随访而出现 诊断 和通知的可能性较高,则以此组为参考,计算 18 至 45 岁非孕妇中登革热 诊断 的漏报情况。使用逻辑回归评估了报告不足与家庭健康战略覆盖率、每千名育龄妇女初级卫生保健单位数量以及城市化程度等因素之间的关联。还进行了漏报的空间自相关分析。结果:在分析的 645 个城市中,有 108 个城市 (16.7%) 有证据表明存在少报情况,比例从 1% 到 95% 不等。据估计,研究期间未在非孕妇中发现 8,624 例登革热病例。在分析的因素和漏报之间没有发现统计学上显着的关联。空间分析发现该州西北部地区存在大量漏报城市。结论:所提出的方法可以识别可能漏报的领域,并支持对 监测 系统敏感性决定因素的分析,从而有助于指导干预措施。尽管可能适用于其他法定疾病,但由于风险、脆弱性和诊断标准的差异,应谨慎解释孕妇和非孕妇之间的比较。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
以孕妇的 发病率 作为参考,提出并应用一种方法来估计非孕妇中可能的登革热病例的漏报情况。
设计与对象
使用 2024 年 1 月至 6 月巴西圣保罗州报告的可能登革热病例数据进行的生态研究。
核心方法
假设孕妇因产前随访而发生诊断和通知的可能性较高,则以此组为参考来计算18至45岁非孕妇中登革热诊断的漏报情况。使用逻辑回归评估了报告不足与家庭健康战略覆盖率、每千名育龄妇女初级卫生保健单位数量以及城市化程度等因素之间的关联。还进行了漏报的空间自相关分析。
主要结果
在分析的 645 个城市中,有 108 个城市 (16.7%) 有证据表明存在少报情况,比例从 1% 到 95% 不等。据估计,研究期间未在非孕妇中发现 8,624 例登革热病例。在分析的因素和漏报之间没有发现统计学上显着的关联。空间分析发现该州西北部地区存在大量漏报城市。
解释与创新
所提出的方法可以识别可能漏报的领域,并支持对 监测 系统敏感性决定因素的分析,从而帮助指导干预措施。尽管可能适用于其他法定疾病,但由于风险、脆弱性和诊断标准的差异,应谨慎解释孕妇和非孕妇之间的比较。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果可以为漏报率高的地区制定有针对性的 监测 和干预策略提供信息,从而改善登革热病例检测和公共卫生应对措施。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到其生态设计和孕妇通知率较高的假设的限制,这可能无法完全解释风险和诊断标准的差异。由于研究的设计和解释组间比较时需要谨慎,证据强度中等。

Pregnant women as a reference population to estimate dengue under-reporting: methodological proposal and application in São Paulo State, Brazil.

Authors: Leandro Cesar Pompeu, Fredi Alexander Diaz-Quijano
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: To propose and apply a methodology to estimate the under-reporting of probable dengue cases among non-pregnant women, using the incidence in pregnant women as a reference. METHODS: Ecological study using data on probable dengue cases reported in the state of São Paulo, Brazil, between January and June 2024. Assuming that pregnant women have a higher likelihood of diagnosis and notification due to prenatal follow-up, this group was taken as a reference to calculate the under-reporting of dengue incidence among non-pregnant women aged 18 to 45 years. The association between under-reporting and factors such as Family Health Strategy coverage, number of Primary Health Care Units per thousand women at reproductive age, and degree of urbanization was assessed using logistic regression. Spatial autocorrelation analysis of under-reporting was also performed. RESULTS: Of the 645 municipalities analyzed, 108 (16.7%) showed evidence of under-reporting, ranging from 1% to 95%. It was estimated that 8,624 dengue cases were not detected among non-pregnant women during the study period. No statistically significant associations were found between the analyzed factors and under-reporting. Spatial analysis identified significant clusters of municipalities with underreporting in the northwestern region of the state. CONCLUSION: The proposed methodology may identify areas with probable underreporting and support analyses of determinants of surveillance system sensitivity, thereby helping to guide interventions. Although potentially applicable to other notifiable diseases, comparisons between pregnant and non-pregnant women should be interpreted with caution due to differences in risk, vulnerability, and diagnostic criteria.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
To propose and apply a methodology to estimate the under-reporting of probable dengue cases among non-pregnant women, using the incidence in pregnant women as a reference.
Design and population
Ecological study using data on probable dengue cases reported in the state of São Paulo, Brazil, between January and June 2024.
Core methods
Assuming that pregnant women have a higher likelihood of diagnosis and notification due to prenatal follow-up, this group was taken as a reference to calculate the under-reporting of dengue incidence among non-pregnant women aged 18 to 45 years. The association between under-reporting and factors such as Family Health Strategy coverage, number of Primary Health Care Units per thousand women at reproductive age, and degree of urbanization was assessed using logistic regression. Spatial autocorrelation analysis of under-reporting was also performed.
Main results
Of the 645 municipalities analyzed, 108 (16.7%) showed evidence of under-reporting, ranging from 1% to 95%. It was estimated that 8,624 dengue cases were not detected among non-pregnant women during the study period. No statistically significant associations were found between the analyzed factors and under-reporting. Spatial analysis identified significant clusters of municipalities with underreporting in the northwestern region of the state.
Interpretation and novelty
The proposed methodology may identify areas with probable underreporting and support analyses of determinants of surveillance system sensitivity, thereby helping to guide interventions. Although potentially applicable to other notifiable diseases, comparisons between pregnant and non-pregnant women should be interpreted with caution due to differences in risk, vulnerability, and diagnostic criteria.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings can inform targeted surveillance and intervention strategies in areas with high under-reporting, improving dengue case detection and public health response.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited by its ecological design and the assumption that pregnant women have higher notification rates, which may not fully account for differences in risk and diagnostic criteria. The evidence strength is moderate due to the study's design and the caution needed in interpreting comparisons between groups.

登革热流行地区与埃及伊蚊存在和持续存在相关的家庭特征、居民行为和季节因素

Household Characteristics, Residents’ Behaviors, and Seasonal Factors Associated With the Presence and Persistence of Aedes aegypti in a Dengue-Endemic Area
作者： Alejandro Cortés-Meda, Guadalupe Ponciano Rodríguez, Guadalupe Silvia García-de la Torre, Julián Everardo García-Rejón, Jorge Fernando Méndez-Galván, Julián E. García‐Rejón, Jorge F. Méndez-Galván
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在评估家庭特征、居民的预防行为、季节性因素以及登革热流行地区埃及伊蚊的存在和持续性之间的关联。研究结果旨在确定可改变的家庭和行为因素,这些因素可以为有针对性的、季节性调整的 媒介 控制策略提供信息。
设计与对象
这项前瞻性 队列研究 研究在墨西哥瓦哈卡州圣佩德罗波楚特拉进行。一次性选择了 68 处居住住宅,并根据其结构、边缘化程度、脆弱性和环境进行了特征分析。
核心方法
在 12 个月的时间内,在室内和室外进行了七次昆虫学评估,以评估埃及伊蚊的存在、持久性和季节性模式。代表埃及伊蚊存在和持续存在风险的住宅特征是门上没有蚊帐、床上没有凉亭、家里有动物以及室内没有使用杀虫剂。
主要结果
总共进行了 476 次访问,确定了 494 个埃及伊蚊幼虫或蛹呈阳性的繁殖地点。总共 48.5% 的研究家庭至少两次呈阳性,且存在明显的时间变化,特别是在雨季 (67.7%)。大多数繁殖地点位于室外(96%),主要在可控容器中(73%)。最重要的栖息地是鼓、桶/水槽/浴缸/水池、罐头/水桶、轮胎和厨房/洗涤用具(72.8%)。
解释与创新
家庭条件、人类行为和季节变化显着影响埃及伊蚊的存在和持久性。这些发现可以为有针对性的 媒介 控制策略提供信息,特别是关注可改变的风险因素和季节性干预措施。
科研与公卫意义
它们提供了对导致埃及伊蚊在登革热流行地区持续存在的环境和行为因素的见解。
局限与证据强度
该研究是在一个地点进行的,这可能会限制普遍性。证据是强有力的,因为它基于对繁殖地点的直接观察以及与家庭和行为因素的关联。

Household Characteristics, Residents’ Behaviors, and Seasonal Factors Associated With the Presence and Persistence of Aedes aegypti in a Dengue-Endemic Area

Authors: Alejandro Cortés-Meda, Guadalupe Ponciano Rodríguez, Guadalupe Silvia García-de la Torre, Julián Everardo García-Rejón, Jorge Fernando Méndez-Galván, Julián E. García‐Rejón, Jorge F. Méndez-Galván
Original abstract (English)
Objective: This study aimed to evaluate the association between household characteristics, residents' preventive behaviors, seasonal factors, and the presence and persistence of Aedes aegypti in a dengue-endemic area. The findings are intended to identify modifiable household and behavioral factors that can inform targeted, seasonally adapted vector control strategies. Methodology: This prospective cohort study was conducted in San Pedro Pochutla, Oaxaca, Mexico. A total of 68 inhabited dwellings were selected and characterized on a single occasion according to their structure, degree of marginalization, vulnerability, and environment. Over a period of 12 months, seven entomological evaluations were performed indoors and outdoors to assess the presence, persistence, and seasonal patterns of Aedes aegypti. Results: A total of 476 visits were conducted, identifying 494 breeding sites positive for Aedes aegypti larvae or pupae. A total of 48.5% of the homes studied were positive on at least two occasions, with marked temporal variation, particularly during the rainy season (67.7%). Most breeding sites were located outdoors (96%), predominantly in controllable containers (73%). The most important habitats were drums, barrels/sinks/tubs/pools, cans/buckets, tires, and kitchen/washing utensils (72.8%). The characteristics of the dwelling that represented a risk for the presence and persistence of Aedes aegypti were the lack of mosquito nets on doors (OR=1.31, 95% CI=1.08-5.20), lack of pavilions on beds (OR=1.95, 95% CI=1.16-8.35), animals in the household (OR=2.27, 95% CI=1.19-7.63), and not applying insecticide indoors (OR=1.64, 95% CI=1.10-4.21). Conclusion: Household conditions, human behavior, and seasonal variation significantly influence the presence and persistence of Aedes aegypti. These findings can inform targeted vector control strategies, particularly focusing on modifiable risk factors and seasonal interventions.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to evaluate the association between household characteristics, residents' preventive behaviors, seasonal factors, and the presence and persistence of Aedes aegypti in a dengue-endemic area. The findings are intended to identify modifiable household and behavioral factors that can inform targeted, seasonally adapted vector control strategies.
Design and population
This prospective cohort study was conducted in San Pedro Pochutla, Oaxaca, Mexico. A total of 68 inhabited dwellings were selected and characterized on a single occasion according to their structure, degree of marginalization, vulnerability, and environment.
Core methods
Over a period of 12 months, seven entomological evaluations were performed indoors and outdoors to assess the presence, persistence, and seasonal patterns of Aedes aegypti. The characteristics of the dwelling that represented a risk for the presence and persistence of Aedes aegypti were the lack of mosquito nets on doors, lack of pavilions on beds, animals in the household, and not applying insecticide indoors.
Main results
A total of 476 visits were conducted, identifying 494 breeding sites positive for Aedes aegypti larvae or pupae. A total of 48.5% of the homes studied were positive on at least two occasions, with marked temporal variation, particularly during the rainy season (67.7%). Most breeding sites were located outdoors (96%), predominantly in controllable containers (73%). The most important habitats were drums, barrels/sinks/tubs/pools, cans/buckets, tires, and kitchen/washing utensils (72.8%).
Interpretation and novelty
Household conditions, human behavior, and seasonal variation significantly influence the presence and persistence of Aedes aegypti. These findings can inform targeted vector control strategies, particularly focusing on modifiable risk factors and seasonal interventions.
Scientific and public-health significance
They provide insights into the environmental and behavioral factors that contribute to the persistence of Aedes aegypti in dengue-endemic areas.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was conducted in a single location, which may limit generalizability. The evidence is strong as it is based on direct observations of breeding sites and associations with household and behavioral factors.

登革病毒-4 人类挑战模型 2243013 中非中和抗体反应的表征

Characterization of Non-Neutralizing Antibody Responses in a Dengue Virus-4 Human Challenge Model 2243013
作者： Ying Yi Zheng, Jeffrey Currier, Heather Fribert, Jane Morwitzer
摘要中文翻译摘要 简介 四种 登革病毒 (DENV-1-4) 之一的初次感染会诱导血清型特异性抗体 (Ab),提供持久的保护,并产生交叉反应抗体,提供短暂的免疫。虽然中和抗体滴度通常用于评估保护作用,但它们不能可靠地预测疾病结果,并且对介导非中和效应功能的抗体的作用仍知之甚少。我们提出了一种评估人类实验性 DENV 感染的非中和抗体 (nNAb) 反应的方法。方法 在一项 1 期开放标签研究中,14 名未接触过登革热的受试者皮下接种了轻微减毒的活 DENV-4 毒株 H-241。感染前和感染后 6 个月收集血浆,以测量 Ab 依赖性补体沉积、Ab 依赖性细胞吞噬作用和 Ab 依赖性细胞毒性 (ADCC)。所有测定均使用感染 DENV 或转染表达 DENV 衍生表面结合 NS1 蛋白的靶细胞。结果感染受试者产生 DENV-4 特异性补体固定抗体和抗 NS1 抗体,促进单核细胞介导的靶细胞吞噬作用。DENV-4 特异性抗体还在一部分受试者中介导 NK 细胞活化。结论 未来的工作将侧重于将这项功能研究扩展到四价 疫苗 的接受者,然后进行 DENV-1 攻击,以研究 nNAbs 如何与疾病保护/风险相关,并扩大我们对中和 Abs 之外的登革热免疫防御的理解。资金来源 DOD 主题类别 病毒免疫学 (VIR)
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在评估非中和抗体 (nNAb) 在 登革病毒 感染中的作用,因为中和抗体滴度不能可靠地预测疾病结果,而且 nNAb 的功能仍然知之甚少。
设计与对象
在一项1期开放标签研究中,14名登革病毒无感染史受试者接受了皮下接种,接种物为一种轻度减毒的活登革病毒4型毒株H-241。
核心方法
使用感染 DENV 或转染表达 DENV 衍生的表面结合 NS1 蛋白的靶细胞,从感染前和感染后 6 个月收集血浆,以测量 Ab 依赖性补体沉积、Ab 依赖性细胞吞噬作用和 Ab 依赖性细胞毒性 (ADCC)。
主要结果
受感染的受试者产生 DENV-4 特异性补体固定抗体和抗 NS1 抗体,促进单核细胞介导的靶细胞吞噬作用,并且 DENV-4 特异性抗体还在受试者亚群中介导 NK 细胞活化。
解释与创新
作者证明,非中和抗体在 免疫应答 至 DENV-4 感染中发挥作用,为除中和抗体之外的免疫防御提供了新的见解。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现为未来关于 nNAb 如何与疾病保护或风险相关的研究提供了信息,可能会改善 疫苗 的开发和对登革热免疫的理解。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量较小且只关注单一血清型的限制,但使用功能性抗体反应的直接测量提供了中等的证据强度。

Characterization of Non-Neutralizing Antibody Responses in a Dengue Virus-4 Human Challenge Model 2243013

Authors: Ying Yi Zheng, Jeffrey Currier, Heather Fribert, Jane Morwitzer
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Primary infection with one of the four dengue viruses (DENV-1—4) induces serotype-specific antibodies (Abs) that confer durable protection and cross-reactive Abs that offer transient immunity. While neutralizing Ab titers are commonly used to assess protection, they do not reliably predict disease outcome, and the role of Abs that mediate non-neutralizing effector functions remains poorly understood. We present an approach to evaluate the non-neutralizing Ab (nNAb) response to experimental DENV infection in humans. Methods In a phase 1 open-label study, 14 dengue—naïve subjects were subcutaneously inoculated with a slightly attenuated, live DENV-4 strain, H-241. Plasma was collected from pre- and 6 months post-infection to measure Ab-dependent complement deposition, Ab-dependent cellular phagocytosis, and Ab-dependent cellular cytotoxicity (ADCC). All assays used target cells infected with DENV or transfected to express DENV-derived surface-bound NS1 protein. Results Infected subjects generated DENV-4-specific complement-fixing Abs and anti-NS1 Abs that facilitated monocyte-mediated phagocytosis of target cells. DENV-4-specific Abs also mediated NK cell activation in a subset of subjects. Conclusion Future work will focus on extending this functional study to recipients of a tetravalent vaccine followed by DENV-1 challenge to investigate how nNAbs correlate with disease protection/risk, and broaden our understanding of immune defense against dengue beyond neutralizing Abs. Funding Source DOD Topic Categories Viral Immunology (VIR)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to evaluate the role of non-neutralizing antibodies (nNAbs) in dengue virus infection, as neutralizing antibody titers do not reliably predict disease outcomes, and the function of nNAbs remains poorly understood.
Design and population
In a phase 1 open-label study, 14 dengue-naïve subjects were subcutaneously inoculated with a slightly attenuated, live DENV-4 strain, H-241.
Core methods
Plasma was collected from pre- and 6 months post-infection to measure Ab-dependent complement deposition, Ab-dependent cellular phagocytosis, and Ab-dependent cellular cytotoxicity (ADCC), using target cells infected with DENV or transfected to express DENV-derived surface-bound NS1 protein.
Main results
Infected subjects generated DENV-4-specific complement-fixing Abs and anti-NS1 Abs that facilitated monocyte-mediated phagocytosis of target cells, and DENV-4-specific Abs also mediated NK cell activation in a subset of subjects.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors demonstrated that non-neutralizing antibodies play a role in immune responses to DENV-4 infection, providing new insights into immune defense beyond neutralizing antibodies.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform future research on how nNAbs correlate with disease protection or risk, potentially improving vaccine development and understanding of dengue immunity.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by its small sample size and focus on a single serotype, but the use of direct measurements of functional antibody responses provides moderate evidence strength.

未经管理的塑料废物显着增加登革热负担

Unmanaged plastic waste significantly increases dengue burden
作者： Prasad Liyanage, Sakunthala Janaki, Anoja Dheerasinghe, Preshila Samaraweera, Jagath Amarasekera, Sampath Weerakoon, Costas A. Velis, Marina Treskova, Joacim Rocklöv
摘要中文翻译摘要 塑料污染越来越被认为是全球环境挑战,但其作为传染病风险可衡量驱动因素的作用仍然缺乏量化。我们开发了 WHO 推荐的 Breteau 指数 (BI) 的特定材料扩展,将废弃废物纳入常规伊蚊幼虫 监测 中,并使用斯里兰卡全国 监测 数据评估了其解释登革热风险的能力。我们发现废弃的不可生物降解的废物是与登革热 传播 相关的主要繁殖栖息地,其中塑料容器构成主要材料部分。2024-2025 年期间,与废物相关的繁殖栖息地占全国报告的登革热负担的 30-34%。根据经验得出的材料划分方法进一步估计,与塑料相关的栖息地约占哨点地区登革热负担的 22-23%。总之,这种特定于材料的 监测 框架将不受管理的环境废物转化为可测量的传染病风险,为下游 媒介 监测 和上游废物管理和塑料政策提供了实用的指标工具。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究解决了塑料污染作为传染病风险可衡量驱动因素的量化不足的问题,特别关注登革热 传播。
设计与对象
该研究开发了世界卫生组织推荐的 Breteau 指数的特定材料扩展,并使用斯里兰卡全国登革热 监测 数据对其进行了评估。
核心方法
该研究将废弃废物纳入常规伊蚊幼虫 监测 中,并使用凭经验得出的材料分配方法来估计与废物相关的栖息地的登革热负担。
主要结果
废弃的不可生物降解废物,特别是塑料容器,被确定为与登革热 传播 相关的主要繁殖栖息地,占全国报告的登革热负担的 30-34%,占哨点地区的 22-23%。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为表明,未管理的环境废物,尤其是塑料,对登革热 监测 有显着影响,提供了一个将废物与疾病风险联系起来的新颖的 监测 框架。
科研与公卫意义
特定材料的 监测 框架为下游 监测 和上游废物管理和塑料政策提供了实用指标,直接为公共卫生干预措施提供信息。
局限与证据强度
尽管全国范围和实证方法为结论提供了强有力的支持,但证据强度中等,因为该研究依赖于观察性 监测 数据和新颖的指数。

Unmanaged plastic waste significantly increases dengue burden

Authors: Prasad Liyanage, Sakunthala Janaki, Anoja Dheerasinghe, Preshila Samaraweera, Jagath Amarasekera, Sampath Weerakoon, Costas A. Velis, Marina Treskova, Joacim Rocklöv
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Plastic pollution is increasingly recognized as a global environmental challenge, yet its role as a measurable driver of infectious disease risk remains poorly quantified. We developed a material-specific extension of the WHO-recommended Breteau Index (BI) that incorporated discarded waste into routine Aedes larval surveillance and evaluated its ability to explain dengue risk using nationwide surveillance data from Sri Lanka. We found that discarded non-biodegradable waste represented a major breeding habitat associated with dengue transmission, with plastic containers comprising the dominant material fraction. Waste-associated breeding habitats accounted for 30–34% of reported dengue burden nationally during 2024–2025. An empirically derived material-partitioning approach further estimated that plastic-associated habitats contributed approximately 22–23% of dengue burden across sentinel districts. Together, this material-specific surveillance framework translates unmanaged environmental waste into measurable infectious disease risk, providing a practical indicator instrument for both downstream vector surveillance and upstream waste-management and plastic policies.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study addressed the poorly quantified role of plastic pollution as a measurable driver of infectious disease risk, specifically focusing on dengue transmission.
Design and population
The research developed a material-specific extension of the WHO-recommended Breteau Index and evaluated it using nationwide dengue surveillance data from Sri Lanka.
Core methods
The study incorporated discarded waste into routine Aedes larval surveillance and used an empirically derived material-partitioning approach to estimate dengue burden contributions from waste-associated habitats.
Main results
Discarded non-biodegradable waste, particularly plastic containers, was identified as a major breeding habitat associated with dengue transmission, accounting for 30–34% of reported dengue burden nationally and 22–23% across sentinel districts.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted the findings as demonstrating that unmanaged environmental waste, especially plastic, significantly contributes to dengue transmission, providing a novel surveillance framework linking waste to disease risk.
Scientific and public-health significance
The material-specific surveillance framework offers a practical indicator for both downstream vector surveillance and upstream waste-management and plastic policies, directly informing public health interventions.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate, as the study relies on observational surveillance data and a novel index, though the nationwide scope and empirical approach provide robust support for the conclusions.

巴西圣保罗州巴鲁地区疑似登革热患者伯内特立克克斯体的分子检测。

Molecular detection of Coxiella burnetii in suspected dengue patients from the Bauru region, São Paulo State, Brazil.
作者： Mateus de Souza Ribeiro Mioni, Danilo Alves de França, Célio Valério de Jesus Mangueze, Matheus Porto Cortezi, Gabriela Cardoso Pereira, Hélio Langoni, Felipe Fornazari, Virgínia Bodelão Richini-Pereira, Marcelo Bahia Labruna, Jane Megid
摘要中文翻译由伯内特立克克斯体引起的 Q 热在巴西是一种诊断不足的 人兽共患病,因为其临床表现非特异性且诊断能力有限。本研究调查了巴西圣保罗州 34 个城市最初怀疑患有登革热的急性发热性疾病患者血清样本中伯内特念珠菌的分子检测。对三年内从疑似 登革热 患者收集的总共 300 份血清样本进行了分析。仅包括从发烧长达 7 天的患者获得的样本。所有样本的登革热、钩端螺旋体病和布鲁氏菌病检测均为阴性,并通过针对 IS1111 基因的实时 PCR 进行分析。在 1.3% (4/300) 的样本中检测到伯内特氏菌 DNA (95%CI: 0.37%-3.35%),这与早期症状阶段的近期感染相符。这项研究是巴西急性发热综合征患者中伯内特念珠菌分子检测的第二份报告,也是圣保罗州的第一份报告。先前的血清学研究表明广泛接触病原体,强调了分子和血清学方法的互补作用。这些发现支持将 Q 热纳入未分化发热性疾病的鉴别诊断 诊断 中,并表明以单一健康为导向的 监测 策略可能有助于减少巴西的漏诊率,尽管需要更大样本量的进一步研究来证实这一建议。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
这项研究旨在解决巴西 Q 热诊断不足的问题,由于非特异性症状和有限的诊断工具,伯内特立克克斯体感染经常被忽视。
设计与对象
该研究分析了三年来从巴西圣保罗州 34 个城市收集的疑似 登革热 患者的血清样本,重点关注那些发烧时间长达 7 天的患者。
核心方法
使用标准方法对所有样本进行登革热、钩端螺旋体病和布鲁氏菌病检测,然后通过针对 IS1111 基因的实时 PCR 进行分析,以检测伯内氏立克次体。
主要结果
在 1.3% (4/300) 的样本中检测到伯氏柯克斯体 DNA (95%CI: 0.37%-3.35%),表明在早期症状阶段有近期感染。
解释与创新
研究结果表明,在鉴别未分化发热性疾病的 诊断 时应考虑 Q 热,因为分子检测显示,之前在疑似登革热病例中存在未被充分报告的伯内特立克次体。
科研与公卫意义
这些结果凸显了将分子方法纳入巴西发热性疾病 监测 策略的必要性,这可能会改善 Q 热的检测并为 One Health 方法提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究的证据因其侧重于特定地理区域的分子检测和相对较小的样本量而受到限制,这可能会影响普遍性。这些发现的可信度是中等的。

Molecular detection of Coxiella burnetii in suspected dengue patients from the Bauru region, São Paulo State, Brazil.

Authors: Mateus de Souza Ribeiro Mioni, Danilo Alves de França, Célio Valério de Jesus Mangueze, Matheus Porto Cortezi, Gabriela Cardoso Pereira, Hélio Langoni, Felipe Fornazari, Virgínia Bodelão Richini-Pereira, Marcelo Bahia Labruna, Jane Megid
Original abstract (English)
Q fever, caused by Coxiella burnetii, is an underdiagnosed zoonosis in Brazil due to its non-specific clinical presentation and limited diagnostic capacity. This study investigated the molecular detection of C. burnetii in serum samples from patients with acute febrile illness initially suspected of dengue in 34 municipalities of São Paulo State, Brazil. A total of 300 serum samples collected over a three-year period from patients with suspected dengue fever were analyzed. Only samples obtained from patients presenting with up to seven days of febrile illness were included. All samples tested negative for dengue, leptospirosis, and brucellosis and were analyzed by real-time PCR targeting the IS1111 gene. burnetii DNA was detected in 1.3% (4/300) of the samples (95%CI: 0.37%-3.35%), compatible with recent infection during the early symptomatic phase. This study represents the second report of molecular detection of C. burnetii in patients with acute febrile syndrome in Brazil and the first one in São Paulo State. Previous serological studies indicate widespread exposure to the pathogen, highlighting the complementary roles of molecular and serological approaches. These findings support the inclusion of Q fever in the differential diagnosis of undifferentiated febrile illnesses and suggest that One Health-oriented surveillance strategies may help reduce underdiagnosis in Brazil, although further studies with larger sample sizes are needed to confirm this recommendation.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to address the underdiagnosis of Q fever in Brazil, where Coxiella burnetii infections are often overlooked due to non-specific symptoms and limited diagnostic tools.
Design and population
The study analyzed serum samples from patients with suspected dengue fever collected over three years from 34 municipalities in São Paulo State, Brazil, focusing on those with up to seven days of febrile illness.
Core methods
All samples were tested for dengue, leptospirosis, and brucellosis using standard methods and then analyzed by real-time PCR targeting the IS1111 gene to detect Coxiella burnetii.
Main results
Coxiella burnetii DNA was detected in 1.3% (4/300) of the samples (95%CI: 0.37%-3.35%), indicating recent infection during the early symptomatic phase.
Interpretation and novelty
The findings suggest that Q fever should be considered in the differential diagnosis of undifferentiated febrile illnesses, as molecular detection revealed a previously underreported presence of Coxiella burnetii in suspected dengue cases.
Scientific and public-health significance
These results highlight the need for integrating molecular methods into surveillance strategies for febrile illnesses in Brazil, potentially improving the detection of Q fever and informing One Health approaches.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study's evidence is limited by its focus on molecular detection in a specific geographic area and a relatively small sample size, which may affect generalizability. The confidence in these findings is moderate.

A 高优先级学术文献 · 研究A high priorityAcademic literature · Research

NS1抗原和登革病毒血清型与登革热患者感染状况的相关性

Correlation of NS1 Antigen and Dengue Virus Serotype with Infection Status in Dengue Patients
作者： Anindya Widyasari, Aryati Aryati, Hantoro Gunawan, Puspa Wardhani, Lulut Kusumawati, Nasronudin Nasronudin, Betty Agustina Tambunan, Atika
摘要中文翻译非结构蛋白1(NS1)抗原是诊断感染早期诊断的重要生物标志物。然而,在原发性和继发性感染期间,NS1 检测可能会受到 登革病毒 血清型和 宿主 免疫应答 的影响,之前的研究报告的结果不一致。旨在研究 RT-PCR 确诊的登革热感染患者中 NS1 抗原阳性、登革病毒 血清型与感染状态之间的关联。对 65 名经 RT-PCR 确诊为登革热感染的住院患者进行了一项横断面分析研究。在发烧后的前五天内采集血样(中位时间为 3 天;范围为 1-5 天)。使用 STANDARD M10 虫媒病毒组鉴定 登革病毒 血清型,而使用 STANDARD F 登革热 IgM/IgG 荧光免疫测定确定感染状态。使用 Fisher 精确检验分析 NS1 抗原阳性、登革病毒 血清型和感染状态之间的关联。65例RT-PCR确诊病例中,NS1阳性55例(84.6%),NS1阴性10例(15.4%)。血清型分布为DENV-1(36.9%)、DENV-2(21.5%)、DENV-3(29.2%)、DENV-4(9.2%)和DENV-2/DENV-3混合感染(3.1%)。NS1 抗原阳性与 登革病毒 血清型 (p = 0.969) 或感染状态 (p = 0.456) 之间没有发现显着关联。尽管原发感染的 NS1 阳性率高于继发感染(86.4% vs. 66.7%),但差异无统计学意义。NS1 抗原阳性与 登革病毒 血清型或感染状态没有显着相关性。本研究中观察到的 NS1 高阳性率可能反映了疾病急性期的早期标本采集,无论感染状态或病毒血清型如何。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
非结构蛋白1(NS1)抗原是登革病毒感染早期诊断的重要生物标志物,但其检测可能受到原发和继发感染期间登革病毒血清型和宿主 免疫应答的影响,以往研究报道的结果不一致。
设计与对象
对 65 名经 RT-PCR 确诊的登革热感染住院患者进行了一项横断面分析研究,在发烧后的前 5 天内(中位时间为 3 天;范围为 1-5 天)采集了血液样本。
核心方法
使用 STANDARD M10 虫媒病毒组鉴定 登革病毒 血清型,使用 STANDARD F 登革热 IgM/IgG 荧光免疫测定确定感染状态,并使用 Fisher 精确检验分析 NS1 抗原阳性、登革病毒 血清型和感染状态之间的关联。
主要结果
在65例RT-PCR确诊病例中,55例(84.6%)NS1阳性,10例(15.4%)NS1阴性;未发现NS1抗原阳性与登革病毒血清型(p = 0.969)或感染状态(p = 0.456)之间存在显著关联,尽管NS1阳性率在原发性感染中高于继发性感染(86.4% vs.
解释与创新
作者解释说,NS1 抗原阳性与 登革病毒 血清型或感染状态没有显着相关,观察到的高 NS1 阳性可能反映了疾病急性期的早期标本采集,无论感染状态或病毒血清型如何。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,NS1 抗原仍然是登革热感染的有价值的早期诊断生物标志物,但其阳性率不受病毒血清型或感染状态的显着影响,支持其在临床环境中的实用性。
局限与证据强度
该研究受到样本量小和单中心设计的限制,但由于使用标准化实验室方法和登革热感染的RT-PCR确认,证据强度中等。

Correlation of NS1 Antigen and Dengue Virus Serotype with Infection Status in Dengue Patients

Authors: Anindya Widyasari, Aryati Aryati, Hantoro Gunawan, Puspa Wardhani, Lulut Kusumawati, Nasronudin Nasronudin, Betty Agustina Tambunan, Atika
Original abstract (English)
Non-structural protein 1 (NS1) antigen is an important biomarker for the early diagnosis of dengue virus infection. However, NS1 detection may be influenced by dengue virus serotype and the host immune response during primary and secondary infections, with inconsistent findings reported across previous studies. To investigate the association between NS1 antigen positivity, dengue virus serotypes, and infection status in patients with RT-PCR-confirmed dengue infection. An analytical cross-sectional study was conducted among 65 hospitalized patients with RT-PCR-confirmed dengue infection. Blood samples were collected within the first five days after fever onset (median, 3 days; range, 1–5 days). Dengue virus serotypes were identified using the STANDARD M10 Arbovirus Panel, while infection status was determined using the STANDARD F Dengue IgM/IgG fluorescence immunoassay. Associations between NS1 antigen positivity, dengue virus serotypes, and infection status were analyzed using Fisher's exact test. Among 65 RT-PCR-confirmed cases, 55 (84.6%) were NS1-positive, and 10 (15.4%) were NS1-negative. Serotype distribution was DENV-1 (36.9%), DENV-2 (21.5%), DENV-3 (29.2%), DENV-4 (9.2%), and mixed DENV-2/DENV-3 infection (3.1%). No significant associations were found between NS1 antigen positivity and dengue virus serotypes (p = 0.969) or infection status (p = 0.456). Although NS1 positivity was higher in primary than secondary infections (86.4% vs. 66.7%), the difference was not statistically significant. NS1 antigen positivity was not significantly associated with dengue virus serotypes or infection status. The high NS1 positivity observed in this study may reflect early specimen collection during the acute phase of illness, regardless of infection status or viral serotype.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Non-structural protein 1 (NS1) antigen is an important biomarker for the early diagnosis of dengue virus infection, but its detection may be influenced by dengue virus serotype and host immune response during primary and secondary infections, with inconsistent findings reported across previous studies.
Design and population
An analytical cross-sectional study was conducted among 65 hospitalized patients with RT-PCR-confirmed dengue infection, with blood samples collected within the first five days after fever onset (median, 3 days; range, 1–5 days).
Core methods
Dengue virus serotypes were identified using the STANDARD M10 Arbovirus Panel, while infection status was determined using the STANDARD F Dengue IgM/IgG fluorescence immunoassay, and associations between NS1 antigen positivity, dengue virus serotypes, and infection status were analyzed using Fisher's exact test.
Main results
Among 65 RT-PCR-confirmed cases, 55 (84.6%) were NS1-positive and 10 (15.4%) were NS1-negative; no significant associations were found between NS1 antigen positivity and dengue virus serotypes (p = 0.969) or infection status (p = 0.456), although NS1 positivity was higher in primary than secondary infections (86.4% vs.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that NS1 antigen positivity was not significantly associated with dengue virus serotypes or infection status, and the high NS1 positivity observed may reflect early specimen collection during the acute phase of illness, regardless of infection status or viral serotype.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings inform that NS1 antigen remains a valuable early diagnostic biomarker for dengue infection, but its positivity is not significantly influenced by viral serotype or infection status, supporting its utility in clinical settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited by its small sample size and single-center design, but the evidence strength is moderate due to the use of standardized laboratory methods and RT-PCR confirmation of dengue infection.

社区行为与骨干在登革热发生中的作用

Hubungan Perilaku Masyarakat dan Peran Kader dengan Kejadian Demam Berdarah Dengue
作者： Faiza Candrarini Widyadana, Teti Oktianingsih, Dayuningsih
摘要中文翻译背景:登革出血热(DHF)仍然是印度尼西亚的一个主要公共卫生问题,与社区预防行为和卫生干部在 媒介 控制中的作用密切相关。目的:本研究旨在分析坦格朗县 Jalan Emas 公共卫生中心工作区登革出血热 发病率 的社区行为与卫生干部角色的关系。方法:本研究采用横断面方法的分析观察设计。人口由 Jalan Emas 公共卫生中心工作区内的家庭组成。通过简单随机抽样,共有 81 名受访者被选中。使用结构化问卷收集数据并使用费舍尔精确检验进行分析。结果:结果显示,大多数受访者有中等程度的登革热预防行为(63.0%),超过一半的人认为卫生干部的作用良好(58.0%)。统计分析显示社区预防行为与登革热 发病率 之间存在显着关系 (p < 0.05)。还发现卫生干部的作用与登革热 发病率 之间存在显着关系(p < 0.05)。具有良好预防行为和对干部角色有积极看法的受访者感染登革热的可能性较小。结论:社区预防行为和卫生干部作用与登革出血热发病率显着相关。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
登革出血热(DHF)仍然是印度尼西亚的一个主要公共卫生问题,与社区预防行为和卫生干部在 媒介 控制中的作用密切相关。本研究旨在分析唐格朗县 Jalan Emas 公共卫生中心工作区登革出血热 发病率 的社区行为与卫生干部角色之间的关系。
设计与对象
这项研究采用了横断面方法的分析观察设计。人口由 Jalan Emas 公共卫生中心工作区的家庭组成,通过简单随机抽样抽取了 81 名受访者。
核心方法
使用结构化问卷收集数据并使用费舍尔精确检验进行分析。统计分析显示,社区预防行为与登革热发病率之间存在显着关系(p < 0.05),卫生干部的作用与登革热发病率之间也存在显着关系(p < 0.05)。
主要结果
结果显示,大多数受访者有中等程度的登革热预防行为(63.0%),超过一半的人认为卫生干部的作用良好(58.0%)。具有良好预防行为和对干部角色有积极看法的受访者感染登革热的可能性较小。
解释与创新
社区预防行为和卫生干部的作用与登革出血热发病率显着相关。研究结果强调了社区参与和干部参与登革热预防工作的重要性。
科研与公卫意义
该研究强调需要加强以社区为基础的登革热预防计划,并增强卫生干部在 媒介 控制中的作用,以减少登革热 媒介。这些发现可以为类似环境中的有针对性的公共卫生干预措施提供信息。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单个公共卫生中心的工作区域,这可能会限制普遍性。由于横断面设计和小样本量,证据强度中等,但显着的关联为登革热预防策略提供了宝贵的见解。

Hubungan Perilaku Masyarakat dan Peran Kader dengan Kejadian Demam Berdarah Dengue

Authors: Faiza Candrarini Widyadana, Teti Oktianingsih, Dayuningsih
Original abstract (English)
Background: Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever (DHF) remains a major public health problem in Indonesia and is closely associated with community preventive behavior and the role of health cadres in vector control. Objective: This study aimed to analyze the relationship between community behavior and the role of health cadres with the incidence of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever in the working area of Jalan Emas Public Health Center, Tangerang Regency. Methods: This study employed an analytical observational design with a cross-sectional approach. The population consisted of households in the working area of Jalan Emas Public Health Center. A total of 81 respondents were selected using simple random sampling. Data were collected using structured questionnaires and analyzed using Fisher’s Exact Test. Results: The results showed that most respondents had moderate dengue prevention behavior (63.0%), and more than half perceived the role of health cadres as good (58.0%). Statistical analysis revealed a significant relationship between community preventive behavior and dengue incidence (p < 0.05). A significant relationship was also found between the role of health cadres and dengue incidence (p < 0.05). Respondents with good preventive behavior and positive perceptions of cadre roles were less likely to experience dengue infection. Conclusion: Community preventive behavior and the role of health cadres are significantly associated with the incidence of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever (DHF) remains a major public health problem in Indonesia and is closely associated with community preventive behavior and the role of health cadres in vector control. This study aimed to analyze the relationship between community behavior and the role of health cadres with the incidence of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever in the working area of Jalan Emas Public Health Center, Tangerang Regency.
Design and population
This study employed an analytical observational design with a cross-sectional approach. The population consisted of households in the working area of Jalan Emas Public Health Center, with 81 respondents selected using simple random sampling.
Core methods
Data were collected using structured questionnaires and analyzed using Fisher’s Exact Test. Statistical analysis revealed a significant relationship between community preventive behavior and dengue incidence (p < 0.05), and a significant relationship was also found between the role of health cadres and dengue incidence (p < 0.05).
Main results
The results showed that most respondents had moderate dengue prevention behavior (63.0%), and more than half perceived the role of health cadres as good (58.0%). Respondents with good preventive behavior and positive perceptions of cadre roles were less likely to experience dengue infection.
Interpretation and novelty
Community preventive behavior and the role of health cadres are significantly associated with the incidence of Dengue Hemorrhagic Fever. The findings highlight the importance of community engagement and cadre involvement in dengue prevention efforts.
Scientific and public-health significance
The study underscores the need to strengthen community-based dengue prevention programs and enhance the role of health cadres in vector control to reduce dengue incidence. These findings can inform targeted public health interventions in similar settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study was limited to a single public health center’s working area, which may limit generalizability. The evidence strength is moderate due to the cross-sectional design and small sample size, but the significant associations provide valuable insights for dengue prevention strategies.

幼儿大面积脑室内出血,并发登革热诱发的凝血病,最初被误诊为胃肠炎:A 病例报告

Extensive intraventricular hemorrhage, complicated by dengue-induced coagulopathy, initially misdiagnosed as gastroenteritis in a toddler: A case report
作者： Ibrahim A. Bakari, Lulu Sakafu, Mwajabu A. Saleh
摘要中文翻译体检不完整,尤其是中枢神经系统(CNS)评估的遗漏,仍然是导致具有非特异性症状的发热儿童严重神经系统疾病漏诊或延迟诊断的主要原因。我报告了一名 22 个月大的男性病例,该病例最初表现为长期发烧、咖啡渣呕吐、非血性腹泻和反复抽搐,导致 Mwananyamala 地区转诊医院推定为 诊断 胃肠炎伴败血症。尽管进行了初步治疗,他的病情还是恶化了。随后在 Muhimbili 国立医院进行的评估显示,患者有局灶性神经功能缺损、肝肿大伴黄疸以及严重凝血功能障碍。脑磁共振成像(MRI)显示,在 重症登革热 相关凝血病的情况下,存在广泛的脑室内出血、梗阻性脑积水和多灶性急性脑梗死。尽管考虑了潜在的血管畸形,但未进行血管成像,这一点仍未得到证实。该病例凸显了儿科护理中的关键诊断挑战,其中常见的胃肠道症状可能掩盖严重的全身和神经系统疾病,特别是在登革热流行地区。它强调,对于出现持续性非特异性症状和新发癫痫发作的儿童,迫切需要高度怀疑中枢神经系统病理学、全面的神经学检查和早期神经影像学检查,这对于及时 诊断 和治疗至关重要。

问题与背景
体检不完整,尤其是中枢神经系统(CNS)评估的遗漏,仍然是导致具有非特异性症状的发热儿童严重神经系统疾病漏诊或延迟诊断的主要原因。
设计与对象
我报告了一名 22 个月大的男性病例,该病例最初表现为长期发烧、咖啡渣呕吐、非血性腹泻和反复抽搐,导致 Mwananyamala 地区转诊医院推定为 诊断 胃肠炎伴败血症。
核心方法
随后在 Muhimbili 国立医院进行的评估显示,患者有局灶性神经功能缺损、肝肿大伴黄疸以及严重凝血功能障碍。脑磁共振成像(MRI)显示,在 重症登革热 相关凝血病的情况下,存在广泛的脑室内出血、梗阻性脑积水和多灶性急性脑梗死。

尽管考虑了潜在的血管畸形,但未进行血管成像,这一点仍未得到证实。

该病例凸显了儿科护理中的关键诊断挑战,其中常见的胃肠道症状可能掩盖严重的全身和神经系统疾病,特别是在登革热流行地区。
科研与公卫意义
它强调,对于出现持续性非特异性症状和新发癫痫发作的儿童,迫切需要高度怀疑中枢神经系统病理学、全面的神经学检查和早期神经影像学检查,这对于及时 诊断 和治疗至关重要。
局限与证据强度
证据强度中等,因为这是一个单一的 病例报告,这限制了普遍性,但详细的临床和影像学发现为登革热引起的神经并发症的诊断挑战提供了有价值的见解。

Extensive intraventricular hemorrhage, complicated by dengue-induced coagulopathy, initially misdiagnosed as gastroenteritis in a toddler: A case report

Authors: Ibrahim A. Bakari, Lulu Sakafu, Mwajabu A. Saleh
Original abstract (English)
Incomplete physical examinations especially omissions of Central nervous system (CNS) assessments remain a major cause of missed or delayed diagnoses of serious neurological conditions in febrile children with nonspecific symptoms. I report the case of a 22-month-old male initially presenting with prolonged fever, coffee-ground vomiting, nonbloody diarrhea, and recurrent convulsions, leading to a presumptive diagnosis of gastroenteritis with sepsis at Mwananyamala regional referral hospital. Despite initial management, his condition deteriorated. Subsequent evaluation at Muhimbili National Hospital revealed focal neurological deficits, hepatomegaly with jaundice, and severe coagulopathy. Brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) demonstrated extensive intraventricular hemorrhage with obstructive hydrocephalus and multifocal acute cerebral infarcts in the setting of severe dengue-associated coagulopathy. Although an underlying vascular malformation was considered, vascular imaging was not performed and this remained unconfirmed. This case highlights the critical diagnostic challenge in pediatric care where common gastrointestinal symptoms can mask severe systemic and neurological conditions, particularly in dengue-endemic areas. It highlights the urgent need for high suspicion of central nervous system pathology, comprehensive neurological examinations, and early neuroimaging in children presenting with persistent nonspecific symptoms and new-onset seizures, crucial for timely diagnosis and management.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Incomplete physical examinations, especially omissions of Central nervous system (CNS) assessments, remain a major cause of missed or delayed diagnoses of serious neurological conditions in febrile children with nonspecific symptoms.
Design and population
I report the case of a 22-month-old male initially presenting with prolonged fever, coffee-ground vomiting, nonbloody diarrhea, and recurrent convulsions, leading to a presumptive diagnosis of gastroenteritis with sepsis at Mwananyamala regional referral hospital.
Core methods
Subsequent evaluation at Muhimbili National Hospital revealed focal neurological deficits, hepatomegaly with jaundice, and severe coagulopathy. Brain magnetic resonance imaging (MRI) demonstrated extensive intraventricular hemorrhage with obstructive hydrocephalus and multifocal acute cerebral infarcts in the setting of severe dengue-associated coagulopathy.
Main results
Although an underlying vascular malformation was considered, vascular imaging was not performed and this remained unconfirmed.
Interpretation and novelty
This case highlights the critical diagnostic challenge in pediatric care where common gastrointestinal symptoms can mask severe systemic and neurological conditions, particularly in dengue-endemic areas.
Scientific and public-health significance
It highlights the urgent need for high suspicion of central nervous system pathology, comprehensive neurological examinations, and early neuroimaging in children presenting with persistent nonspecific symptoms and new-onset seizures, crucial for timely diagnosis and management.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate as this is a single case report, which limits generalizability, but the detailed clinical and imaging findings provide valuable insights into the diagnostic challenges of dengue-induced neurological complications.

IgA 登革病毒 NS1 免疫复合物与体外中性粒细胞激活相关 2308886

IgA Dengue Virus NS1 Immune Complexes Are Associated with In Vitro Neutrophil Activation 2308886
作者： Dorca Marcano, Mercedes Lacourt, William Messer, Marcos Ramos Benítez, Vanessa Rivera Amill, Andrea Rivera Torres, Rachel Rodríguez
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
该研究旨在调查 登革病毒 感染期间循环中靶向非结构蛋白 1 (NS1) 的 IgA 在调节中性粒细胞激活中的作用,从而解决人们对 IgA 免疫复合物对登革热免疫发病机制的贡献知之甚少的知识空白。
设计与对象
该研究使用波多黎各 DENV 血清阳性个体 (n = 20) 的血清样本和从健康捐赠者分离的中性粒细胞来评估重组 DENV-2 NS1 和包膜蛋白的 IgA 和 IgG 识别,并评估中性粒细胞活化。
核心方法
通过 ELISA 评估血清样本对重组 DENV-2 NS1 和包膜蛋白的 IgA 和 IgG 识别,用 IgA-NS1 或 IgG-NS1 免疫复合物刺激中性粒细胞 6 小时,并使用 Incucyte 活细胞成像系统对 NETosis 进行定量,并在 GraphPad Prism 中进行相关分析。
主要结果
与 IgG(平均 NS1/E 比率 1.09)相比,IgA 比 E 蛋白更倾向于识别 NS1(平均 NS1/E 比率 1.71),并且 NETosis 证明与 IgA-NS1 免疫复合物(R2 = 0.42,p < 0.0001)的关联性强于与 IgG-NS1 复合物(R2 = 0.15,p = 0.026)的关联性。
解释与创新
作者解释说,IgA-NS1 免疫复合物可能作为登革热中中性粒细胞驱动的炎症的潜在调节剂,代表了一种独特的免疫致病途径,在异源 DENV 感染期间尤其相关。
科研与公卫意义
这些发现提出了一种登革热免疫发病机制的新机制,可以为未来的 监测、诊断策略和针对 IgA-NS1 相互作用的治疗干预措施提供信息,以减轻中性粒细胞介导的炎症。
局限与证据强度
由于样本量较小(n = 20)并且依赖于体外中性粒细胞刺激,这可能无法完全重现体内条件,因此证据强度中等;然而,研究结果的统计显着性支持了结论。

IgA Dengue Virus NS1 Immune Complexes Are Associated with In Vitro Neutrophil Activation 2308886

Authors: Dorca Marcano, Mercedes Lacourt, William Messer, Marcos Ramos Benítez, Vanessa Rivera Amill, Andrea Rivera Torres, Rachel Rodríguez
Original abstract (English)
Abstract Introduction Dengue virus (DENV) causes ∼400 million infections annually, with severe disease occurring more frequently during heterologous secondary infections, partly due to antibody-dependent enhancement and heightened inflammation. While IgG responses have been extensively studied, the role of circulating IgA, particularly IgA targeting non-structural protein 1 (NS1), in modulating neutrophil activation remains poorly understood. IgA immune complexes (ICs) can engage Fc receptors on neutrophils and trigger effector functions such as neutrophil extracellular trap (NET) formation (NETosis), but their contribution to dengue immunopathogenesis has not been fully characterized. Methods Serum samples from DENV-seropositive individuals in Puerto Rico (n = 20) were evaluated for IgA and IgG recognition of recombinant DENV-2 NS1 and envelope (E) proteins by ELISA. Neutrophils isolated from healthy donors were stimulated for 6 hours with IgA-NS1 or IgG-NS1 immune complexes. NETosis was quantified using the Incucyte live cell imaging system. Correlation analyses were performed in GraphPad Prism. Results IgG recognized NS1 and E proteins at comparable levels, while IgA favored the recognition of NS1 over E protein. The mean NS1/E ratio was 1.09 for IgG (range 1.02—1.16) and 1.71 for IgA (range 1.18—2.10). NETosis demonstrated a stronger and more statistically significant association with IgA-NS1 ICs (R2 = 0.42, p < 0.0001) when compared to IgG-NS1 complexes (R2 = 0.15, p = 0.026). Conclusion Collectively, these findings suggest that IgA—NS1 immune complexes may serve as potential modulators of neutrophil-driven inflammation in dengue. This isotype-specific linkage to NETosis suggests a distinct immunopathogenic pathway that may be particularly relevant during heterologous DENV infection and merits further mechanistic and translational investigation. Funding Source R01AI153434 & U54MD007579 Topic Categories Viral Immunology (VIR)
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
The study aimed to investigate the role of circulating IgA targeting non-structural protein 1 (NS1) in modulating neutrophil activation during dengue virus infection, addressing a knowledge gap in the poorly understood contribution of IgA immune complexes to dengue immunopathogenesis.
Design and population
The study used serum samples from DENV-seropositive individuals in Puerto Rico (n = 20) and neutrophils isolated from healthy donors to evaluate IgA and IgG recognition of recombinant DENV-2 NS1 and envelope proteins and assess neutrophil activation.
Core methods
Serum samples were evaluated for IgA and IgG recognition of recombinant DENV-2 NS1 and envelope proteins by ELISA, neutrophils were stimulated with IgA-NS1 or IgG-NS1 immune complexes for 6 hours, and NETosis was quantified using the Incucyte live cell imaging system with correlation analyses performed in GraphPad Prism.
Main results
IgA favored the recognition of NS1 over E protein (mean NS1/E ratio 1.71) compared to IgG (mean NS1/E ratio 1.09), and NETosis demonstrated a stronger association with IgA-NS1 immune complexes (R2 = 0.42, p < 0.0001) than with IgG-NS1 complexes (R2 = 0.15, p = 0.026).
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpreted that IgA-NS1 immune complexes may serve as potential modulators of neutrophil-driven inflammation in dengue, representing a distinct immunopathogenic pathway particularly relevant during heterologous DENV infection.
Scientific and public-health significance
These findings suggest a novel mechanism for dengue immunopathogenesis that could inform future surveillance, diagnostic strategies, and therapeutic interventions targeting IgA-NS1 interactions to mitigate neutrophil-mediated inflammation.
Limitations and evidence strength
The evidence strength is moderate due to the small sample size (n = 20) and reliance on in vitro neutrophil stimulation, which may not fully recapitulate in vivo conditions; however, the statistical significance of the findings supports the conclusions.

护理教育:临床模拟在 登革热 患者护理中的应用。

Nursing education: use of clinical simulation in the care of patients with dengue fever.
作者： Thais Lazaroto Roberto Cordeiro, Marcia Bucco, Karyna Turra Osternack, Karin Rosa Persegona Ogradowski
摘要中文翻译目的:分析临床模拟如何影响初级卫生保健背景下护生在临床评估、决策、治疗沟通和报告 登革热 方面的熟练程度。方法:大三护理本科生参加活动,患者为规范化患者,女,45岁,曾去过登革热流行区,出现持续呕吐、发热、腹痛。使用结构化检查表来评估他们在诊断怀疑、病史、体检、止血带测试、严重性体征识别、报告处方的完成、患者转诊决定以及与患者沟通方面的表现。演员收到了详细的指示,以确保真实性和标准化。该项目遵循伦理原则,并得到研究伦理委员会的批准。结果:学生表现出较高的参与度,逻辑推理能力、接近患者的信心以及沟通能力都有所提高。他们中的大多数人正确识别了登革热嫌疑,尽管他们在识别警告信号方面有困难,这丰富了汇报并促进了对决策的反思。结论:临床模拟是登革热教学、理论与实践相结合的有力工具。因此,它提供了重要的学习机会,并加强了对准备更充分的护士的教育,以使其在初级保健方面取得合格的表现。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
本研究旨在分析临床模拟如何影响护生在初级卫生保健背景下临床评估、决策、治疗沟通和 登革热 报告与警告信号的熟练程度。
设计与对象
三学期护理本科生参加了此次活动,活动涉及一名标准化患者,她是一名45岁女性,在去过登革热流行区后出现持续呕吐、发烧和腹痛。
核心方法
使用结构化检查表来评估他们在诊断怀疑、病史、体检、止血带测试、严重性体征识别、报告处方的完成、患者转诊决定以及与患者沟通方面的表现。演员收到了详细的指示,以确保真实性和标准化。该项目遵循伦理原则,并得到研究伦理委员会的批准。
主要结果
学生们表现出高度的参与度,逻辑推理、接近患者的信心和沟通能力都有所提高。他们中的大多数人正确识别了登革热嫌疑,尽管他们在识别警告信号方面有困难,这丰富了汇报并促进了对决策的反思。
解释与创新
临床模拟是登革热教学、理论与实践相结合的有力工具。因此,它提供了重要的学习机会,并加强了对准备更充分的护士的教育,以使其在初级保健方面取得合格的表现。
科研与公卫意义
研究结果表明,临床模拟可以加强 登革热 的护理教育,有助于医疗保健专业人员为管理初级医疗机构中的登革热病例做好更充分的准备。
局限与证据强度
该研究仅限于单一的教育干预,并没有提供有关临床模拟在现实世界场景中有效性的定量数据。由于关注特定的教育背景,证据的强度适中。

Nursing education: use of clinical simulation in the care of patients with dengue fever.

Authors: Thais Lazaroto Roberto Cordeiro, Marcia Bucco, Karyna Turra Osternack, Karin Rosa Persegona Ogradowski
Original abstract (English)
OBJECTIVE: To analyze how clinical simulation impacts nursing students' proficiency in clinical evaluation, decision-making, therapeutic communication, and reporting of dengue fever with warning signs within a primary health care context. METHODS: Third-term nursing undergraduates participated in the activity, which involved a standardized patient, a 45-year-old female with persistent vomiting, fever, and abdominal pain after a trip to an endemic dengue area. A structured checklist was used to assess their performance in terms of diagnostic suspicion, anamnesis, physical examination, tourniquet test, recognition of severity signs, completion of the reporting formulary, patient referral decisions, and communication with the patient. The actor received detailed instructions to ensure realism and standardization. This project followed ethical tenets and was approved by the Research Ethics Board. RESULTS: Students showed high engagement, with improvements in their logical reasoning, confidence in approaching patients, and communication. Most of them correctly identified dengue fever suspicion, although they had difficulties recognizing the warning signs, which enriched the debriefing and promoted reflection on decision-making. CONCLUSION: Clinical simulation is a powerful tool for teaching dengue fever and integrating theory and practice. Thus, it provides significant learning and strengthens the education of better-prepared nurses for qualified performance in primary healthcare.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
This study aimed to analyze how clinical simulation impacts nursing students' proficiency in clinical evaluation, decision-making, therapeutic communication, and reporting of dengue fever with warning signs within a primary health care context.
Design and population
Third-term nursing undergraduates participated in the activity, which involved a standardized patient, a 45-year-old female with persistent vomiting, fever, and abdominal pain after a trip to an endemic dengue area.
Core methods
A structured checklist was used to assess their performance in terms of diagnostic suspicion, anamnesis, physical examination, tourniquet test, recognition of severity signs, completion of the reporting formulary, patient referral decisions, and communication with the patient. The actor received detailed instructions to ensure realism and standardization. This project followed ethical tenets and was approved by the Research Ethics Board.
Main results
Students showed high engagement, with improvements in their logical reasoning, confidence in approaching patients, and communication. Most of them correctly identified dengue fever suspicion, although they had difficulties recognizing the warning signs, which enriched the debriefing and promoted reflection on decision-making.
Interpretation and novelty
Clinical simulation is a powerful tool for teaching dengue fever and integrating theory and practice. Thus, it provides significant learning and strengthens the education of better-prepared nurses for qualified performance in primary healthcare.
Scientific and public-health significance
The findings suggest that clinical simulation can enhance nursing education for dengue fever, contributing to better-prepared healthcare professionals for managing dengue cases in primary healthcare settings.
Limitations and evidence strength
The study is limited to a single educational intervention and does not provide quantitative data on the effectiveness of clinical simulation in real-world scenarios. The evidence is moderate in strength due to its focus on a specific educational context.

用于全面登革热血清型检测的多重 RT-LAMP-侧流平台

A Multiplex RT-LAMP-Lateral Flow Platform For Comprehensive Dengue Serotype Detection
作者： Alina Darcheva, Nuhamin Dejene, Davis Hackel, Christine Kim, Maya Potter
摘要中文翻译
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
Dengue virus (DENV) causes approximately 400 million infections annually and exists as four distinct serotypes (DENV-1–4), with secondary infection by a different serotype increasing the risk of severe dengue. Rapid serotype differentiation is critical for clinical management and outbreak control.
设计与对象
该研究开发了一种低成本、便携式诊断平台,将逆转录环介导的等温扩增 (RT-LAMP) 与多重侧流测定 (LFA) 相结合,用于快速检测和区分所有四种登革热血清型。
核心方法
针对 RT-LAMP 设计和优化了血清型特异性引物,并构建了使用脂肪酸相变材料和镁铁合金反应的便携式加热装置,以实现免仪器扩增。通过固定对照捕获探针验证了侧流测定机制,证明了盐介导的固定、清晰的比色响应以及成功的生物素介导的金纳米颗粒捕获。
主要结果
合成 DENV-2 DNA 的初步测试表明 RT-LAMP 扩增成功。
解释与创新
作者将这些发现解释为建立了一种廉价、可现场部署的 RT-LAMP-LFA 平台用于登革热血清型检测的可行性。
科研与公卫意义
该平台有潜力在资源有限且无法进入集中实验室的环境中改善早期登革热 诊断 和 暴发 监测。
局限与证据强度
所提供的证据并未报告针对 RT-PCR 等金标准方法的临床验证、敏感性、特异性或性能,从而将证据的强度限制在可行性水平的发现范围内。

A Multiplex RT-LAMP-Lateral Flow Platform For Comprehensive Dengue Serotype Detection

Authors: Alina Darcheva, Nuhamin Dejene, Davis Hackel, Christine Kim, Maya Potter
Original abstract (English)
Dengue virus (DENV) is a mosquito-borne RNA virus responsible for approximately 400 million infections annually and exists as four distinct serotypes (DENV-1-4). Secondary infection with a different serotype significantly increases the risk of severe dengue, making rapid serotype differentiation critical. Current gold-standard methods, such as reverse transcription polymerase chain reaction (RT-PCR), require expensive equipment and centralized laboratories, limiting accessibility in resource-limited settings. This project aims to develop a low-cost, portable diagnostic platform that combines reverse transcription loop-mediated isothermal amplification (RT-LAMP) with a multiplex lateral flow assay (LFA) for rapid detection and differentiation of all four dengue serotypes. Serotype-specific primers are being designed and optimized for RT-LAMP. A portable heating device utilizing a fatty acid phase change material (PCM) and an Mg-Fe alloy reaction was successfully constructed to provide instrument-free amplification. The LFA mechanism was also validated by successfully immobilizing the control capture probe, demonstrating effective salt-mediated immobilization, a clear colorimetric response, and successful biotin-mediated gold nanoparticle capture. Initial testing with synthetic DENV-2 DNA demonstrated successful RT-LAMP amplification. These findings establish the feasibility of an inexpensive, field-deployable RT-LAMP-LFA platform with potential to improve early dengue diagnosis and outbreak surveillance in resource-limited settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
Dengue virus (DENV) causes approximately 400 million infections annually and exists as four distinct serotypes (DENV-1–4), with secondary infection by a different serotype increasing the risk of severe dengue. Rapid serotype differentiation is critical for clinical management and outbreak control.
Design and population
The study developed a low-cost, portable diagnostic platform combining reverse transcription loop-mediated isothermal amplification (RT-LAMP) with a multiplex lateral flow assay (LFA) for rapid detection and differentiation of all four dengue serotypes.
Core methods
Serotype-specific primers were designed and optimized for RT-LAMP, and a portable heating device using a fatty acid phase change material and an Mg-Fe alloy reaction was constructed to enable instrument-free amplification. The lateral flow assay mechanism was validated by immobilizing the control capture probe, demonstrating salt-mediated immobilization, a clear colorimetric response, and successful biotin-mediated gold nanoparticle capture.
Main results
Initial testing with synthetic DENV-2 DNA demonstrated successful RT-LAMP amplification.
Interpretation and novelty
The authors interpret the findings as establishing the feasibility of an inexpensive, field-deployable RT-LAMP-LFA platform for dengue serotype detection.
Scientific and public-health significance
This platform has potential to improve early dengue diagnosis and outbreak surveillance in resource-limited settings where access to centralized laboratories is limited.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report clinical validation, sensitivity, specificity, or performance against gold-standard methods such as RT-PCR, limiting the strength of the evidence to feasibility-level findings.

印度南部日本脑炎患者脑脊液 IgM 虫媒病毒交叉反应性。

Cerebrospinal fluid IgM cross-reactivity among arboviruses in Japanese encephalitis patients from southern India.
作者： Amrita Pattanaik, D Swapna Reddy, Chiranjay Mukhopadhyay
摘要中文翻译背景:在多种虫媒病毒共同循环的地区,神经侵袭性感染的血清学 诊断 因抗体交叉反应而变得复杂。脑脊液 (CSF) 中的 IgM 检测通常被认为是病因感染的证据。方法:我们回顾性筛查日本脑炎病毒(JEV)IgM 阳性的脑脊液样本中的 登革病毒(DENV)、基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)和西尼罗河病毒(WNV)的 IgM 抗体。对部分确诊病例的临床、实验室和放射学数据进行了分析。结果:在 60 份 JEV IgM 阳性脑脊液样本中,检测到针对 DENV(46.7%)、CHIKV(10%)和 WNV(23%)的交叉反应 IgM 抗体。交叉反应 IgM 水平始终低于 JEV 特异性 IgM。不良的临床结果与较高的脑脊液白细胞计数、蛋白质和乳酸水平相关。结论:虫媒病毒之间的脑脊液 IgM 交叉反应在日本脑炎中很常见,可能会混淆病因学 诊断。流行病环境中需要集成诊断算法。
查看研究七要素
问题与背景
在多种虫媒病毒共同循环的地区,神经侵袭性感染的血清学 诊断 因抗体交叉反应而变得复杂,脑脊液 (CSF) 中的 IgM 检测通常被认为是病因感染的证据。
设计与对象
该研究回顾性筛选了日本脑炎病毒(JEV)IgM阳性的脑脊液样本,以获取针对登革病毒(DENV)、基孔肯雅病毒(CHIKV)和西尼罗河病毒(WNV)的IgM抗体。
核心方法
对部分确诊病例的临床、实验室和放射学数据进行了分析。
主要结果
在 60 份 JEV IgM 阳性脑脊液样本中,检测到针对 DENV(46.7%)、CHIKV(10%)和 WNV(23%)的交叉反应 IgM 抗体。交叉反应 IgM 水平始终低于 JEV 特异性 IgM。
解释与创新
虫媒病毒之间的脑脊液 IgM 交叉反应在日本脑炎中很常见,可能会混淆病因学 诊断。
科研与公卫意义
在流行环境中需要集成诊断算法来解决交叉反应并提高诊断准确性。
局限与证据强度
提供的证据不报告研究设计细节、样本量合理性或统计分析,限制了有关交叉反应频率和临床影响的结论的强度。

Cerebrospinal fluid IgM cross-reactivity among arboviruses in Japanese encephalitis patients from southern India.

Authors: Amrita Pattanaik, D Swapna Reddy, Chiranjay Mukhopadhyay
Original abstract (English)
BACKGROUND: In regions where multiple arboviruses co-circulate, serological diagnosis of neuroinvasive infections is complicated by antibody cross-reactivity. IgM detection in cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) is often considered evidence of etiologic infection. METHODS: We retrospectively screened CSF samples positive for Japanese encephalitis virus (JEV) IgM for IgM antibodies against dengue virus (DENV), chikungunya virus (CHIKV), and West Nile virus (WNV). Clinical, laboratory, and radiological data were analyzed for a subset of confirmed cases. RESULTS: Among 60 JEV IgM-positive CSF samples, cross-reactive IgM antibodies were detected against DENV (46.7%), CHIKV (10%), and WNV (23%). Cross-reactive IgM levels were consistently lower than JEV-specific IgM. Poor clinical outcomes were associated with higher CSF leukocyte counts, protein, and lactate levels. CONCLUSIONS: CSF IgM cross-reactivity among arboviruses is frequent in Japanese encephalitis and may confound etiological diagnosis. Integrated diagnostic algorithms are required in endemic settings.
View research seven-element analysis
Question and background
In regions where multiple arboviruses co-circulate, serological diagnosis of neuroinvasive infections is complicated by antibody cross-reactivity, and IgM detection in cerebrospinal fluid (CSF) is often considered evidence of etiologic infection.
Design and population
The study retrospectively screened CSF samples positive for Japanese encephalitis virus (JEV) IgM for IgM antibodies against dengue virus (DENV), chikungunya virus (CHIKV), and West Nile virus (WNV).
Core methods
Clinical, laboratory, and radiological data were analyzed for a subset of confirmed cases.
Main results
Among 60 JEV IgM-positive CSF samples, cross-reactive IgM antibodies were detected against DENV (46.7%), CHIKV (10%), and WNV (23%). Cross-reactive IgM levels were consistently lower than JEV-specific IgM.
Interpretation and novelty
CSF IgM cross-reactivity among arboviruses is frequent in Japanese encephalitis and may confound etiological diagnosis.
Scientific and public-health significance
Integrated diagnostic algorithms are required in endemic settings to address cross-reactivity and improve diagnostic accuracy.
Limitations and evidence strength
The supplied evidence does not report study design details, sample size justification, or statistical analyses, limiting the strength of conclusions about cross-reactivity frequency and clinical impact.
